Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 301: Only for Those with Karmic Affinity.
-------------------------------------------------
The journey with Pyeonjung was more enjoyable than anything.

In the carriage he drove so skillfully, I cultivated the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art, and we stopped at inns with good food to eat. As on the way there, whenever there was a place with beautiful scenery, we stopped the carriage and looked around together. We saw a rainbow rising beyond a waterfall, and over a cup of wine, I listened to him talk about the life of a coachman.

And so, when we arrived at Maga Village, I even paid him the cost of returning.

“There are many rough demonic practitioners here, so it would be dangerous to take on passengers for the return trip. This money is not for you, but for you to buy your children a set of clothes, so do not refuse my sincerity.”

Pyeonjung looked as though he had far too much he wanted to say, but he offered only this in return.

“I only look forward to the day when I may serve you again.”

I might never see this man again, but one never knew where human ties might lead. Perhaps someday I would find myself climbing into the carriage of a white-haired Pyeonjung.

“Return safely.”

After sending Pyeonjung back, I entered Maga Village.

“Ah, wonderful! This smell!”

The distinctive scent of Maga Village greeted me. Only then did I truly feel that I had finally returned home.

“Young Cult Leader!”

The person who spotted me from over there and came running was Jo Chunbae of the Pungryu Tavern. He ran over so eagerly that I worried he might trip and fall.

“Innkeeper, have you been well?”

“When did you return?”

“I just arrived. Seeing our innkeeper first thing upon my return makes me think my luck will be excellent today.”

“Oh, please don’t say such things! What am I, after all?”

Though he waved his hands in denial, he was smiling broadly. Seeing Jo Chunbae as well made it truly sink in that I had returned.

He had been on his way to the Pungryu Tavern with ingredients loaded onto a cart.

“You buy the ingredients yourself?”

“Of course. I choose every fresh ingredient myself. Now, let us go.”

Just as Jo Chunbae was about to push the cart, I had him sit on it.

“Let’s sit together and go.”

I sat side by side with Jo Chunbae at the front of the cart.

Jo Chunbae blinked and asked,

“If we both sit, who will pull the cart?”

“We pull it with our hearts.”

Thinking I was joking, Jo Chunbae laughed and said,

“I’ll push it for you. Please ride my cart for the first time!”

At the very moment he tried to get down from the cart, the cart began moving on its own.

“Oh my! It’s moving by itself!”

Of course, I was using void-object manipulation to move the cart. Jo Chunbae seemed fascinated, exclaiming in wonder again and again.

“I started working at taverns from a young age and have experienced all sorts of things, but this is a first for me.”

“To tell the truth, it’s a first for me too.”

“Is it all right to use the Young Cult Leader’s precious internal energy for something like this?”

“That precious internal energy is always being used on trash. If anything, this is the most precious use it has seen.”

Jo Chunbae looked at me and said,

“If I were a martial artist, I would certainly have served the Young Cult Leader.”

“I would hate that.”

When Jo Chunbae stiffened, worried that he might have misspoken, I smiled and said,

“Then there would be no Pungryu Tavern, would there? Even without you, innkeeper, I have many subordinates.”

A deep smile formed on Jo Chunbae’s lips.

And so we arrived at the Pungryu Tavern.

I went up to the second-floor seat where I always sat and drank.

“You still keep this place empty?”

“Yes. It is a seat for the Young Cult Leader and special guests.”

“Isn’t this a loss for you, innkeeper?”

“I lived my whole life struggling desperately not to take such losses. Now I ought to have a little fun like this.”

Jo Chunbae looked with satisfaction at the signatures of the Demon Venerables carved into the wall.

I looked there with him, then was startled.

“Father left one too!”

“He came about a month after the Young Cult Leader left the cult.”

“Did he come alone?”

“Yes. He came twice, and both times he came alone.”

I imagined Father sitting here alone, drinking wine.

Leaving his signature there could not have been easy for him. How could I not like him for this? Father was Father after all.

“Will you have a drink?”

“I must pay my respects to Father first. I’ll stop by later.”

“Then I will see you later.”

After leaving the Pungryu Tavern, my steps quickened. Seeing Father’s signature made me want to see him even more.

* * *

In front of the Heavenly Demon Hall, Jeokyeon, the captain of the guards, was waiting.

It seemed the news of my return had already been conveyed.

He quickly looked me up and down, checking whether I had been injured.

“I am relieved you have returned safely.”

He must have been angry that I had sent the guards back and gone to the Jiangxi Branch alone, but his eyes were filled with relief that I had returned unharmed.

“You need to get used to things like this. The road ahead is very long.”

“No matter what you say, it is not something easily gotten used to.”

He was right. If I died, they would have to bear all responsibility.

I asked Jeokyeon as he was about to turn away.

“How is your eye?”

“It is fine.”

“Let me see.”

Now he obediently removed his eye patch without protest. Looking at the red energy in his eye, it seemed his cultivation had progressed in the meantime.

“Once you get through this hurdle, you will enter the stage where the pain decreases.”

I placed my palm over Jeokyeon’s eye and infused him with gentle internal energy.

“I believe I will be the first to know when the Young Cult Leader’s martial realm changes.”

“Does it feel different?”

“Yes. It feels different again from the last time you examined me.”

His eye was sensing even the subtle changes that I myself could not feel.

“When on earth do you cultivate?”

“I may look like I’m lazing around, but I’m surprisingly diligent. Even on the way back, I cultivated the entire time while riding in the carriage.”

Though I said that, I did not think the change came from cultivating the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art in the carriage on the way there and back.

Had it not changed when I watched the sunset with Pyeonjung? Had it not changed when I placed a coin in Ju Yang’s hand? Had it not changed when I listened to the hardships of Branch Leader Ho Gyeong?

I still believed it.

When life changed and people changed, martial arts changed as well.

* * *

At last, Father, whom I had wanted so much to see, was sitting far ahead.

I strode along the Path of Blood and walked to the foot of the grand chair. Father was guarding our cult in his unchanging form.

“I have returned. Have you been at peace during my absence?”

I greeted Father with proper courtesy.

Whenever I go out and return, I always go to see Father first. I have done it several times already, and I offer the same greeting, but each time I feel something different, and the heart with which I face him changes. As time passes, I am more and more glad to see Father, and I like him more.

“Whenever you go out, you return with blood on your hands.”

In Hunan and in Jiangxi as well. There was no room for excuses.

“Is that not because I take after Father?”

A sneer brushed Father’s lips. How glad I was to see that sneer after so long.

Father, too, had changed a great deal from before. He had once been someone who looked down from that grand chair from beginning to end.

“Let us go out and walk a little.”

These days, he liked walking with me like this.

I followed Father out of the Heavenly Demon Hall. We walked together along the narrow path laid through the rear garden.

“I met a good coachman and learned of many inns with skilled cooks. There were hidden delicacies in places I never expected. Later, I will take you to all of them.”

I did not speak of the people who had died by my hand. He would have received reports of everything that happened through the Tongcheon Pavilion anyway. What I wanted to tell Father were things like this.

Where the scenery was beautiful, where one could find a teahouse with a good atmosphere, how people lived. Just as Father had come out from his grand chair to this rear garden, I now wanted to go out with him into the Central Plains.

“Do you know how much interest Yeomwangchae has been charging these days? They are madmen.”

I also told him about Yeomwangchae and relayed the conversations I had with the warriors of the Demon Sword Corps.

“If Father personally sends a gift to the Demon Sword Corps, their morale will rise greatly.”

“I shall.”

When Father answered so readily, I could not help but put on an act.

“You discriminate far too much.”

“What discrimination?”

“You are too different when I am the Second Young Master and when I am the Young Cult Leader.”

“I should be. Only then will you know how precious your position is. Why? Shall I treat you as I did before?”

“You mean the days when Father’s finger wind nearly punched a hole through this handsome face? I decline!”

It felt so good to move into a new relationship with Father.

Father stopped walking for a moment and gazed at Daecheon Mountain in the distance.

And what I most wanted to say to Father was this.

“Next time, let us go somewhere to enjoy ourselves.”

“If I move, the gangho will be startled.”

“Let them be startled. Stop guarding the Heavenly Demon Hall and now… go guard your own life, Father.”

For an instant, I felt Father’s back flinch.

I do not want Father’s emergence into the murim to become a war. I want Father’s emergence to be a joyful journey.

“I say this because I want to have fun with you, Father.”

Father merely gazed silently at Daecheon Mountain far away.

Yes, how could Father not want to go out? The weight of that seat could not be known unless one had sat in it.

“The Fist Demon has gained a daughter.”

“I was surprised as well. I did not think Master would make such a decision.”

“You did not intervene?”

“I do enjoy meddling in all sorts of affairs, but not this one. It is a matter between parent and child. How could I dare interfere?”

“It was unexpected.”

Father could hardly be the only one who was surprised.

“He looks as though he would live well even if left alone in the world, but he was lonely. What do you think? Would you like to have a daughter too, Father?”

For some reason, Father obediently nodded.

“What if I disappeared and you gained a daughter?”

Father nodded even more firmly.

“That is too much!”

It felt good to play the fool in front of Father after so long.

“Follow me.”

Father led me deep into the rear garden of the Heavenly Demon Hall. After passing through all sorts of defenses that forbade outside intrusion, and then through a series of secretly laid formations, I was finally able to see our destination. It was my first time coming here, and I was startled when I saw what was written on the signboard.

Heavenly Demon Treasury.

He had brought me to the treasure vault of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“Ah! This is my first time here.”

I followed Father inside.

Countless weapons flashed with sharp edges, and spiritual medicines and spirit herbs tempted people with all manner of fragrances. There were also innumerable dazzling jewels such as luminous night pearls and poison-warding pearls.

Since I was a martial artist, the first things my eyes went to were naturally the weapons and spiritual medicines.

There were swords, sabers, and spears, of course, but also axes, wheels, sickles, rulers, fans, brushes, needles… Every sort of divine weapon and wondrous armament was on display. Each and every one was a divine weapon that would stir up a storm of blood if it appeared in the murim.

That was not all. As for spiritual medicines, they did not even deal in thousand-year snow ginseng here. The highest-grade spiritual medicines and spirit herbs, on the level of ten-thousand-year snow ginseng, were classified and stored according to whether they possessed yin or yang properties.

There were also spiritual medicines stored separately. Eight in total.

“These here are spiritual medicines that will be immediately distributed to the Demon Venerables if war breaks out.”

Not only that, there were poisons and hidden weapons to be used in war. The hidden weapons were forbidden hidden weapons banned from use in the murim, and the poisons were weapons of mass slaughter that could take hundreds or thousands of lives at once. Even the costly Formless Ultimate Poison was here by the bottle; simply seeing how many poisons had been prepared made my heart grow cold. If war broke out, these poisons would go straight to the Poison King, would they not?

And was that all? On one side, protective armors were piled up. Piled like this, they might at a glance look like cheap martial garments from the marketplace, but each one was a protective armor difficult to obtain in the murim. These too would be distributed to the Demon Venerables and the heads of each organization if war broke out.

Father had been steadily preparing for war.

Father, I respect you more than anyone, but war alone must not happen.

Father, too, knew that I felt this way. Even so, the reason he was showing me this was to persuade me. To say this was the path you and I must take.

Then Father said something unexpected.

“Choose one from among these.”

Truly unexpected words followed.

“It is my gift to the Young Cult Leader.”

Because it was a gift I had not anticipated, I was all the more moved.

After becoming the Young Cult Leader, I had heard “Young Cult Leader” countless times, but now I felt as though I had truly become the Young Cult Leader.

“May I really choose anything here?”

“If you become the Heavenly Demon, it will all be yours anyway. Though of course, for now, it is only one.”

Choosing one from among these precious treasures was enough to make my heart tremble. Which among them would be the most helpful to me?

I passed by the weapons and spiritual medicines and walked elsewhere.

“Where are you going? There are no spiritual medicines there.”

Father naturally thought I would choose one of the spiritual medicines. Since I had the Black Demon Sword, the greatest sword after the Heavenly Demon Sword, I had no need for weapons.

“Since you showed me that side first, I somehow feel there must be something better over here.”

I continued walking as I looked through the items in the treasury. There truly were all sorts of things. There was a cushion that replenished yin energy, and an incense burner that invigorated yang energy. There was also an iron doll that performed martial arts through mechanical devices, and a statue of a beautiful woman that danced. The iron doll’s martial arts seemed as though they would be peerless divine arts, and if the statue danced, it seemed as though it would display peerless footwork.

But I kept walking past them.

There were all sorts of precious, splendid, and wondrous things, but none of them drew my heart.

Just as I was about to turn back, something caught my eye.

It was a square lump of metal the size of a palm.

“What is this?”

“It is called a Secret Coffer.”

If it bore the name coffer, that meant it was a box, but I could not see a lid anywhere.

“What is it used for?”

“I do not know either.”

When I looked back at Father, he shook his head with an expression that said he truly did not know.

“It is an object that has been passed down in our cult since some unknown time. Nothing has been discovered about where or how it is used, and only the words that it will open only for one who has a bond with it have been passed down.”

“What about you, Father?”

Father nodded.

“To me as well, it was nothing more than a lump of metal.”

It opens only for one who has a bond with it.

Could this feeling that drew me to it now be that bond?

I wanted to choose this. More than anything else, this dull lump of metal drew my eye.

Even though I knew full well that I was more likely not the one with that bond, there was this feeling in me that wanted to choose it from among all these treasures. I did not hesitate long.

“I will take this.”

Chapter 302: Now Is the Time for Us to Grow Stronger.
-----------------------------------------------------
“Are you serious?”

That single question from Father contained the meaning, “Are you truly going to make such an uncertain choice?” There was a chance I might have no affinity with it, and even if I did, something completely useless might pop out.

“If it has no affinity with me… I’ll use it to bash in an enemy’s skull. Or I can melt it down and make a sword out of it.”

“Well, in any case, the choice is yours.”

“I’ve shown you nothing but perfection until now, so in the sense of letting you know that I do make mistakes from time to time, I’ll choose this.”

I slowly reached out toward the secret coffer.

The moment I carefully placed my hand on it—

“Ahhh!”

At my deep sigh, Father asked,

“What is it?”

“When I picked this up, I was supposed to feel some kind of thrilling sensation, or hear some mysterious guuuuung sound that only I could hear…”

I sighed and added,

“I feel nothing.”

Father shook his head.

“It was your choice. Among the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng over there, there should have been a bottle containing a few drops of Gongcheong Stone Milk.”

“No, right? Please tell me you’re joking!”

I rushed over to where the spiritual medicines were. There really was a bottle of Gongcheong Stone Milk nestled among the spiritual medicines and herbs. It seemed to be saying, You didn’t see me? This milky, resplendent glow?

“You should have told me sooner!”

“Why would I tell you, when I ought to have hidden it on purpose?”

I put on a pained expression and asked Father,

“Don’t tell me there’s something else too?”

Father was thoroughly enjoying teasing me.

“This is, in name and fact, the treasury of our cult. How could that be all? There is also the Bingji Snow Fruit.”

I went to Father’s side and spoke in a low voice.

“Let me change my choice.”

“Impossible!”

Right. If he let me change it, he wouldn’t be Father.

“Don’t use any of these! Make sure you leave them exactly as they are! Keep them until the day I become cult leader! Father!”

I thought Father was amazing for keeping such things stored away. Would Father really have no greed for them? He must have been saving them not for now, but for some decisive moment. As expected, Father had been drawing a larger picture all along.

I looked down at the secret coffer and said,

“You little thing, you’re more precious than Gongcheong Stone Milk!”

I was only joking around with Father for no reason, but still, my instincts were drawn to this secret coffer. I didn’t feel that sinking chill in my chest that came when I had made the wrong choice.

Even if I were told to choose again, I felt as if I would choose the secret coffer. This feeling was not some foolish stubbornness desperately denying a mistaken choice.

Because this dull lump of metal was still pulling me in with a fierce force.

Father was no different from me. On the outside, he wore an expression that said, Serves you right for fooling around, but on the other hand, he seemed expectant. As if wondering whether I might be able to unravel the secret he had failed to solve.

“It will open when the time comes. ‘O fated one, you have chosen well! I was offended that you compared me to some paltry Gongcheong Stone Milk!’ Like that.”

“Yes, you ought to live with at least that much hope.”

Shouting “O fated one!” once more, I tucked the secret coffer into my robe.

Ah, why is it so damn heavy?

Together with Father, I left the Heavenly Demon Treasury.

We conversed as we retraced the path we had taken in. In truth, today I had received a gift far greater than the secret coffer. The fact that Father had given me a congratulatory gift. That was the greatest gift of all.

“What will you do now?”

“I have to train. When I’m tired from training, I’ll play with the Demon Venerables. Even if you ask if I’m playing again, I’ll play. Even if you ask if I’m not sick of it, I’ll play.”

Watching Father’s mood, I asked casually,

“Should I call you too when I play?”

Father snorted, but he did not tell me not to call him.

“Father. What I said earlier about wanting to go out and have fun together was not idle talk. Come out with me sometime soon. If Strategist Sama tries to stop us, the two of us will secretly run away in the middle of the night. We’ll just dash off with the Heavenly Demon Flight Art and Rapid Steps.”

In the end, Father did not say, Let’s do that.

“Then I’ll take my leave. Thank you very much for the gift.”

I bowed politely and was about to turn away when Father spoke.

“Let us play a game of baduk sometime soon.”

I smiled brightly and said,

“You’ll need to be on your guard from now on. My strategist has started learning baduk.”

* * *

When I came out of the Heavenly Demon Hall, the day-shift guards, including Jeok Yeon, fell into line behind me in perfect order. It was a silent greeting and an expression of welcome.

“You’ve all trained hard.”

Just by looking at their revealed presence and the way they walked, I could tell the results of their training. It seemed they had trained quite fiercely.

Yes, let’s all become stronger. Because right now, it is time for all of us to grow strong.

From there, I went straight to find the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Elder!”

I ran toward him happily as usual, but then stopped in my tracks.

An unfamiliar sight was unfolding before me.

A flower garden had been made in the yard, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was moving that massive Heaven-Destroying Great Saber with meticulous care as he pruned the branches of flowering trees.

“Oh my! I thought I’d come to the Southern Blade Sect, but I’ve arrived at the Northern Heaven Sword Clan instead. It has been a long time, Sword Venerable. How have you become so unsightly in the meantime?”

“Stop spouting nonsense and sprinkle some water over there.”

“Yes.”

It seemed that lately, while getting along well with the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, he had learned from her how to grow flowers.

Judging by appearances alone, it was something that did not suit him at all, but I knew. Tending a flower garden suited the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who loved books, quite well. Beneath that manliness, there was the Blade Demon’s softness.

“If you give too much water to one spot, it will die. Spread it evenly.”

I watered another area as the Blood Heaven Blade Demon instructed.

“You seem to be getting along well with Senior Sword Venerable these days.”

“She’s busy after belatedly becoming absorbed in martial arts training. She says she doesn’t want to show me her face without makeup and won’t even meet me.”

It seemed that after our last spar, she had devoted herself to martial arts training. For the Sword Venerable, who loved adorning herself, not to put on makeup meant she was truly serious.

“It’s because of you, isn’t it?”

“No.”

“As if it isn’t. At her age, what competitive spirit is this?”

Because there are things that only hit home after one grows old. Things you thought were nothing when you were young now look different, don’t they? It’s the same for you, Elder, isn’t it? Gifting a blade to your disciple and growing flowers in your yard? Would that have been even remotely possible in the past?

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon added one more thing to that.

“Don’t just look after the masked fellow. Look after the drunkard too.”

“Brother Drunken Demon?”

“On the day I returned, he was drinking on the roof, waiting for you.”

“Good grief, my pathetic brother. He got drunk and acted pitiful again.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon said only that and spoke no more about the Drunken Demon. In truth, the fact that he had looked after him at all was change enough.

“There, done. That’s enough for today.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stopped pruning and went inside the house.

When I tried to follow him in, he attempted to chase me out.

“You’ve seen my face, so that’s enough. You don’t need to concern yourself with me for no reason.”

“This place is the most comfortable and pleasant for me.”

“It’s uncomfortable for me. I’m comfortable alone.”

I quickly slipped in and threw myself onto the bed, but with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s telekinetic grasp, I stopped in midair, floating.

“I am the young cult leader! The young cult leader, I say!”

My body slowly flew toward the window.

“Even if you become cult leader, you still can’t! Wash up, change your clothes, then lie down.”

While the Blood Heaven Blade Demon changed his clothes, I sat by the window. On the table lay the book the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had been reading. It was a book about tending a flower garden.

It was a book unrelated to saber arts, but I believed his Heaven-Destroying Great Saber would grow even more powerful and fearsome. Because sometimes, one gains enlightenment not while cutting off an enemy’s neck, but while pruning the branches of a flowering tree.

Besides, if the Ilhwa Sword Venerable rushed into training, there was no way the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who already trained diligently, would stay still. If only to avoid losing to the Sword Venerable, he would run alongside her. It had not been my intention, but all of us were growing stronger together.

“Elder. Do you know what this is?”

I took the secret coffer from my robe and showed it to him. I expected him to reveal curiosity and ask what it was, but he continued changing clothes and spoke as if it were nothing special.

“It’s the secret coffer.”

“How do you know?”

“How would I not? In his younger days, the cult leader made such a fuss trying to solve that thing’s secret.”

It was a moment that once again confirmed that there had been a time when the young Blood Heaven Blade Demon and Father had been close. Compared to then, their relationship seemed a little distant these days.

In any case, if Father had made such a fuss over it himself, he should have stopped me before I chose it. It really was just like Father.

“Why is that in your hands?”

“I received it as a gift for becoming the young cult leader. No, I chose it.”

“Why would you choose that of all things?”

“It opens only to one with affinity. How could I possibly endure after hearing that? You would have chosen it too, Elder.”

“I wouldn’t have. There is no way a man as unlucky as I am would have any affinity with treasure.”

It was something the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had said when we first met.

That his life had been accompanied by misfortune.

I stared at him and asked,

“But not anymore, right?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon smiled faintly and nodded.

“Now, I might choose it.”

Of course. That’s how it should be. Even if only for my sake, when I came running to see you right after meeting Father, that is how you should be.

My gaze turned toward the secret coffer.

“One day, it will suddenly click open. I’ll tell it every day, Open, please open, until it opens, so even if only because it gets sick of hearing me, it’ll open.”

After putting the secret coffer away, I rose from my seat.

“Then I’ll be going. Don’t forget. I came to see you first, Elder, even so.”

“Showing off, are you?”

Though he spoke that way, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon would be inwardly pleased.

After leaving the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s residence, I returned to my own. I had originally planned to see the Fist Demon too, but I postponed it until tomorrow.

I did not tell the guards to rest. It was the first day of my return, so how much must they have wanted to stand guard?

“I’ll be in your care!”

“Do not worry and rest well!”

Leaving the guarding to them, I entered the house. Though I had been away from home for quite some time, it had been cleaned neatly.

“Still, home is nice.”

I lay down on the bed and slept first.

Since leaving the cult, there had not been a single day when I had slept deeply. I had substituted sleep with circulation of internal energy, and poured all my remaining time into martial arts training.

Today, I intended to sleep well and recover from the fatigue of travel. No matter how much of an expert one is, long-distance carriage travel is tiring.

* * *

When I woke, it was already night.

I had truly slept deeply without dreaming even once. The refreshed feeling after a good sleep put me in a pleasant mood.

Lying down, I stared for a while at the night sky visible through the window. Come to think of it, the scenery from here while lying down was nice.

After rolling around on the bed for quite some time, I went outside.

The guards had been replaced by the night shift.

“You are awake?”

The one leading tonight’s night shift was Samho.

“I’m hungry. I want to eat home cooking made by your mother.”

A smile naturally spread across Samho’s face.

“I thought you might be hungry when you woke, so we prepared a simple meal.”

Another guard brought the prepared dishes into the room.

“Let’s all go in and eat together.”

“We have already eaten.”

“How do you eat your meals?”

“We eat before the night duty begins, and at dawn we take turns eating.”

“Your days and nights have already been reversed, so don’t eat carelessly. Eat properly. You’ll ruin your stomachs.”

“Thank you for your concern.”

“Don’t just say thank you. Make sure you do it!”

I went into the room and ate.

They had called it a simple meal, but it was a proper dinner. Since they did not know when I would wake, they had prepared dishes that would be fine even if they cooled.

After eating, I drank a cup of warm tea. Sitting by the window and drinking tea, my mind grew calm.

It was truly a joy of daily life that I had not felt in a long time.

If there is joy in being with people, there is also happiness in quietly being alone like this. The more I joke around when I am with people, the more precious time to myself becomes.

Then I thought of my life before regression. Back then, I had been sick and tired of being alone. People are so fickle.

After finishing my meal, I took the secret coffer from my robe and placed it on my palm.

I examined it carefully.

Just what is this? If it is a box, what could be inside?

I shook the secret coffer. It just felt like a solid lump of metal, and did not feel like a box at all.

Father and the previous Heavenly Demons must have tried all sorts of things. Infusing it with internal energy, soaking it in water, applying heat to it.

So I intended to do nothing.

They said it would open if there was affinity. Not that it would open if its secret were solved.

“Fine. If you don’t want to open now, open when it’s convenient for you.”

As I was looking at the secret coffer like that, Samho spoke from outside.

“The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon has come.”

When I went out, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon, wearing his white mask, was indeed standing beneath the moonlight.

“Smiling Demon!”

“Young cult leader!”

“I was going to visit you tomorrow. What brings you here?”

I tensed, wondering if something had happened, but the reason he had come was something truly unexpected.

“I came because I missed you.”

“!”

“Until now, you were always the one who came to find me, Young Cult Leader. There should be times when I come to find you as well.”

The moment I heard those words, my heart swelled. Whenever I went to him, he had always been standing there looking at a white wall. No matter how much that helped his martial arts training, the thought of him alone in a room with nothing in it had always weighed on my heart.

But now, he had come out of that room of his own will and come to find me. Today, Father had descended from the Grand Preceptor’s chair, and the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon had come out of the white room.

The faces of the guards standing watch around the residence were full of astonishment. They likely never dreamed that the fearsome Extreme Evil Smiling Demon would say such a thing.

“How was your journey outside the cult?”

“Thanks to your concern, I returned safely.”

The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon’s gaze had grown even deeper than before.

“Somehow, your aura feels different, Smiling Demon.”

“Thanks to the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng you gave me, Young Cult Leader, I made progress in my martial arts. I am truly grateful.”

“How could that be because of a mere ginseng? It was thanks to your efforts, Smiling Demon.”

Perhaps the expression mere ginseng was amusing, because the Smiling Demon laughed aloud.

“I have seen your face, so that is enough.”

As if he had truly come not on any other business, but because he wanted to see me, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon immediately bid farewell.

There are times when a single action is more moving than a hundred kind words. This was one of those times.

I went to the side of the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon as he turned and walked away, and walked alongside him.

“What do you mean, enough? It’s been so long since I’ve seen you, and we can’t part like this. I’m going to play with you all night, Smiling Demon. I’ve already declared it to Father too, that I’m going to play.”

After a brief pause, the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon said,

“Judging by how things have gone so far, I do not know whether the fate of the murim will allow you to play, Young Cult Leader.”

Even without going as far as something grand like fate, I probably wouldn’t be able to play around because I myself would be in too much of a hurry. Of course, today was an exception.

“Then we’ll have to hurry. Before that thing called fate gets in our way.”

Chapter 303: Because Our Relationship Is as Light as a Feather.
---------------------------------------------------------------
I was with Geukak Soma atop a giant stone statue erected in the Grand Martial Training Ground.

I was sitting on the fingertip of the Demon Ghost Statue’s outstretched hand, and Geukak Soma was standing beside me. Looking down from there, the magnificent yet splendid night scenery of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult came into view all at once.

I had suggested we play all night, but what could we possibly do for fun? Simply being together like this with Geukak Soma was what playing meant.

Suddenly, I recalled our first meeting.

The sight of him standing alone, absorbed into the white room, and the first words I had spoken to him.

“What are you looking at?”

Then Geukak Soma had answered thus.

“I was not looking at anything.”

Now, I asked him again.

“What are you looking at?”

Then Geukak Soma answered.

“I’m watching someone slack off. Do you see that bastard with the torch beneath the western wall over there? He was supposed to be patrolling back and forth from there to there, but he’s been sitting on his haunches taking a break for a while now.”

At the unexpected answer, I laughed aloud. It was a laugh of joy at how much he had changed.

Thanks to the Divine Eye Technique, I could even see the bored expression of the martial artist, who looked as tiny as an ant. My telepathic message flew toward him. It was the Thousand-Li Voice Transmission Technique, something I had become able to perform as my internal energy and martial arts reached a higher realm.

“You should stop slacking off. A scary person is watching.”

At my telepathic message, he sprang to his feet and began moving.

“I suppose he heard Master Soma’s words. His hearing is keen as well.”

Even at my joke, Geukak Soma couldn’t hide his surprised look. He had realized that I had sent a Thousand-Li Voice Transmission. He was the one who knew my abilities best.

Our gazes turned once more toward the man who had snapped to attention. After his patrol had gone back and forth a couple of times, Geukak Soma abruptly spoke.

“If it hadn’t been for the Young Cult Leader, I would have died then.”

Owing someone something can certainly lead a relationship in a favorable direction. If it is a life debt, there is all the more reason.

But I did not want to form such a relationship with Geukak Soma. Rather, I thought this life debt was an impediment to our relationship.

“If it hadn’t been for me, you wouldn’t have been in danger of dying in the first place.”

I stood up from where I had been sitting and looked at Geukak Soma.

I conveyed my true feelings to him.

“I hope you do not think you owe me your life.”

“Why?”

“Because then the relationship becomes heavy. The moment you feel indebted to someone, the relationship becomes distorted in some way or another. When you want to vent your frustrations, when you want to curse at someone, you can’t, can you? But I want to. If you are doing something wrong, I will curse you out without hesitation. And I want to be cursed at too. I want you to curse me out when I’m wrong. Because you are the one who saved my life. That is why I do not want us to become a relationship where we hold back what we truly wish to say.”

As I saw Geukak Soma’s gaze deepen, I continued.

“I believe that truly lasting relationships are light, like a feather. Like a feather that spins and flies into the air when the wind blows, and gently drifts back down to its place when the wind ceases. I do not wish to build an impregnable fortress with you, Geukak Soma. At first, it would be nice. Because it feels like the relationship stands firm and tall. But later, the bricks used to build that fortress would be made of burdens like, ‘What do I have to do this time to move you?’ or disappointments like, ‘I thought we were this close, but you only thought of me this much?’ I just wish that our relationship could remain as light as a feather, so that we may comfortably be with each other until the day we die.”

Geukak Soma’s eyes shone brightly.

Until the day we die.

I hoped he would understand that these five words were the core of what I had said. That I wished for a relationship where we would be together until the very end.

And Geukak Soma understood my true feelings well.

“I nearly died because of the Young Cult Leader. Buy me a drink later.”

This was why I liked Geukak Soma.

I answered with a bright smile.

“That’s right. Let’s make time to go see the master of Cheonhwa Tower.”

That was the very place where Geukak Soma became Soma, where he could drink with the most peace of mind.

We stood there like that on the fingertip of the Demon Ghost Statue, watching the night scenery of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult deepen.

* * *

The next morning, the first place I visited was the East Fist Gate. Having met Father and seen the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, it was only right to go pay my respects to Master.

“Master!”

Inside the Fist Demon’s residence, a spectacle as unfamiliar as the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s garden was unfolding.

The Fist Demon and Ian were engaged in a bout.

He wasn’t teaching fist techniques. He was matching Ian’s Soaring Sword Technique with his Thunderbolt Asura Fist.

What training could be more helpful than actual combat with the Fist Demon? It seemed she would achieve the great completion of the Soaring Sword Technique faster than expected. Once she attained great completion, unless they were a master of the Demon Venerable level, ordinary experts wouldn’t be able to kill her.

Everyone I met was immersed in martial arts. It made me think that perhaps the approaching destiny was pushing us toward the center of a great war.

Because I knew both martial arts well, watching the bout naturally led to such speculation.

What if it were me?

I became the protagonist of this bout. I became Ian wielding the sword, and I became the Fist Demon throwing punches. My image overlapped with the two of them above the exchange. Sometimes I performed the same movements; other times, I executed moves different from theirs.

At the end of that imagination, I even envisioned fighting the two of them simultaneously. I fought the Fist Demon with the Soaring Sword Technique, and I fought Ian with fist arts.

A fierce two-against-one battle.

When that fight ended, I organized the enlightenment I had gained from that imagination. At my current realm, I was achieving such martial enlightenment even from watching a bout without any conscious effort.

When I opened my eyes, which had been closed, the Fist Demon and Ian, who had finished their exchange, were looking at me. They probably thought I had closed my eyes because I couldn’t bear to watch the bout.

“Master.”

“Have you come?”

After exchanging greetings with the Fist Demon, Ian greeted me happily.

“Young Master.”

“Ian. You could think of me as a father, you know!”

I felt that Ian wanted to retort to my joke. But since we were in front of the Fist Demon, I could sense her holding herself back.

If it had been just the two of us, she probably would have played along with a quip like, “Ah! Do I have to defy even the natural order to get stronger?”

“Father envies you greatly, Master.”

“Me?”

“He says you now have a daughter. Indeed, I would feel the same. Me or my older brother? Ian is a hundred times better.”

Ian’s face flushed slightly.

“Did the Cult Leader really say that?”

Ever since Father had permitted Ian to receive the complete transmission of the Soaring Sword Technique, he had been an absolute existence to her.

“Father might ask to spar with you soon. Saying he wants your adopted daughter to be his own.”

The Fist Demon played along with my jest.

“Tell him not to covet my daughter.”

Since the Fist Demon was not someone who usually said such things, Ian’s face flushed even redder, as moved as she was.

“Then please continue your conversation.”

Ian stepped aside.

The Fist Demon took me and left the residence.

“There is somewhere we must go for a moment.”

The place we arrived at with the Fist Demon was in front of the lodgings where the East Fist Gate’s practitioners were staying. When the Fist Demon and I visited unexpectedly, the practitioners were startled.

The practitioners who were passing by all clasped their fists respectfully and observed propriety.

“Have everyone come out.”

At the Fist Demon’s command, all of the East Fist Gate’s practitioners assembled in the training ground. Everyone gathered, from White Fist practitioners to Blue Fist, Red Fist, and finally Black Fist practitioners, who were the highest rank. Among them was Cheon Sohui.

Since it was the first time the Fist Demon had come and assembled them like this, all the practitioners were nervous.

As the Fist Demon raised his momentum, the surrounding air grew cold and heavy. It was an emphasis on how important the announcement he was about to make was. In that breathtaking moment, a shocking announcement was made.

“From this hour today, I take Cheon Sohui as my second disciple and simultaneously designate her as the next Fist Demon.”

Everyone was startled by the utterly unexpected declaration.

Cheon Sohui was so shocked that she stood there dumbly, eyes wide and mouth agape.

Coming to her senses belatedly, she knelt down and observed propriety.

“I do not know how to conduct myself, having such a heavy position bestowed upon one so lacking. I will strive with desperate resolve and never disappoint you.”

Her voice trembled. She probably had no idea what she was even saying right now.

The person who must have been the most disappointed when Ian became the adopted daughter was naturally her. She must have thought that Ian would now become the Fist Demon’s successor. Because of that, her shock was all the greater.

“I believe you will do well.”

At those words, Cheon Sohui shed tears of emotion. I could sense what kind of resolution she was making at this moment.

Everyone cheered and congratulated her. She possessed the greatest skill in the East Fist Gate. Therefore, this decision followed the general trend, and while there were likely those inwardly dissatisfied, none would dare show it outwardly.

“Martial sister, congratulations.”

My congratulations would lend great weight to this decision as well. After all, the future Cult Leader was congratulating her.

“Thank you, Young Cult Leader.”

“You should call me senior brother now.”

“……Senior Brother.”

Having announced his successor so abruptly, the Fist Demon left the place in a flash. I followed him and exited as well.

“Why did you bring me here?”

“So that Sohui will think you had an influence on this decision, no?”

It was a consideration for me. He had brought me along to signal to the future Fist Demon that she should serve the future Cult Leader well.

“To think our Master was such a meticulous person!”

Come to think of it, the Demon Venerables each possessed their own transcendence and meticulousness. After all, they were people who had put in effort incomparable to that of ordinary martial artists, and it was a position absolutely impossible to achieve with a careless personality.

“You did well. My martial sister will become a more excellent Fist Demon than anyone.”

I tried picturing Cheon Sohui having inherited all of the Fist Demon’s martial arts. She would truly be magnificent. She would become someone who fit that position more than anyone else.

“You paid your respects upon returning, so the Cult Leader must be pleased?”

“He even showed me the Heavenly Demon Report.”

“That means he acknowledges you as his true successor.”

“I am grateful for that, but……”

I stopped walking for a moment. There was something I needed to say to the Fist Demon regarding the Heavenly Demon Report. It was something to say to the Fist Demon, not the other Demon Venerables.

“Seeing the spirit medicines prepared in the report, I could tell that Father is preparing for war.”

The Fist Demon said nothing. How could he not know that fact when he enjoyed Father’s deep trust? Should war break out, the Fist Demon would be the vanguard commander.

“I have a request, Master. It may be a very difficult one.”

“Speak.”

“When Father makes the decision to push out the Martial Arts Alliance and the Four Paths Alliance, you will be the first among the Demon Venerables to whom he reveals his will.”

The Fist Demon listened to my words in silence.

“I would not dare ask you to go against Father’s will. You are not the type of master who would bend your will simply because I asked.”

“Then what is the request?”

“Please tell me. I will be the one to persuade and stop Father, so just tell me.”

This request alone was an enormous burden. It would mean leaking such important affairs to me. Moreover, it was a situation where I did not know how I would stop Father.

Even so, the Fist Demon accepted my request.

“I shall tell you.”

He said nothing else. He did not make a show of how difficult this was. This was the true nature of the man called the Fist Demon.

“Thank you.”

I, too, said nothing else and bowed my head respectfully.

After parting with the Fist Demon and leaving the East Fist Gate, Ian was waiting at the entrance.

“Earlier, since Father was present, I couldn’t ask comfortably. Was the matter with the Gangseo Branch handled well?”

I approached her and asked, staring at her face intently.

“Honestly, you weren’t worried about me at all, were you?”

“Ah! Should I have been?”

She reacted as if joking, but I could feel that she hadn’t been worried at all.

“I miss the days when you stamped your feet and worried only about me.”

“Did I do that? I don’t quite remember.”

She, too, took my quip as a joke.

“What I vaguely remember is someone telling me, ‘Find happiness in your life!’”

“I don’t know who that was, but they didn’t mean for you to be this happy.”

Knowing it was praise for doing so well, Ian smiled brightly.

“How does it feel to have a father?”

“It’s bewildering. This is a moment when I need the Young Master’s advice.”

“I’ve never been a parent either.”

But if the relationship between parent and child is ultimately a relationship between people, there was something I wanted to say to Ian.

“If you try too hard to do everything right, Master will feel burdened too. Think about it. She took you as a daughter because she likes you. But if you try too hard, walking on eggshells, rigid and stiff—would that be nice? Or would it be better to be endlessly bright and cheerful? So funny that laughter bursts out the moment you meet. And you make mistakes along the way. It’s fine. Why? Because you’re her child. So don’t miss a chance to make her laugh. Make your father laugh. I think the answer to your relationship lies there.”

Ian spoke with a suspicious gaze.

“You have children, don’t you? About five hidden away somewhere?”

“Imagine five kids like me running around? Ah, horrifying.”

“Why is it horrifying? I think it would be truly reassuring.”

Ian's face turned red at her own careless words. It seemed she had momentarily imagined those children as ours.

She quickly changed the subject.

“Speaking of the Ghost Shadow Corps’ second team leader. I’m thinking of bringing in the younger sibling of the Ghost Spirit we met before.”

“Do as you see fit.”

The moment had finally come for Seojin to enter my life. Whether she would actually come depended on Ian.

After parting with Ian and passing through the Donggwon Gate, someone spoke from the rooftop.

“You’re so busy, so when exactly do you plan to come to me?”

It was Chwima’s voice.

Without looking up, I kept walking.

“Get in line.”

I could feel Chwima walking along the roof.

“The line is too long. Where are you going now? Poison King? Sword Venerable? Grand Young Master? Demon Buddha? Yellow Heaven Pavilion Master? Demon Monarch?”

He didn’t mention Blood Heaven Saber Demon, Fist Demon, or Extreme Evil Young Demon. He already knew I had met them.

“Were you upset that I didn’t come to you first?”

“A little. But you should see to your elders first.”

“Sure, I would have come if you waited. What was so urgent that you came here like this?”

Blood Heaven Saber Demon had told me that Chwima was waiting for me. I had also asked him to look after Chwima. For him to go this far, he clearly had something to talk to me about. I cracked a joke that only Chwima would appreciate.

“What’s this? Did you make some mistake while sober?”

At those words, Chwima smiled faintly.

About to say something, he looked behind me and spoke.

“The line really is long. See you later.”

When I turned around, someone I had never expected was walking this way. Moreover, she wasn’t even among the people Chwima had mentioned earlier. She was none other than Soul-Reaping Demon Venerable Cheongseon.

Walking slowly toward me, she said with a serious face.

“I have an urgent matter to discuss with the Young Sect Leader.”

Chapter 304: What Were You Counting On to Risk Your Life?
---------------------------------------------------------
I walked the inner garden with Cheongseon.

“I have told you before, but I consider my becoming a Demon Lord to be thanks to you, Young Cult Lord.”

“I am grateful for your words, but Seophon, your ascension to the Demon Lord seat is due to your own ability.”

“That must be why. Why I have come to find you like this today. Because you are someone who trusts me so.”

After arriving at a deserted place, I asked her,

“What is it?”

Cheongseon could not easily open her mouth. Though she had worked up the courage to come, she seemed unable to get the words out.

Was it because of martial arts?

Though the Heavenly Wind Cult Lord had taught her, since he was currently absent, there must have been many parts where she was stuck.

Or? Perhaps?

The name of one person connected to her came to mind.

After hesitating for a long while, she finally opened her mouth. I had hoped it was the former, but her worry was the latter.

“Before becoming a Demon Lord, there was a man with whom I was involved. He is Saujong of the Northern Heaven Sword Family.”

I already knew that the two had been involved. I had even used that fact in the past to make her go to the Heavenly Wind Cult Lord.

In my previous life before the regression, Saujong, under the effects of the Soul-Seizing Technique, had tried to kill the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and died instead. After his death, it was revealed that he had harbored romantic feelings for her, and at the time, the person who had cast the Soul-Seizing Technique on him was never revealed. Of course, the current me knows that person was Cheongseon.

“After ascending to the Demon Lord seat, I declared our separation to him. Yet he could not let go and lingered around me. As time passed, I thought he had forgotten me and was living well, but recently he suddenly came to me. He said that if I did not see him again, he would expose what had transpired between us.”

Saujong, a pitiful wretch who did not think to succeed on his own but resorted to blackmail using exposure as leverage.

“Was that not before you became a Demon Lord? Does it matter?”

The blackmail was not limited to that.

“There was a time in the past when I stole my senior’s folding fan at his request. He is threatening to expose that as well.”

At that time, the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord had been periodically performing the Heart-Soul Great Technique, extracting people’s hearts and absorbing their vitality.

The one who had informed me of that fact was none other than Saujong. Of course, it was not with good intentions. His purpose was to use the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord to keep me in check.

A corpse sacrificed by the Heart-Soul Great Technique was discovered, and together with the corpse, a folding fan belonging to a disciple of the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord appeared. The one who had stolen it was Cheongseon, at Saujong’s request.

Regardless of intent, thanks to Saujong the evil deeds of the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord were stopped, and I had spared her because she could not be killed merely for having drawn him in. But now this had resurfaced.

“If that matter is revealed as well, my reputation will be tarnished.”

Had she been a Demon Lord who had completely solidified her position, she would not have such worries. But because she was in the midst of seizing control of the organization and growing, she regarded this matter seriously.

“To speak honestly……”

Ghostly aura flowed from her two eyes. She did not hide her killing intent.

“I even considered eliminating him.”

“Why did you not eliminate him?”

Saujong’s swordsmanship was no ordinary skill, but even so, he could not withstand her Soul-Seizing Technique now that she had become a Demon Lord. She could have lured him separately and killed him without a soul knowing.

“There are two reasons. First, a doubt arose. This person is by nature suspicious and cautious, so why would he make such a reckless threat against me? He could end up being killed to silence me.”

It was certainly a questionable matter. The Saujong I had seen was also a cautious person who did not reveal his inner thoughts. When such a person suddenly acted recklessly, there had to be a reason.

“The second reason was something he said carelessly. Everyone has weaknesses. Could the Sword Venerable be without them? The moment I heard those words, I felt it. Ah, this person holds the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s weakness as well. Having served the Sword Venerable for so long, he is someone who could very well do so. Then I thought this was not something I should handle alone. That I must not simply kill him, and the person who came to mind then was you, Young Cult Lord.”

I smiled as I looked at her. She passes. Just earlier, while conversing with Gwonma, I had thought that the Demon Lords each possessed their own extraordinariness and meticulousness. This was exactly it.

She must have known instinctively. That she should not handle this unilaterally, but inform me. Both the first and second reasons she spoke of were warnings not to be swayed by momentary emotions.

Her instinct must have told her something else as well. That this would draw her closer to me. Her instinct was functioning well.

“You judged very well. I shall handle the rest.”

Cheongseon’s stiff expression loosened. Considering the results I had shown until now, she would be able to sleep soundly from here on. Yes, as a reward for a smart choice, she deserved at least that much.

Before parting with her, I gave her a word.

“You know that the Demon Lords the Orthodox and Unorthodox factions fear most are the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord and the Poison King. I hope that tradition of fear continues.”

Cheongseon silently bowed her head in respect and left that place. That very pride would become her greatest motivation to strive.

After parting with her, I moved my steps in the opposite direction.

Had it been a simple blackmail? Or did he truly know the Sword Venerable’s weakness? If there was a weakness, what could it be?

My heart naturally grew cold. Those who held another’s weakness and tried to gain something through blackmail were the worst of the worst.

Straight away, the place I headed to was the Northern Heaven Sword Family.

* * *

“These days, the Sword Venerable does not meet anyone.”

At my visit, the demonic guard watching the entrance spoke cautiously. As the Hyeolcheon Demon had said, she seemed to be devoted solely to martial arts training.

“Just let her know I am here.”

“Yes, please wait a moment.”

The demonic guard hurriedly ran inside.

I stood there briefly, and at that moment, Saujong was walking across the training ground.

Upon seeing me, he courteously bowed his head in greeting.

“My greetings are late. Congratulations on becoming the Young Cult Lord.”

“It has been a while, Warrior Sa.”

He showed the exact same appearance as usual. Originally, dealing with visitors to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable had been his role. But now, having been pushed down from the position of second-in-command, he had become an ordinary demonic guard.

After bowing respectfully once more, he walked on his way. Who would have known? That while treating people so nonchalantly like that, behind the scenes he was threatening the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord. I had let it pass last time, but this time would be different.

Just then, the demonic guard who had gone to report to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable returned.

“She asks you to enter.”

If you want to know how hard someone is working, look at how they use their spare time. A person who truly works hard never neglects that time we carelessly let slip by.

Just as I trained in the Heavenly Demon Guardian Spirit Art whenever I had the chance, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was the same.

She was wielding her sword in the front yard of her residence. Having received her subordinate’s report, she had been practicing swordsmanship even in that short time until I arrived, loathe to waste it.

The momentum of her sword swings was clearly different from before.

‘Truly, everyone is getting stronger.’

But what truly surprised me was not a martial achievement.

There was not a trace of makeup on her face. I had heard as much from the Hyeolcheon Demon, but to think she would have a bare face like this.

She truly seemed like a different person. Not that she was ugly without makeup, but her atmosphere was completely different.

Sensing my surprise, she made a slightly embarrassed expression.

“This is the first time. Showing my face without makeup to another person.”

I could tell.

She was not showing me her face without makeup right now.

She was showing me her will right now. That she had changed. That she was no longer the Sword Venerable who only obsessed over that former beauty. Look, at my will. My will to see you with my makeup wiped away.

“To think I see your bare face, Senior! It is an honor.”

When I exclaimed in a deliberately excited tone, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable formed a faint smile.

“What honor. It is shabby and old.”

“Rather, you are more beautiful.”

“Do not speak nonsense.”

“It is not empty flattery. You are truly more beautiful. To the point where I wonder why you wore such heavy makeup until now.”

When I spoke to that extent, she asked quietly,

“Do you speak from the heart?”

“Has no one told you that?”

“Because I have shown no one.”

It seemed she had lived for a very long time believing that wearing makeup was naturally beautiful.

“Your current appearance is far more charming. Clean and elegant.”

“What about these wrinkles?”

“They are as beautiful as the sword lines your blade created just now.”

The Sword Venerable smiled a pleasant smile.

“As expected! The only one who lifts my mood is the Young Cult Lord.”

“I merely spoke my heart.”

No matter how much she dressed up and adorned herself, could she ever match the beauty of Ian or Jin Haryeong? But at least compared to other middle-aged women, the Sword Venerable was overwhelmingly beautiful even without makeup. In her world, the Sword Venerable had been Ian and Jin Haryeong.

“I have felt it before, but you truly never tire. Even someone like you must tire from looking after people. How is it you speak such pleasant words?”

“It is because I am a flatterer.”

She shook her head and stared at me intently.

“Only those with high self-esteem can truly praise another. I envy your self-esteem.”

Judging by her appearance and atmosphere alone, she was someone who seemed to possess the highest self-esteem among the Demon Lords. But seeing her like this, her self-esteem was at rock bottom.

“I believe that getting close to the Demon Lords is the very path to my becoming stronger. I think each and every word of mine is like swinging a sword once or twice. We train until our arms fall off, so compared to that, this is easy.”

“Is dealing with people not harder?”

“Perhaps you are training too easily, Senior?”

“Ah! It seems my training intensity has been weak.”

We looked at each other and laughed. That is right—dealing with people is ten times harder.

“I have something to tell you, Senior.”

“Speak.”

“Saujong is……”

When his name was mentioned, slight discomfort flashed across her face.

“He is causing problems.”

No matter how I handled Saujong, I had to report to her beforehand. I did not speak about her weakness, nor did I mention the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord’s involvement.

“Please do not ask for the details, and leave it to me.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable nodded silently.

In my heart, I wanted to just handle Saujong without telling her.

But that could not be done. Because Saujong was a despicable man, but not a foolish one.

‘Saujong, just what are you staking your life on?’

* * *

“Third disciple, if you wish to bloom, endure until spring comes.”

Seo Daeryong sighed as he looked at the flowerpot that had gone from second disciple to third disciple.

“No, spring will never come for us.”

Since becoming the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord, he had been spending busy days every day.

His desk was piled high with work. Now that he was the Pavilion Lord, there was much to do and many people to meet. He had thought that by becoming the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord, he could change the world with his own hands. Surprisingly, the world flowed on unchanged.

“Ah! I hate working so much.”

You know those days, right? Days when you just don’t feel like working for no reason. Days when you keep staring out the window.

He thumped his head against the desk.

Even when Geom Mugeuk was the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord and helped from the side, the workload was similar. Even then, Seo Daeryong had essentially handled almost everything himself.

But when that work had been given full-blown as his own, the feeling and pressure were different. The same work felt harder.

“I miss the days I left the sect with the Young Cult Lord.”

Especially that moment when he fought in the Little Dragon Tournament and won—it was truly unforgettable. Those cheers coming from the dueling stage! That glorious moment he could no longer bring up to Ian or Jang Ho because he had boasted too much to them!

“Ah! I want to go back! To that time!”

Just then, the door clicked open.

Seo Daeryong, who had been lying on the desk, sprang up as fast as lightning and straightened his back, asking in a low voice,

“What brings you in here in such a hurry?”

Then, a welcome voice.

“If you go back to that time, who will change the main sect, Pavilion Lord?”

Seo Daeryong jumped up and shouted brightly,

“Young Cult Lord!”

Geom Mugeuk stood smiling in front of the door.

“Is it hard?”

Seo Daeryong said deliberately, putting on a tearful expression.

“It is hard.”

He had uttered words admitting it was hard, spoken so comfortably, for the first time since becoming the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord. Who would have known that the recipient would be the person who stood directly below the Heavenly Demon?

“It is hard, which is why I left it to you. Otherwise, I would have kept doing it myself.”

“How could you!”

Shouting like this after a long time, Seo Daeryong felt joy. And he realized. He had truly missed Geom Mugeuk. Being the Hwangcheon Pavilion Lord was good, being the disciple of the Hyeolcheon Demon was good—all of it was good, but still, he had missed Geom Mugeuk the most.

“If it is that hard, let us switch jobs.”

“With you, Young Cult Lord?”

“Here, let me tell you what you would have to do. Go meet Choema and listen to his worries, check if your older brother and the Demon Buddha are doing well, meet the Poison King and harvest poisonous herbs, play baduk with Father, and train in martial arts all night.”

How much he had missed Geom Mugeuk, who would run his mouth like this regardless of his status.

“Suddenly my work feels too easy? I will endure while receiving my monthly salary until I die. I have put down roots here.”

Geom Mugeuk’s comfort did not end with banter. He spoke while quietly gazing into Seo Daeryong’s eyes.

“You have worked hard, my right arm.”

At the words “right arm,” which he was hearing after so long, Seo Daeryong choked up. He had been so busy that he had forgotten that he was Geom Mugeuk’s right arm. Yes, that one phrase saying he had worked hard was enough. With someone who recognized his hard work, how could he not work harder?

Yes, one must be recognized. Because if no one recognizes you, even picking up trash fallen on the street becomes hard. Seo Daeryong was learning through experience how he should treat his subordinates.

“Hard work—my life might be someone else’s dream. I must work hard. I will work to the death! Spring has come with you, Young Cult Lord. I will make flowers bloom in my heart!”

After shouting cheerfully for a moment, Seo Daeryong asked,

“But what brings you to Hwangcheon Pavilion?”

In the past, Geom Mugeuk would have said, “Let us hang out,” but today he properly established Seo Daeryong’s authority.

Geom Mugeuk spoke with a more serious face than ever before.

“Pavilion Lord Seo, there is something I must ask of you. Let us catch one nasty villain together.”

Chapter 305: Why the Two Look Good Together.
--------------------------------------------
Seo Daeryong felt light enough to soar.

Now he understood why he hadn't been able to focus on his work today of all days. It was because something so thrilling had been waiting for him.

Seo Daeryong answered, lowering his voice.

"Capturing villains is the duty of our pavilion, Young Cult Lord."

The gravitas lasted only so long.

"Ugh, can't I at least be comfortable when I'm with the Young Cult Lord?"

In truth, what was more difficult than the work itself was showing the dignity befitting a pavilion lord. Having grown used to chatting and joking around with Geom Mugeuk, it was all the harder.

"But who is the man we need to catch?"

"Saujong."

Seo Daeryong knew him as well—the man who had once been the right hand of the Ilhwa Sword Lord.

Geom Mugeuk informed him that Saujong had gone to see Cheongseon. He left out, however, that Saujong had mentioned the Ilhwa Sword Lord's weakness, since it might be a personal matter.

"Something smells fishy. Then what is this pavilion's task?"

"Carry out a special secret investigation on him."

Once the special investigation begins, everything about Saujong will be laid bare. His assets, duties, martial arts, personal relationships, as well as his hobbies and how he spends his days. There is nothing about him we cannot uncover.

"Request support from Tongcheon Pavilion as well."

"Understood."

Then Seo Daeryong raised a question.

"But if he threatened a Demon Lord, isn't that in itself a grave crime?"

Depending on the circumstances, execution could even be ordered. That was how absolute a Demon Lord's position was within the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. The question was why they wouldn't simply resolve the matter and instead launch such a grand investigation.

"Saujong must know that as well. I want to know why someone who knows better would do a thing like this."

"Right? Why would he? If it's not a desperate struggle before death... then he must have some hidden source of confidence, don't you think?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded in agreement.

"Let's find out what it is."

"I'll begin the investigation at once."

In truth, Seo Daeryong wasn't curious about the reason at all. Right now, he was simply happy that he would be working with Geom Mugeuk.

"I'm off. If you don't want to work, switch places with me!"

Geom Mugeuk turned to leave, and Seo Daeryong called out from behind.

"Welcome back."

Though still the same mischievous Seo Daeryong, there was a newfound steadiness to him as a pavilion lord that hadn't been there before. He simply couldn't sense the change himself.

"Then I look forward to your work, Pavilion Lord Seo."

* * *

After leaving Hwangcheon Pavilion, I went straight to Thousand Poison Forest.

Thanks to the highly toxic elixir the Poison King had procured, my internal energy had increased greatly.

He had even chipped in his own money during the process. Of course, what I was most grateful for was that someone who hated going outside had personally made the trip to the Central Plains to obtain it.

Our handsome Poison King is probably out there digging the earth alone again today, searching for poisonous insects.

"Poison King! Let me dig with you!"

And then, the astonishing sight that unfolded before my eyes.

Hyeolcheon Doma's flower garden had been astonishing, and the duel between Gwonma and Ian had been astonishing too.

But if I were to judge coolly, this current sight was the most astonishing.

The Poison King and Mabul were walking out of the forest side by side. It was none other than Mabul himself. The two of them were even talking.

"Ah, to think you have such a discerning eye! Truly remarkable."

"Something like this is only ordinary."

The Poison King's bag held poisonous herbs. It seemed they had gone herb-picking together. The sight of those two together in this place, while I was away, felt incredibly unfamiliar.

In the past, Mabul and I had once made a bet in Thousand Poison Forest to find poisonous herbs. Back then, Mabul had found the herb, and we'd joked that it was all a scheme by the Poison King to exploit us as gatherers. But now Mabul had come here again?

I shouted toward them.

"Watch out! One of you two is a fake!"

At my quip, the two glanced at me. Then they pretended not to hear and continued their conversation.

"Just how did you find it so quickly? Tell me your secret."

"What secret could there be? I simply had good fortune."

"Truly, Mabul, you have an innate gift as a herb gatherer. I recommend you change professions even now."

To such nonsense, Mabul responded with complete seriousness.

"I've been thinking of taking it up as a hobby. Traveling to places with fresh air seems good for my health as well."

I shook my head, and this time shouted to Sangseon.

"Both of you are fakes!"

Sangseon merely smiled.

I truly never imagined. That these two would get along like this.

"What are you two doing right now?"

Then the Poison King spoke with an excited expression.

"Mabul found the Nine Yin Grass for me today. I had been searching for it for several years."

Judging by what he said, it wasn't his first time here.

"Was there a bet on the line this time too?"

Then the Poison King let out a sigh with a chagrined expression.

"I lost this bet as well."

"What was the stake?"

"A meal wager. Sangseon, prepare the meal."

Sangseon said with a smile.

"I had a feeling you would lose this bet as well, so I've already prepared it."

A lavish meal was spread out in the front courtyard of the residence.

I sat down among them. Sangseon brought me rice and chopsticks.

"How much does one root of Nine Yin Grass go for?"

"At least a hundred thousand taels, no?"

"And you're trying to settle it with just one meal?"

"Winning the bet is what matters."

I spoke to Mabul in a whisper.

"You're being deceived by a crooked gambler."

Mabul answered while enjoying his meal.

"The Poison King is not that kind of person."

To think such words would come from the mouth of someone as jaded as Mabul.

One pretends to trick, the other pretends to be tricked. Truly a pair of adorable people.

The Poison King pulled the dish in front of me over to Mabul.

"You eat only a little. This was made for Mabul, who worked hard."

"I'm the Young Cult Lord! Not the Second Young Master, but the Young Cult Lord! I'm someone capable of flipping the table!"

The Poison King pretended not to hear and only looked after Mabul.

"Please eat your fill."

"The flavor is excellent."

"We've only visited two branch halls, Honam and Gangseo, yet at this rate, if you traveled all over the Central Plains, you would have sworn brotherhood."

Sangseon was the one who laughed at my remark.

The two are polar opposites. One is someone who never leaves Thousand Poison Forest and enjoys his solitude, while the other is someone who traveled everywhere, meeting countless people and engaging in politics.

Seeing those two together, a sudden thought occurred to me. Perhaps, deep down, each harbored a longing for the other's life.

The Poison King might envy Mabul. Mabul, who travels the world freely. Despite his short stature, he meets people without hesitation. Meanwhile, the Poison King himself—handsome above all others—is cooped up in Thousand Poison Forest.

Mabul might envy the Poison King. Perhaps he's tired of traveling, tired of people, and now wishes to live a quiet, solitary life.

Perhaps that is why these two seem to get along so well now.

Then what kind of life do I want?

When I stood before my life after killing Hwamugi, what was I to do, and how was I to live? Whenever I had time, I kept asking myself that.

I try to avoid falling into the trap of "I'll take care of what matters first, then think!" Because something truly important might be slipping away unnoticed even now.

Mabul suddenly asked while eating.

"Gwonma has been quite active. Is that your doing?"

Taking two disciples, saving a daughter. Having even designated a successor, it was only natural he would draw Mabul's attention.

"You know my master's personality. He is not someone who would be swayed by anyone."

"If it's you, perhaps."

"You overestimate me."

The Poison King had kept his eyes on the Nine Yin Grass throughout the meal. I knew he was sincerely, utterly enraptured by that poison herb. By now, the only thought in his head must be rushing inside to concoct a new poison with that Nine Yin Grass.

"When do you plan to meet your brother?"

"Why would I run to him just because I want to? I'll go last."

"I shared a drink with the Eldest Young Master the other day. He brought you up that night."

"I only hope he didn't curse me out."

I'd never felt like seeing my brother in my entire life, but today, I wanted to see him.

"There is a loser's heart that the victor can never know. Your brother is a magnificent loser."

"That is because you remained by my brother's side, Mabul."

Mabul looked at me.

"You don't need to say such things anymore."

"I will continue. It is not flattery, but sincerity."

I felt the golden radiance emanating from Mabul's body grow even more intense.

While I was talking with Mabul, the Poison King, unable to hold back, had already taken the Nine Yin Grass into his quarters. Through the window, I could see him engrossed in cutting the Nine Yin Grass.

"Let's be on our way."

"Very well."

I left his quarters with Mabul. Sangseon did not follow, but bowed politely from where he stood.

A moment later, the Poison King's voice echoed from far behind.

"Young Cult Lord, where did you go? Where are you going! You have to help with the poison experiment!"

I had promised to help with poison experiments in exchange for him procuring those elixirs previously.

I quickened my steps, pretending not to hear.

Then the Poison King's cry reached Mabul as well.

"Let's make our next bet about finding the Returning Immortal Grass!"

A smile formed on Mabul's lips.

I felt that he, too, had changed a great deal. Just watching the path he took out of Thousand Poison Forest was proof enough. In the past, he would have chosen the shortest route and moved his stubby legs busily. Now, he slowly walked the long, winding path with the good scenery. He even paused for a moment to take in the view.

Suddenly, the words I had spoken to him outside the cave on the hill of his homeland came to mind.

—Now, stop and come out of that cramped and suffocating place.

I thought that perhaps Mabul had already left that cave behind and set out in search of a new life.

"Next time, too, I'll work you like a gatherer, Mabul, and find the Returning Immortal Grass."

Mabul answered, shifting his steps leisurely.

"The meal suited my palate... so it's fine."

* * *

At the same time, Saujong was walking through the streets of Maga Village's pleasure quarter.

Passing the pleasure house and walking further down, a small nursery appeared on the outskirts.

It was a place that sold flowers, trees, and seedlings.

That he frequently visited this nursery was already well-known even within the Bukcheon Sword Family.

There had been a large nursery he used to frequent during his time as the Ilhwa Sword Lord's right hand.

But now, having yielded that role to another demonic sword, he had taken to coming to this backwater nursery instead of his old one, sitting blankly before leaving.

Everyone understood this behavior because they knew all too well how immense his ambition had been to succeed the Ilhwa Sword Lord. Even if they heard news of his suicide, it wouldn't be too surprising. His sense of loss was immense, yet no one came to comfort him. It was the result of having run ahead single-mindedly in pursuit of success.

The nursery owner was a woman with a kind face.

"Welcome."

Without returning the greeting, Saujong sat in a corner of the nursery as if it were his own home, blankly staring at the trees. The flowers had all withered; only four-season tree saplings stood in rows.

Perhaps because him coming to sit blankly like this was not a matter of a day or two, the woman simply left him alone.

He appeared to be trying to escape the Five Desires and Seven Emotions by observing the plants, but rather than being freed from anguish, he was wandering amid worldly desires.

'I even brought men as offerings to become a Demon Lord, yet she casts me aside?'

Did she even know what it felt like to offer a man to the woman you loved? He had hurled every curse and insult at her. He never considered that he himself had been the one to try to use a man to bring the Sword Lord under his control.

The object of his love-hate was not only her.

Cheongseon, upon taking the seat of the Demon Lord, bid him farewell as if she had been waiting. The last bastion in his path to becoming the Sword Lord had crumbled.

For a time, he lived nurturing nothing but hatred. He dreamed every day. Some days he dreamed of killing the Ilhwa Sword Lord and then himself; other days he dreamed of killing Cheongseon and then himself. Some days he killed both women and then himself.
