"Now, unfold the Mind Soul Art."

Due to the Pungcheon Cult Leader's continuous testing, Geommugeuk stepped aside with Gowol for a moment.

They knew this was to find out what kind of Soul-Seizing Art she had fallen victim to, but it was also improper to remain while the Demon Lord displayed her martial arts.

Geommugeuk and Gowol took a stroll through the inner courtyard.

"You've had a hard time?"

"Not at all. I enjoy working. So please do not concern yourself."

"Don't say such things yourself. If I think you truly enjoy work, I'll make you do even more."

Gowol smiled.

Geommugeuk felt he should give Gowol a bit more information.

"Keep in mind the possibility that those who orchestrated this incident are the same group that caused trouble in the Martial Alliance previously."

Even if true, it would be a serious matter, yet Gowol was less surprised than expected.

"Did you suspect as much?"

"Given that both schemes targeted the highest echelons, I had suspected they might share the same mastermind. If the Young Cult Leader's conjecture is correct, I expect their conspiracy extends to the Heretical Alliance as well."

Indeed, Gowol had a firm grasp of the situation.

"It never hurts to be careful, so collect information with that possibility in mind."

"Understood."

"Also, stay at the Cult Leader's residence and mine for the time being. Soon, I shall obtain my father's permission and establish a main branch for Hidden Moon, even if on a small scale."

At that, Gowol carefully refused Geommugeuk's goodwill.

"I ask that you postpone that matter."

"Why?"

"If that happens, Hidden Moon becomes an official organization of the Cheonma Divine Cult. Please make Hidden Moon an official organization only when the Young Cult Leader becomes the Cult Leader."

It meant that for now, she wished Hidden Moon to remain an organization solely for Geommugeuk, carrying out only his orders.

At this show of loyalty, Geommugeuk expressed his delight with a bright smile.

His smile said, 'Your loyalty brings me such joy.' And with his eyes, he also said, 'Thank you, Gowol.'

"Military Advisor Go."

"Yes, Young Cult Leader."

"As you know, we have a long road ahead. Rest moderately as you go. I worry you'll wear yourself out working too hard."

"Do people not collapse from exhaustion not because the work is hard, but because no one recognizes their toil? Since the Young Cult Leader recognizes my efforts so well, I have no worries at all."

Gowol added with a smile.

"Besides, the Pungcheon Cult Leader was exaggerating—I don't work so hard that I would collapse."

At this rare joke, Geommugeuk laughed aloud.

The two resumed their steps.

Not knowing when they would get busy again, this stroll after so long was an exceedingly precious time.

* * *

When they returned to the residence with Gowol, Cheongseon had already left.

"How did it go?"

The Pungcheon Cult Leader had found out exactly what Cheongseon had fallen victim to.

"She has been afflicted by the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Art."

It was a martial art neither I nor Gowol had heard of. And with good reason—it was not a martial art of the Central Plains, and one that had been lost long ago.

"It is a frontier martial art said to have been lost long ago."

The reason the Pungcheon Cult Leader's expression had hardened was that it was a martial art from a particularly special place even among frontier arts.

"It is a martial art of the Blood Cult, which has now vanished."

The predecessor of the Pungcheon Cult was the Blood Cult. It was troubling that a martial art lost long ago, one that had failed to be passed down to the Pungcheon Cult, had suddenly appeared.

In every era, there were ghostly rumors floating about of the 'Blood Cult's revival.'

Naturally, the Pungcheon Cult Leader would be even more sensitive to such rumors.

But to state the result first, Hwamugi had nothing whatsoever to do with the Blood Cult.

After Hwamugi sealed the three orthodox-demonic sites, even when the Twelve Earthly Branch Kings dominated the Central Plains, there had been no incidents related to the Blood Cult's revival.

In any case, what mattered now was this:

"Can the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Art afflicting the Soul-Seizing Demon Lord be undone?"

The Pungcheon Cult Leader alternated his gaze between me and Gowol with a grave expression.

He had wanted to put on a grave act as though it were impossible, but his desire to show off ruined his performance.

In the end, he couldn't hold back and said,

"Of course. I am the Pungcheon Cult Leader, after all."

Gowol and I cheered.

"As expected! The Cult Leader is the best!"

"Amazing, Cult Leader!"

The Pungcheon Cult Leader shrugged his raised shoulders and continued showing off.

"The one who served as the medium is dead?"

He knew of Saujong's death.

"How did you know?"

"The Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Art can be cast in reverse upon the opponent when they cast a Soul-Seizing Art during intimate relations. The conditions are extremely intricate. But what reason is there to cast a Soul-Seizing Art during such a moment?"

"When trying to kill the opponent, I suppose."

"If Cheongseon had tried to kill him, he would be dead. Since the mastermind is a woman, she wouldn't have appeared personally but used a man as a medium. The one she used would have been left to die by Cheongseon's hand to avoid future trouble."

"You seem like a completely different person! Just who is the person before me? Could it be that the Cult Leader has fallen victim to a Soul-Seizing Art?"

"This is an insult, isn't it? Not praise?"

It wasn't empty flattery meant to lift his spirits. The Pungcheon Cult Leader truly felt like a different person when doing what he was meant to do.

"The dead man was merely a medium. The woman who taught him the mnemonic formula will have a Spirit-Control Seal branded on her body. If we extract and destroy it with the great technique, the implanted soul within Cheongseon will naturally disappear as well."

The Pungcheon Cult Leader's eyes sank coldly, and an icy chill flowed out.

"I shall take responsibility for my disciple's life and death. So just bring me before that damned woman."

I trust the Pungcheon Cult Leader. That he will subdue the Phantom Woman and succeed in the great technique. If I cannot trust him in matters of the Soul-Seizing Art, whom else could I trust? However, there was one problem.

"What happens to that woman when the great technique is performed?"

"If the Spirit-Control Seal is forcibly extracted, she cannot survive. If she intended to make another her puppet, she ought to have been prepared for that much."

"There is something we must find out before killing that woman."

Namely, where the Phantom King is.

If we kill the Phantom Woman, the Phantom King will surely seek revenge. I know well how much those twins care for each other.

For the Phantom King to hone his blade of revenge from the shadows, and for the target of that revenge to be the Pungcheon Cult Leader?

That was absolutely not a situation I wished for. If we kill the Phantom Woman, we must kill the Phantom King as well.

"I will inform you of the timing for the great technique. Until then, please be patient."

The frigid light that had flowed in the Pungcheon Cult Leader's eyes returned to normal.

"I was straining my eyes too hard anyway, so it's tiring."

After rubbing his eyes and joking around, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked me,

"What in the world are you thinking?"

I'm thinking of saving you. So that you won't have to live a life being targeted for revenge by the likes of the Phantom King.

"I'm contemplating how to let our Cult Leader make a glorious appearance."

Chapter 311: Now Even My Weakness.
----------------------------------
Had my words put him in a good mood?

“I shouldn’t only look after my other disciple; I must look after this one too.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord flicked his fingers and unleashed the spacetime displacement technique.

When I had learned the spacetime displacement technique from him, I had said that I would serve him as my master, at least while I was here.

“My disciple. What troubles you?”

Rather than answering the Pungcheon Cult Lord’s question, which brimmed with mischief, I looked around.

It was the center of a battlefield, where blood flowed like a stream and corpses were piled high.

“Why on earth did you open such a place? Where is this?”

“Because I thought a place like this would suit you.”

Since I knew it was not mere playfulness, I asked him in a calm tone.

“Is this what my heart looks like to the Cult Lord?”

He had been staring at me intently, and now he asked in return.

“Isn’t the reason you grow stronger every time I see you because your heart is always a battlefield?”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord, stripped of mischief, was this kind of person. One who instinctively caught the scent of blood.

“I am sorry, but my heart is like this.”

This time, I imitated the Pungcheon Cult Lord and snapped my fingers. He liked to snap his fingers like that when opening the spacetime displacement technique.

Swoosh!

The location changed. This time, it was a beautiful field where pure white petals fluttered in the wind.

“My heart is like this.”

After looking around, the Pungcheon Cult Lord smiled and asked.

“It isn’t that you dream of such a place?”

Without arguing further, I too smiled faintly.

The Pungcheon Cult Lord picked up a fallen petal from the ground and spoke.

“Stay here long enough, and there are times you get confused whether this is the real world or that is. It must be because of the desire to remain in the space I created. What about you?”

“I am the same. There are times when I want to forget everything and flee here. Because when I rest here, it feels so wonderful.”

The sun and the sea, a sandy shore, and a comfortable chair beneath a broad-leaved tree. Perhaps the Pungcheon Cult Lord also had a space of his own, like me.

“If this place becomes a sanctuary, it will be paradise; if it becomes a refuge, it will be hell. Both this place and the outside world.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord tossed the petals he held into the air and asked.

“Now, I ask again. What are you thinking? What do you intend to find out from the one behind this?”

Worry flickered across his face. I was grateful for his concern, but I could not tell him about Hwangwang.

“You are a man who carries storms with him. To think you have drawn in even those who cast Soul Subjugation Techniques upon the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord.”

“If anyone heard that, they would think those people appeared because of me.”

“If it were not for you, would this not have passed by unnoticed? In that case, one could say they were revealed to the world because of you.”

It was sophistry, yet it was also true. If it were not for me, Hwanyeo would have been remembered merely as the owner of the flower garden and then disappeared.

“Still, since this matter is related to the Blood Cult, I shall handle it myself.”

He was trying to protect me. He intended to take responsibility and put an end to this matter that began with the Soul Subjugation Technique. I was grateful that he cared enough to do this.

“I have something to ask the Cult Lord.”

“What is it?”

“What is the weakness of those who have mastered the Soul Subjugation Technique?”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord looked at me with an absurd expression, as if asking what kind of question this was.

“Now, after all this, you want me to reveal even my own weakness?”

“Is it not best to cast aside bad things quickly?”

“Gowol! Look, this is the Young Cult Lord you respect so much. Didn’t I tell you? He is not just a Young Cult Lord, but a wicked Young Cult Lord.”

Though he jested like that, the Pungcheon Cult Lord must have realized it was a question to uncover the weakness of the woman of the flower garden.

“Leave it to me. Just what are you trying to find out from that woman?”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord slowly began to walk. I followed him along a path covered with petals.

With every step, the Pungcheon Cult Lord seemed to go back against the flow of time, recalling that distant moment.

“I cannot forget when I first succeeded with the Soul Subjugation Technique. Not just me—anyone who has mastered the Soul Subjugation Technique would be the same. How wondrous it is to make people move according to my will? But after succeeding a few times, fear suddenly strikes. If someone stronger than me were to control me like this, manipulating my heart—what then?”

This was also the reason Seo Daeryong had once said the previous generation’s Soul Subjugation Demon Lord was the most terrifying among the Demon Lords. To die miserably while being controlled like a jiangshi was worse than dying cleanly by the blade.

“Do you know what happens when that period passes?”

I listened silently.

“Human hearts begin to look amusing. Seeing even the most steadfast people eventually fall to the Soul Subjugation Technique, one realizes human will is nothing special. Do you know what happens then?”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord stopped walking for a moment and looked at me.

“People begin to look amusing.”

Had he been ashamed of who he was then? His gaze, which had been fixed on me, turned toward the distant mountains beyond the path.

“When people begin to look amusing, the world begins to look amusing as well.”

“How did you overcome it?”

“Overcome? I could not.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord confessed his honest feelings.

“They say becoming a master changes you? That is like the groundless assumption that growing old brings wisdom. It is that a wise youth becomes a wise old man.”

For the first time since I had known him, the Pungcheon Cult Lord sighed.

“You would have been different. Even if you had mastered the Soul Subjugation Technique, you would not look down on others. But ordinary people like us are different. There is always a price to pay for stealing another’s mind.”

Perhaps he was confessing that he had been like that when he kept Gowol bound in shackles.

I know. Though he told me he had not overcome it, he already had. Because when he released Gowol’s shackles, he began to see people differently than before.

“Somewhere in that woman’s heart, there will surely be arrogance, conceit, and emptiness. She will look down on your heart, look down on people, and look down on the world. Somewhere in that heart, there will be a pitch-black, gaping hole.”

A pitch-black, gaping hole in the heart.

I know all too well what that means. In my life before the regression, there had been such a hole in my heart too. A hole so endlessly deep that nothing could fill it.

Then the Pungcheon Cult Lord spoke suddenly.

“Thank you.”

He believed that he had begun to fill that hole thanks to me.

“If it were not for you, I would still be in the leader’s hall of the Pungcheon Cult. Looking at the same divine objects every day, living in fear of losing them, with Gowol bound tight. At the time, I thought it was the most comfortable life in the world, but it was a life that was never comfortable. Now, I realize it was I who wore the shackles.”

Pure white petals showered down upon us.

“How was it? Was it helpful?”

What thanks could I offer to a man who had even given me his weakness?

Yes, this one phrase would be answer enough.

“Yes, Master.”

As if to ask when he had ever been serious, the Pungcheon Cult Lord laughed heartily and said in a loud voice.

“It has been a while since I came here; shall we stay a bit longer before leaving?”

I stayed for quite some time to let him fully enjoy the feeling of being a master today. Though it was spring inside, winter was approaching outside.

* * *

I entered the Maryongwon, where the Demonic Troops resided.

The Demonic Troops passing by recognized me and greeted me politely.

It had been a while since I had seen these brutes.

They were not the Demonic Troops of the past. Their eyes and movements held discipline and spirit.

And it was all thanks to this man.

“I had heard that you returned, Young Cult Lord.”

The Demonic Troop Commander, Jang Ho, welcomed me with a stately bearing. They say people change according to their station; the Jang Ho of the past and the man before me now felt completely different. Now he suited the position of Demonic Troop Commander more than anyone.

“I came because I wished to see Commander Jang.”

“I should have come to see you first. I apologize.”

“A busy man has no need for that. It is an idle man like me who should come.”

Jang Ho laughed. He knew well how busily I moved. I was someone he could not see even if he wished to pay his respects.

In any case, seeing the grisly scar on his face felt so welcome that I realized it truly had been a long time.

“How have you been?”

“I am unchanged. Rather, this warrior and Lord Seo have grown busy, so we cannot even gather for drinks.”

“Lazy people are the ones who pretend to be busy.”

Jang Ho laughed again. Like Seo Daeryong, he likely had little cause to laugh when among his subordinates. Both organizations absolutely required the authority of their leader. Yes, laugh comfortably like that when you are with me.

Whether it was Gwonma or Jang Ho. When these usually taciturn men smiled like this, I could not help but feel my own mood lift.

“You have kept my faith.”

“What do you mean?”

“Did I not say so? That you would become the greatest Demonic Troop Commander in history.”

“You overpraise me.”

It was not overpraise. He was already hearing such words.

My words seemed to remind Jang Ho of the past.

“I have not yet achieved my wish.”

“What wish?”

“When you seated me as Demonic Troop Commander, I said it then. That I wished to become a person like the Young Cult Lord. The kind of person who seems capable of overcoming any difficulty when we are together. I have not achieved that wish.”

“Is that not already achieved?”

I spoke to him as he showed a puzzled look.

“A difficult matter has arisen, so I have come to find you. I need your help.”

Jang Ho’s expression brightened. The words “I need your help” were surely more pleasing to him than any praise.

“I heard that in the Demonic Troops’ training, there is also training to counter sorcery and illusion techniques.”

“Yes, we conduct special training in preparation for unorthodox experts.”

“Soul Subjugation Techniques as well?”

“There is a special unit composed of those with innately strong mental fortitude.”

“Support me with them.”

Joy flashed across Jang Ho’s face. He had not accompanied me in anything recently; finally, there was something in which he could be of help.

“When do you need them?”

“Right now.”

Shortly after, I came out leading over twenty Demonic Troops, including Jang Ho.

I could feel the tension from Jeokyeon and the guards waiting outside. The mobilization of the Demonic Troops meant that a truly dangerous undertaking was underway.

* * *

Hwanyeo could not leave the flower garden.

It was because of the Young Cult Lord’s threat that if she left before the investigation ended, he would spread wanted posters of her throughout the Central Plains.

More than anything, she must not be revealed to the world.

But neither could she remain trapped here indefinitely when she did not know when the investigation would end.

Just then, a man pulling a cart entered the flower garden. He had come to deliver saplings and flower pots. As he unloaded a flower pot from the cart, he sent a telepathic message to Hwanyeo.

—Reporting. The Pungcheon Cult Lord has returned to the main headquarters of the Demonic Cult.

In that instant, Hwanyeo’s expression grew grave. The Pungcheon Cult Lord, who had been absent, had returned at this very moment?

‘Could it be?’

Had he realized she had unleashed the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Technique upon the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord? If it was the Pungcheon Cult Lord, he might have noticed that the demon lord had been afflicted by the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Technique. No, perhaps he had sensed something from the very beginning and summoned him for that reason.

—I believe you must leave.

Despite the worried message, she remained calm.

—Do not panic.

—My apologies.

After a moment of thought, she gave an order.

—Stand the Blood Spirits by, and tell Gwishuk that we may have guests.

The man flinched in surprise, but soon replied politely.

—Understood.

Having received his orders, the man pulled his cart and left the flower garden.

It was precisely when Hwanyeo picked up the delivered flower pot to move it that—

The wooden wind chime hanging beneath the eaves made a sound.

Hwanyeo picked up the flower pot that had been unloaded from the cart and headed toward the back of the flower garden.

The moment she turned the corner, her figure vanished in an instant.

*Ooooom.*

She was walking toward a place where a low, resonant sound could be heard. The surrounding walls squirmed as if alive, and grotesque protrusions jutted out.

It was as if she were walking inside the belly of a monster.

When she entered this place, her appearance had changed as well. Her lips had turned bright red, and her eyes were frighteningly slit.

That was not all that had changed. The flowers in the pot she held had transformed into carnivorous plants that captured and devoured insects. Judging by their eerie aura, insects were likely not all they ate.

After passing through a complex passage like a maze, she came to a small space.

When she entered that place, where a ghastly atmosphere lingered, the wall in front began to squirm and protrude.

*Screeeeech.*

A man thrust his face forward. It was a bizarre sight, as if he were struggling to push his face through a transparent membrane. Naturally, his voice was strange as well.

“He is wondering why you have not returned.”

It was a message from her younger brother.

“Tell him I cannot return yet.”

“He will be curious about the reason.”

“Later. Tell him I will explain later.”

“He is worri—”

Before the message could finish, she waved her hand.

As if flicking the man away, the wall tightened, and the protruding face disappeared.

She threw herself backward, lying down.

The floor squelched and bulged before wrapping around her like a comfortable bed.

This place was her favorite and the only space where she could rest comfortably.

She closed her eyes for a moment and fell into thought about how to handle this matter. The problem was the Young Cult Lord. The fact that she could not read his thoughts agitated her.

*Screeeeech.*

Again, a face pushed out from that wall. A face struggling to break through the membrane.

—I told you I would handle it.

The next moment, she flinched. It was not the face of the subordinate who handled the connection to her younger brother. The face pushing through the membrane seemed somehow familiar.

*Rip!*

Someone thrust their face through, tearing the membrane. Surprisingly, it was Geom Mugeuk. When their eyes met, Geom Mugeuk smirked and said.

“So there was a villain among those who like flowers?”

At that moment, Hwanyeo’s eyes snapped open.

It was a dream.

She had fallen asleep for a moment and dreamed. It was her first time dreaming of someone simply because she was conscious of them.

Then, from the outer flower garden, she heard someone calling for the owner.

“Owner! Owner, are you there?”

It was the voice of the protagonist of her nightmare.

Chapter 312: That Door Can Never Be Opened.
-------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk called for Hwanyeo in the courtyard of the flower garden.

“Is the proprietress here?”

Then Hwanyeo walked out from behind the garden.

“Welcome, Young Cult Leader.”

Greeting him warmly, she had returned to being the kindly proprietress of the flower garden.

“So you were here, proprietress. I thought you might have run away and was about to call an artist to draw a wanted portrait.”

When Hwanyeo stiffened slightly, Geom Mugeuk laughed and added,

“I’m joking. You’re innocent, so why would our proprietress run away?”

As if he had grown used to the place now, Geom Mugeuk walked toward the flowerpots set in the corner.

“You have new flowers.”

“They came in just a little while ago.”

Geom Mugeuk crouched down and admired the flowers.

The Young Cult Leader’s third visit.

‘There’s clearly some intention behind this.’

Was it because of the dream she had just had? Hwanyeo felt even more uneasy than she had during his previous visits.

Her subordinate had reported that the Cult Leader Pungcheon had returned.

Perhaps he had realized that the Soul-Subjugating Demon Venerable had been caught in the Yin-Yang Soul-Reversal Art.

‘Then just seize me and kill me. What is he hesitating for?’

The Young Cult Leader before her could ambush her at any moment. She readied herself completely so she could use illusion arts at any time.

In contrast to the tension hovering in her heart, Geom Mugeuk was perfectly calm.

“I understand why that dead warrior kept coming here. For some reason, coming here seems to put the heart at ease.”

“I’m truly grateful you think so well of it.”

“Do you know anything about that warrior?”

“No. He was a man of very few words.”

When she had used Sa Ujong, she had never imagined she would be suspected this much. Since he had been in a romantic entanglement with the Soul-Subjugating Demon Venerable, she had naturally assumed they would focus on that.

‘How did things get this tangled?’

The answer to that was crouching right in front of her.

“Do you find it strange that I keep coming here?”

“No. I heard that you were close with the people of Maga Village even before this. You are especially close with the owner of the Pungryu Tavern over there, aren’t you?”

A cold smile formed on Geom Mugeuk’s lips. Beneath those seemingly casual words lay an implicit threat: I know the person you cherish.

Geom Mugeuk rose and turned toward her. In place of the cold smile from a moment ago, a pleasant grin now rested on his lips.

“You know quite a lot about me.”

“I know at least that you are someone who does not care about another person’s status. If you can be friendly with a tavern owner, then surely you can be friendly with a flower garden owner as well?”

Their gazes tangled in the air. Their conduct toward each other was utterly natural.

“Do you have family, by any chance?”

It would have been nice if she had said, I have a twin sister. But she was not such an easy opponent.

“No, I don’t.”

“You must be lonely living alone.”

“I live thinking of the flowers and trees as my family.”

Through today’s conversation, Geom Mugeuk felt it clearly. For the Illusion King to be mentioned from this flawless woman’s lips, it would take a very long time.

Even when winter passed and the spring blossoms began to bud, it would not be easy. But this woman was far too dangerous to spend a long time working on.

“I have news to tell you, proprietress.”

If it would not work in a peaceful situation, then he would have to drive her into a corner.

“What news?”

“There’s good news and bad news. Which would you like to hear first?”

“I’ll hear the bad news first.”

“To resolve this incident, the Cult Leader Pungcheon has returned. Since the Soul-Subjugating Demon Venerable is involved, the Cult Leader Pungcheon will personally investigate this matter. In a little while, he will come here in person with the demon troops and search the flower garden as well.”

Because Geom Mugeuk revealed the information without hiding it, Hwanyeo was instead confused. If he truly suspected her, he could have simply come with the Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“The Cult Leader Pungcheon was confident. He said that if he came and examined things himself, he would surely be able to find a clue.”

She was not worried about the flower garden. There was nothing here that could serve as evidence related to illusion arts or soul-subjugation arts. Nor was there any evidence that could reveal her true identity.

The problem was herself.

What if the Cult Leader Pungcheon saw her in person? If it was him, he might be able to discern her true nature. She had been able to hide it from the Young Cult Leader, but she was not confident against the Cult Leader Pungcheon.

‘I have to leave before I clash with the Cult Leader Pungcheon.’

The reason she had not left immediately even after hearing her subordinate’s report that the Cult Leader Pungcheon had returned was precisely because of this Young Cult Leader.

Could she perhaps make it so that her wanted portrait was not released and a pursuit party was not sent after her?

If he had come ahead of time to tell her this because he truly trusted her? It was still suspicious, but if that was the truth?

“I still believe you are innocent. There is no such thing as a villain who likes flowers.”

Fine, she had nothing to lose. In any case, she was confident she could escape from the Young Cult Leader.

“What is the good news?”

“Let us dine together. I will treat you.”

Hwanyeo was surprised by the unexpected proposal. Why did he want to eat with her? To poison the food? If he wanted to subdue her, there should have been easier methods.

Truly, this man was impossible to understand. Because she could not conclude whether it was this or that, she kept getting swept along.

“Isn’t that even worse news? If I eat with someone as noble as you, Young Cult Leader, I’ll get indigestion.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed loudly.

“As expected. I had a feeling our proprietress would be a rather amusing person.”

Despite Geom Mugeuk’s banter, Hwanyeo was serious.

“Young Cult Leader, you may treat me comfortably, but it is very difficult for me. Just meeting you is hard enough, but if I meet someone like the Cult Leader Pungcheon, I will collapse. If you truly believe I am innocent, please allow me to close the flower garden for a while and visit my hometown.”

Even if her wanted portrait was released and a pursuit party chased her, would she ever be caught by them?

The problem was that the person who had entrusted this task to her would consider her incompetent.

If her identity was exposed and she was hunted by the Demon Cult, then from now on, her younger sibling would take the leading role, and she would be left to support him. That was not what she wanted. She wanted to become the light, not the shadow.

“You said you trusted me.”

“Of course.”

“Then please. Please let me go.”

“I will let you go once you meet the Cult Leader Pungcheon. I will stay by your side, so do not worry.”

Hwanyeo stared fixedly at Geom Mugeuk. The look in her eyes changed.

“For someone so noble, you speak out of both sides of your mouth. You said you did not suspect me.”

“I do not suspect you.”

Now Geom Mugeuk moved the situation into its next phase.

“Suspicion is what you do when you are uncertain. But when you are definitely the culprit, why would I suspect you?”

“!”

Hwanyeo was startled. She truly had not imagined Geom Mugeuk would say such a thing.

“To think someone who likes flowers was a villain.”

By coincidence, Geom Mugeuk had said something similar to what he had said in the dream.

Hwanyeo understood.

‘He was toying with me from the very beginning!’

There was only one thing she was curious about.

“Since when did you know?”

Hwanyeo’s aura changed. Her true self emerged—the self that had cast soul-subjugation arts on the Soul-Subjugating Demon Venerable and could stand proudly even before the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult.

“From the beginning.”

He meant he had known from the time he came to buy flowers for a woman.

Hwanyeo sighed.

“So you were trying to find out why I set all this up.”

She thought that would naturally be the case. But Geom Mugeuk’s answer was unexpected.

“You don’t know the reason either, do you?”

In that instant, Hwanyeo’s expression hardened coldly.

“You are doing this because someone ordered you to, aren’t you? You don’t know what his purpose is, do you? Whether he is trying to kill me or kill my father, he would not have told a disposable piece like you.”

Hwanyeo’s aura grew rough. The anger contained in that changed aura confirmed Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“Until now, your position as Young Cult Leader must have been the shield protecting your life. I wonder if that shield will work on me as well.”

“I do not know that either. I do not know what kind of spear you hold.”

It was right then.

Beyond the wall of the flower garden, a group of warriors could be seen approaching from afar.

The one in the lead was the Cult Leader Pungcheon, and those following behind him were the demon troops. Since he was so large, he was easy to see even from a distance.

“While they search the flower garden, let us have a meal.”

Hwanyeo accepted the proposal coldly.

“I’ll pay for that meal.”

The moment she clapped her hands—

The surroundings changed.

* * *

A space within an illusion.

Before my eyes stood a restaurant that sold wine and food.

Guihon Restaurant.

Hwanyeo’s appearance as she stood before that restaurant had changed. Her eyes were slanted, and her lips had turned crimson. Her appearance inside the illusion and her usual appearance were polar opposites.

“If I had known you looked like this, I would never have asked to eat with you.”

At my words, Hwanyeo frowned and let out a cold sneer.

When she walked ahead, the doors of Guihon Restaurant opened wide on their own.

I followed her inside.

The restaurant was bustling with customers. As soon as I entered, the hall that had been full of laughter and chatter fell silent all at once, and everyone’s gaze turned toward me.

Their empty, unfocused eyes held no souls. They were ghost souls.

The place was filled with ghost souls, and a powerful ghostly energy permeated the restaurant. The Heavenly Demon Protective Art activated on its own and began protecting me.

I slowly walked to the seat where Hwanyeo sat. Even her eyes were relaxed. I could tell that, at least here in Guihon Restaurant, she felt absolutely safe.

An old man stood beside her.

“This is Gwisuk, the owner of Guihon Restaurant.”

Geom Mugeuk could sense it. This old man, too, was an absolute master of illusion arts.

Thinking back to before my regression, the Illusion King and Hwanyeo had commanded many subordinates, just like the other Twelve Zodiac Kings. This old man named Gwisuk must have been one of them.

“Gwisuk, the person who came with me today is an honored figure of the Divine Cult, so please take special care of him.”

Gwisuk glared at me coldly.

“As if there could be anyone of noble status in the Demon Cult! They are all worthless things.”

Powerful ghostly energy could be felt in his voice. If Hwanyeo was proficient in soul-subjugation arts among illusion arts, then I could feel that the old man specialized in ghost arts.

I shouted at Gwisuk’s back as he strode away.

“See here, proprietor. Our cult has changed a great deal too, so cast aside your prejudices!”

Seeing that I was not frightened at all, Hwanyeo was impressed.

“I truly have no choice but to acknowledge it. At your age, such presence and composure are difficult to even imitate.”

From Hwanyeo’s long, slanted eyes, the energy of soul-subjugation now flowed openly. She was attempting to read my inner thoughts using soul-subjugation arts. But because the Heavenly Demon Protective Art was active, her sorcery did not work.

“If you were not trying to grasp my intentions, then why were you watching me?”

“Why, because I was curious, of course. Who could be behind you for you to commit such acts?”

“Why did you give up without finding out? If you had not mobilized the Cult Leader Pungcheon, you could have learned more about me.”

“You are not someone who would answer easily.”

As if to say I had seen correctly, she nodded.

“I judged that there was no value in spending my precious time on someone like you.”

I spoke as if I had given up on everything, but I had not given up. If this final decisive move did not work, I would take her hostage and draw in the Illusion King. I would find out even if I had to torture her. I would find out by any means necessary. This matter concerned the life and death of the Cult Leader Pungcheon. If there was truly no other way, I would turn the target of revenge toward myself and wait for the Illusion King.

This was my repayment to the Cult Leader Pungcheon, who had given me everything without reservation.

Then the waiter brought the wine first. The waiter, too, was a ghost soul with hollow eyes.

I smelled the wine.

“Isn’t this wine for the dead?”

When the words “the dead” came out, the noisy surroundings fell silent, and everyone looked this way.

Ghostly energy filled their empty eyes. If not for the Heavenly Demon Protective Art, I likely would have been seized by fear and trembling all over.

My eyes met those of a ghost soul sitting beside me. I sensed from him the desire to rush at me at any moment. But he did not dare.

Hwanyeo knew exactly why.

“Young Cult Leader, what you trust in must be the Nine Flames Demonic Art. That’s right. Since you have mastered the Nine Flames Demonic Art, you cannot be killed by illusion arts.”

This was the dignity of the Nine Flames Demonic Art. The dignity of an absolute demonic art against which no sorcery or illusion arts could prevail.

“If you knew that, why did you bring me here?”

“Because even if I cannot kill you, I can imprison you.”

I examined the surroundings with the Blood-Eye Essence and the Divine Eye Art. There was no bluish light anywhere. As in my battle with the Soul-Subjugating Demon Venerable before, finding a method to break through would not be easy.

“If I kill you, this place will disappear too.”

Even before death, she was full of confidence.

“I guarantee this. If you kill me here, Young Cult Leader, you will never escape this place. You will be trapped here with those ghost souls for eternity. The same is true if I take my own life. The moment I die, the method to break this place disappears with me.”

As if her words were true, the ghost souls all looked this way at once. Their hollow gazes seemed to say, Let us spend your whole life together.

I looked at the door we had entered through. As if reading the look in my eyes asking whether I could simply leave through that door, Hwanyeo said confidently,

“That door can never be opened from the inside.”

“The Cult Leader Pungcheon will come to rescue me.”

“Do you know what the situation is outside that door?”

With an air of leisure, Hwanyeo boasted,

“Even if the Cult Leader Pungcheon’s grandfather came, not just the Cult Leader Pungcheon, he could not open that door.”

The instant those words ended—

Creeeeeeak.

The door began to open slowly with a loud sound.

Hwanyeo, startled out of her wits, sprang to her feet. Not only she, but Gwisuk, who had stuck his head out from the kitchen, wore a grave expression as well. The ghost souls all looked toward the door at once.

The door opened, and one person stepped inside.

It was the Cult Leader Pungcheon. A ghost soul that had been clinging to his shoulder dropped to the floor with a thud. A severed ghostly hand was attached to his ankle.

“Who said that? That I couldn’t open it?”

Beyond the opened door, the outside could be seen. Just as Hwanyeo had said, the scenery was different from when we came in. It was hell itself, with flames soaring in the distance and a rain of blood falling. There, corpses of ghost souls lay endlessly all the way to the far horizon.

The Cult Leader Pungcheon had broken through those ghost souls to come find me.

“Our wicked Young Cult Leader owes me far too much for me not to collect.”

Chapter 313: What Would You Do Without Me?
------------------------------------------
The Pungcheon Cult Leader who usually joked and blustered was a completely different person from the cult leader standing here now. An unapproachable dignity flowed from him.

The reactions of the ghost souls in the tavern were different from when Geom Mugeuk had appeared. Toward Geom Mugeuk, they had shown curiosity along with blatant hostility.

But they did not dare do so toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader. They, too, had seen the hellscape beyond the open door. The corpses of ghost souls strewn across it.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader slowly passed among the ghost souls and walked over near Geom Mugeuk and Hwanyeo.

At his appearance, Gwisuk, who had been peering out from the kitchen, had a rigid expression. Everyone else could appear, but the one person who must not appear had shown up.

Seeing that reaction, Geom Mugeuk had a thought. Perhaps these two people knew the Pungcheon Cult Leader even better than he did.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, who had walked up to where the two of them stood, looked at Hwanyeo and said coldly,

“Was it you? The bitch who touched my disciple?”

For an instant, Hwanyeo’s expression twitched. Had she ever heard such abuse before? And if she had, would she have endured it?

But she did not dare ask back, “That pathetic bitch was your disciple?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader and smiled.

To think the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s presence within an illusion could feel this reassuring.

“Inside an illusion, Cult Leader, you are the Heavenly Demon.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s shoulders shot up toward the heavens.

“What would you have done if it hadn’t been me, after being dragged all the way here?”

“I had faith in you, Cult Leader.”

As they exchanged banter, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked with his eyes whether he had found out what needed to be found out. Geom Mugeuk gave a slight shake of his head.

Then, as if to say he would try to find it out himself, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked Hwanyeo,

“The Yin-Yang Soul-Reversal Art is a soul-capturing art of the Blood Cult that was lost long ago. How did you learn it?”

Hwanyeo said nothing. The Pungcheon Cult Leader did not know her, but she knew the Pungcheon Cult Leader well. After all, the name she had heard most often while learning illusion arts was neither the Heavenly Demon nor the Martial Alliance Leader, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“So the famous you has become the Demonic Cult’s dog.”

Geom Mugeuk watched to see how the Pungcheon Cult Leader would react to her provocation.

He was the Pungcheon Cult Leader who always flared up at Gowol’s provocations.

He clearly showed how different the heart that faced a friend was from the heart that faced an enemy.

“Isn’t a dog a hundred times better than people who hide in the shadows and weave conspiracies?”

Just then, a ghost soul from behind rushed in and launched a surprise attack.

At the very moment it tried to bite the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s neck.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader swiftly turned and seized the ghost soul by the collar.

“Where did you learn such bad habits? Biting from behind—just like your master!”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader slapped the ghost soul across the cheek.

It looked like a playful smack, but within the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s hand movement was the subtlety of illusion arts that had reached the pinnacle.

For a moment, light seemed to flash in the soulless ghost soul’s eyes, then it vanished.

Thud.

The ghost soul that collapsed to the floor had become a corpse.

The ghost souls that had been in the same place as the ones killed earlier rushed in again.

This time as well, the Pungcheon Cult Leader dealt with them naturally. After lightly subduing them as they charged fiercely, he slammed their faces together.

Thwack!

This time, too, the two ghost souls’ eyes lit up, then went completely dark. They did not die from the impact of the collision. It was because of the profound energy contained in the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s hands.

The ghost souls all rose from their seats at once and let out shrieks.

Kihihihihihihihi.

It was a ghostly wail that shook the human heart.

Geom Mugeuk’s protective astral qi activated, controlling the sound that shook the soul.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader shouted,

“Silence, you bastards!”

As if he had unleashed a Lion’s Roar, his booming shout rang out, and the ghostly wailing disappeared.

At the very moment the ghost souls all became frightened.

Gwisuk quietly recited an incantation. Then, ghostly energy began surging from the bodies of all the ghost souls at once.

Hwanyeo, too, leaped back and began reciting an incantation.

Then madness flowed from the ghost souls’ eyes, as though martial artists had taken a drug to amplify their internal energy. It felt as if those who had grown twice as strong because of Gwisuk were growing twice as strong again.

Even so, Geom Mugeuk did not stop them. The simplest way to end this fight would be to eliminate those two, but Hwanyeo absolutely must not be killed. He had to have her remove the Yin-Yang Soul-Reversal Art placed on Cheongseon through the great rite. It was also his final chance to learn about the Phantom King.

Emitting ghostly energy and killing intent, the ghost souls from every direction rushed in at once. They aimed for vital points and swung both palms. They swung, clawed, bit, and tore while releasing suffocating ghostly energy. Truly, their entire bodies were weapons.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader stepped forward and faced them.

He personally showed how he had broken through that multitude of ghost souls outside the door.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s movements were faster than expected. The assumption that his martial arts would be weak because he had learned illusion arts was nothing more than prejudice.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader roamed among the ghost souls. Using light footwork as if dancing, he tapped their foreheads and chests with his palm.

It looked light, but it was by no means a light technique. At those gestures, the ghost souls that had been rampaging madly dropped like bundles of straw.

Black energy rose from the bodies of Hwanyeo and Gwisuk. Their incantations were recited even faster, and the ghost souls grew even stronger.

The ghostly energy filling the entire hall made it hard to breathe. The Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art activated even more powerfully and protected my body. If not for the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art, it would not have been easy to withstand this energy.

In contrast, the Pungcheon Cult Leader dealt with them without the slightest agitation, just as he had at the start.

He used ghost souls as shields to block attacks, and he lifted them like dolls and swung them around. Whenever they were struck on the cheek, the back of the head, or the stomach, they became corpses.

As they disappeared one by one, the expressions of Hwanyeo and Gwisuk grew increasingly stiff. In the end, they gave up reciting their incantations. They had judged that these things could not possibly do the job.

Then the remaining ghost souls became corpses even faster.

Thwack!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader delivered a proper blow to the last ghost soul. Its entire body shattered, and it flew between the two of them before crashing into the wall.

In the blink of an eye, all the ghost souls inside had become corpses and collapsed. Though since they had no souls to begin with, calling them corpses felt awkward.

Gwisuk and Hwanyeo looked at each other. They had not expected to be able to kill the Pungcheon Cult Leader with ghost souls, but they had not thought he would deal with all of them without a single injury.

Geom Mugeuk was sincerely impressed.

“I had no idea you were this nimble, Cult Leader.”

“I lost weight because of Gowol’s nagging.”

Like seeing someone you never knew was a painter paint a picture, or someone you never knew was a musician play an instrument, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, now revealing his skill, was impressive.

Just looking at those eyes now was enough. Even in a situation where he would normally have joked and blustered endlessly, he was looking at the two of them with deepened eyes. On the battlefield, he did not let down his guard.

As if the real fight began now, Gwisuk stepped forward.

Calmly watching him, the Pungcheon Cult Leader said to Geom Mugeuk,

“The ghostly energy is thick. Be careful.”

He did not look down on Gwisuk.

Gwisuk strode forward, and the Pungcheon Cult Leader also walked out as if to meet him.

“You are a disgrace to the martial world beyond the borders.”

Gwisuk, too, stabbed at the fact that the Pungcheon Cult Leader had joined hands with Geom Mugeuk. He thought it was the greatest weakness, but that was because he did not know what kind of relationship the Pungcheon Cult Leader had formed with Geom Mugeuk.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded.

“Let’s say I’m a disgrace. Then what are you? The pride of the martial world beyond the borders?”

Because he had gained Gowol and Geom Mugeuk, he did not feel angry at all, even hearing himself called a disgrace. In the past, those words would have made him jump up and down.

“If you’re going to insult someone, you should first reveal who you are, shouldn’t you? Who might you be?”

What could they, who operated while hiding their identities, possibly say?

Instead of answering, Gwisuk called out his subordinates in the kitchen.

Men dressed as cooks came pouring out. There were so many of them that it made one wonder if there had really been that many people in that kitchen.

There were nine in all. What they held in their hands were all square kitchen knives. Red ghostly energy flowed from their eyes and blades.

They, too, were not living people, but far more powerful ghostly energy overflowed from them than from the ghost souls that had rushed in earlier. If the ghost souls had been disorderly, these fellows were well-trained martial artists.

This time, Geom Mugeuk stepped forward.

“I’ll handle them.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at Geom Mugeuk. Worry flickered in his eyes. No matter how capable Geom Mugeuk was, this place was a world within an illusion.

“I think what comes next will be stronger, so I’ll take care of the weaker ones first.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s ease, the Pungcheon Cult Leader cast off his worry. Yes, if he did not trust Geom Mugeuk, who could he trust?

On the other hand, the cooks’ momentum grew even sharper. They were beings who existed only within the illusion, but they understood human speech and even possessed emotions.

They rushed toward Geom Mugeuk.

Whoosh! Swish! Whoooosh!

The knives that flew in with sharp sounds of cutting air began to change.

Their attacks were fast and ever-changing. On top of that, they employed perfect combined assault techniques. In an instant, dozens of lines of blades embroidered the air.

Clang clang clang clang clang clang clang!

This time as well, Hwanyeo and Gwisuk tried to pressure Geom Mugeuk with ghostly energy while reciting incantations.

Then the Pungcheon Cult Leader released his aura and blocked their energy.

At the boundary where Geom Mugeuk and the cooks were fighting, two forces collided. It was a two-against-one battle of energy, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader was not pushed back.

Slash!

Geom Mugeuk’s Black Demon Sword cut across the chest of the cook attacking at the very front. The attack went in deep enough to bisect the body, but the opponent did not fall.

The second one was the same. Even after his heart was pierced, he did not collapse. No fatal wound could stop them.

Unlike the ghost souls, they were beings that could not be killed unless one found the precise method to break them and struck it.

Even as they were pushed back by the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s internal energy, smiles appeared on the faces of Hwanyeo and Gwisuk. They were certain that no matter how extraordinary the Young Cult Leader was, he would not be able to face them here.

If Geom Mugeuk had not put the Blood-Eye Essence in his eyes, and if he had not received the Divine Eye Art, this fight would surely have become a blood-draining struggle just as they expected.

But when he activated the Blood-Eye Essence and Divine Eye Art and stared intently at them, one part of each body was glowing bluish. For some, it was an arm; for others, it was the left knee.

When he knew exactly where he had to stab, how could they possibly be a match for Geom Mugeuk?

The Dark Shadow Step, Flicker Step, and Dark King Step he had been saving were unleashed in succession. The Four Wind God Steps became faster and more precise than before. Geom Mugeuk could feel it himself, so those watching could not help but be astonished.

In a flash, Geom Mugeuk moved as if he had transcended time and space.

When he thrust his sword precisely toward the bluish-glowing weak points, those who had seemed as if they would keep attacking even with their heads cut off died on the spot and collapsed.

Each time Hwanyeo and Gwisuk’s shock continued, another one fell.

Gwisuk tried to intervene midway, but he could not find the right moment. Geom Mugeuk revealed no opening whatsoever, so if he carelessly stepped in, it was certain he would die by that sword along with them.

And so the nine cooks became corpses and lay strewn about in an instant.

Gwisuk asked with a startled face,

“How did you find their weakness?”

He was shocked that Geom Mugeuk had been able to see it.

Geom Mugeuk expressed his gratitude to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“It was thanks to you, Cult Leader.”

If he had not blocked those two people’s ghostly energy, his body would have been much heavier. No matter how clearly he could see their weakness, it would not have been an easy fight. In this battle as well, the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s contribution had been great.

Gwisuk misunderstood that expression of gratitude.

“As expected, you are a cowardly man.”

He thought the Pungcheon Cult Leader had told him their weakness. In a sense, it was true. The Blood-Eye Essence, too, was a sacred relic of the Pungcheon Cult.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader did not bother making excuses.

“You bring out things that don’t die even when their hearts are stabbed, and you dare call someone else cowardly?”

After saying that, the Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at Geom Mugeuk. Their gazes tangled in the air. They were both deeply worried about each other, but on the other hand, their trust in each other was growing. After all, this was the first time they had ever truly fought together like this.

This time, Hwanyeo stepped forward.

“This time won’t be easy.”

Blood began dripping from the ceiling.

Each and every drop gradually swelled upon the floor, and before long rose up as a hideous monster. One after another, beings so revolting and terrifying that the mere sight of them was enough to turn the stomach lifted themselves upright. They were larger even than the demon soldiers. Dozens of enormous monsters filled the entire restaurant.

“I don’t know how you figured out the method to break it, but these blood spirits will be different. Blood spirits can never be killed unless this space itself is broken.”

Indeed, there was no bluish light to be seen on the beings called blood spirits.

Geom Mugeuk slowly edged backward and said to Cult Leader Pungcheon,

“I told you the next ones would be stronger.”

“The ones after these will be even more dreadful, though?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon had read Geom Mugeuk’s thoughts exactly.

“Are you worried about me?”

He was saying that they surely still had another move left, and that Geom Mugeuk intended to take on the stronger enemy himself. His perception was frighteningly sharp.

Geom Mugeuk trusted Cult Leader Pungcheon more than anyone, but even so, his heart was set on facing any enemy that was even the slightest bit more dangerous himself.

You may be the one who came to get me, but I’ll be the one who takes you out.

He would take him out without leaving so much as a scratch on his finger.

“Of course not. Please take care of the next ones too, Cult Leader!”

Geom Mugeuk knew better than anyone that only by answering this way would the next words come.

“Not a chance. You’ll take the next ones.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon stepped in front of Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk looked at his back.

The back of Cult Leader Pungcheon as he bantered while cursing him was his back, and this back, showing not the slightest fear even before those terrifying blood spirits, was his back as well. The feeling his back gave off was different from anyone else’s.

Thud! Thud! Thud!

The blood spirits approached with a fearsome momentum, shaking the floor.

Even for Cult Leader Pungcheon, dealing with them did not seem easy, so Geom Mugeuk tensed inwardly, ready to leap into the fight at any moment.

When Cult Leader Pungcheon extended his hand, a single drop of ink fell into the air.

That tiny dot expanded in an instant, and a large black hole appeared in midair.

“Danger!”

The very moment Gwisuk shouted out from instinctive alarm—

Whirrrrrrrr!

The black hole whirled like a vortex and began sucking in the blood spirits.

In an instant, the blood spirits were drawn inside. It was not a hole they could withstand. It was a hole that would unfailingly suck in whatever its target was.

The sight of those huge, terrifying blood spirits being swept in helplessly felt utterly unreal.

Once it had swallowed all the blood spirits, the black circle instantly shrank back into a dot of its original size, then vanished from the air with a pop.

It was as if nothing had ever happened here to begin with.

At that overwhelming illusion art, not only Hwannyeo and Gwisuk but even Geom Mugeuk stood there blankly in astonishment.

Then, from Cult Leader Pungcheon’s mouth, words that only the absolute sovereign of illusion arts could speak flowed out calmly.

“You opened it, but this is my world.”

Chapter 314: What Kind of Life Have You Lived?
----------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk saw a new side of the Pungcheon Cult Leader today. No, he had seen his world. The world within his Illusion Arts.

Inside the Space-Time Transference Illusion Technique last time, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had said:

“If you stay here long enough, there are times you get confused whether this is the real world or that is the real world. I suppose it is because of the greed to want to remain in the space I created?”

Only now could he understand the true meaning contained in those words.

It was not merely the desire to stay longer that he felt while lying on a sandy beach. Having become such a powerful existence in this world, how could he want to go out into the outside world?

“Cult Leader.”

“What is it?”

“Even if I have done things wrong, please forgive me.”

“Then there would be far too many things to forgive. Why? Are you afraid? Afraid of being sucked into a place like that and disappearing?”

“I do not even wish to imagine it.”

“I am the one who does not wish to imagine it. When I think of you tearing through that and emerging.”

“You overestimate me! Please, just curse me out as usual instead.”

Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader conversed while looking at Hwanyeo and Gwisuk standing before them. Even while exchanging banter, they did not let their guard down for a single moment.

Geom Mugeuk sent sincere respect and admiration as a martial artist.

“You are magnificent, Cult Leader.”

Being acknowledged by someone is always a joyful thing, but he was even happier because that someone was Geom Mugeuk.

“Thank you.”

Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader exchanged glances. The two of them, with enemies before them, felt a deeper resonance than usual.

Meanwhile, Hwanyeo and Gwisuk had not given up on fighting even in the face of the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s overwhelming Illusion Arts. They still had moves left to play.

“This time, it is my turn.”

As Geom Mugeuk stepped forward, this time Hwanyeo stepped up.

If Gwisuk was an expert of the Ghost Arts who commanded the dead, then Hwanyeo was an expert of the Soul Subjugation Technique.

“Young Cult Leader, I had intended to avoid touching you if possible.”

Because their goal had been to quietly plant their people in the Demonic Cult. But if they had known things would turn out like this, they would have rather targeted Geom Mugeuk.

“Touching a Demon Venerable is the same as touching me.”

The moment Geom Mugeuk answered coldly, dark red light poured forth from Hwanyeo’s torn eyes.

Sssshhhhaaaaa.

The light flew into Geom Mugeuk’s eyes in an instant.

The next moment, both of their movements stopped. As if time had frozen, the two stood in place with their eyes wide open.

Since carelessly interfering while the Soul Subjugation Technique was being unfolded could endanger both of them, both the Pungcheon Cult Leader and Gwisuk could only watch silently.

“If it were you, wouldn’t you have known that she would use the Soul Subjugation Technique on the Young Cult Leader?”

“I did.”

“Why did you not stop it? Are you not worried that the Young Cult Leader has fallen victim to the Soul Subjugation Technique?”

At Gwisuk’s words, the Pungcheon Cult Leader answered leisurely.

“Not just me, but even if the eight Demon Venerables of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult were here, they would all say the same thing.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked quietly at Geom Mugeuk and added.

“You do not know because you have not experienced it.”

* * *

The Soul Subjugation Technique Hwanyeo chose was not an aggressive one that would make him kill himself or make him kill the Pungcheon Cult Leader. She had instinctively sensed it—that such methods would not work.

So the Soul Subjugation Technique she chose was this very one.

Heart Demon Soul Subjugation Technique (心魔制魂術).

It was a Soul Subjugation Technique that peered into the opponent’s heart.

It was a secret art that uncovered the opponent’s weakness through the heart demon within their heart. At times, it was an ultimate secret art capable of delving into the subconscious that even the victim of the Soul Subjugation Technique did not know.

Because it was a lost Illusion Art of the Blood Cult that only she could perform in this martial world, if she died, this Illusion Art would also be lost forever.

Hwi-i-i-ing.

Hwanyeo was now standing in a snowfield swept by a blizzard.

‘This is his heart.’

In that empty place, there were only footprints left by Geom Mugeuk.

She slowly followed the footprints.

When the Heart Demon Soul Subjugation Technique was cast, the scene shown by the opponent differed for each person. Some showed the slice of life they were most proud of, while others showed secrets they had kept for a lifetime out of shame. Some showed the moment they regretted most, and others showed what was most precious in their life.

None of it mattered. She would turn the opponent’s joy, pain, and secrets into heart demons and shake them.

She would amplify the fear of suddenly losing that joy until it drove them mad. She would amplify that pain until they took their own life. She would make them unable to sleep from the terror that their secret would be revealed.

This was precisely the Heart Demon Soul Subjugation Technique.

The Heart Demon Soul Subjugation Technique could not be used often. Because each time, it consumed one’s innate true qi enormously. Peering into another person’s heart was truly a dangerous and difficult thing.

Hwanyeo was curious what she would find at the end of these footprints. Also, what kind of form Geom Mugeuk would take. What was his secret?

She walked following the footprints in the snow. How long had she walked? No matter how far she went, Geom Mugeuk’s figure did not appear.

This was the first time this had happened. Usually, something would appear after a short while.

Hwanyeo quickened her steps. The blizzard blew fiercer, making it difficult to walk.

No matter how far she walked, only the footprints continued in the snowfield. Geom Mugeuk’s figure was nowhere to be seen.

Was something wrong? No.

Standing alone in that empty place, she understood.

That this was Geom Mugeuk’s life.

That it was a lonely life of walking endlessly through the blizzard.

The emotions he had experienced were conveyed in the wind that brought the blizzard. That hardship, wretchedness, and solitude. All of it was contained in these silent footprints.

‘Just what kind of life have you lived?’

Unable to consume any more innate true qi, she ceased the Heart Demon Soul Subjugation Technique.

* * *

Hwanyeo and Geom Mugeuk regained consciousness at the same time.

She stared at Geom Mugeuk with a dazed expression for a moment.

“What are you? You are the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, aren’t you? You are young and inexperienced. But what was that?”

Both the Pungcheon Cult Leader and Gwisuk were curious about what she had seen.

“You are in a position that all the demonic people of the world envy... why are you so lonely?”

Geom Mugeuk gave no answer.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at Geom Mugeuk. The heart that Hwanyeo had peered into must have been unbearably lonely. And this was the Young Cult Leader who was always busy teasing him and exchanging banter.

While everyone else was silent, only Hwanyeo spoke.

“I know you made all the Demon Venerables your people, but why on earth are you alone?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, who had been listening, opened his mouth.

“It seems you have a black hole in your heart as well.”

It was precisely that hole the Pungcheon Cult Leader had spoken of before. That thing which inevitably formed in the hearts of those who had mastered Illusion Arts.

“Why would I not have one?”

“Your hole must be different from ours. It must be the kind of hole you want to enter holding a torch at least once.”

“You will be disappointed.”

“I’m going in to tease you, so it’s fine.”

After giving the Pungcheon Cult Leader a faint smile, Geom Mugeuk looked at Hwanyeo.

“When you were looking at me, I saw you too. That black hole in your heart—I saw it too.”

In that moment, Hwanyeo flinched in surprise. What did he mean? As she wondered, words she had never expected flowed out.

“I saw your younger sibling.”

Hwanyeo froze instantly. A being Geom Mugeuk could absolutely not know had been mentioned. So surprised, Hwanyeo looked at Gwisuk. She wondered if Gwisuk had told him. But that could not be.

‘Did he really see my heart? How?’

In truth, Geom Mugeuk had not seen her heart. He spoke as if he had seen it because he knew about her sibling. Since her sibling had been mentioned, he might now be able to obtain some clues.

“Your sibling looked exactly like you.”

Strength drained completely from her body.

‘He really saw it.’

She had never dreamed that someone would peer into her heart. And that person was Geom Mugeuk, not even the Pungcheon Cult Leader? Was the Nine Transformations Demonic Art truly such a great martial art?

“You hate your younger sibling, don’t you?”

Hwanyeo’s pupils trembled.

In her previous life before regression, Hwanyeo had done many things for the Illusion King. But the one who seized that glory was the Illusion King. Because the fact that she had a twin older sister was revealed much later.

She had not devoted herself sincerely to her younger sibling. She knew that due to unavoidable circumstances, she had become the darkness while the Illusion King became the light.

Even from her reaction now, he could clearly feel that love-hate.

“Young Cult Leader, I am bound to hate her even more because of you.”

Because this matter had become a mess. Even if she survived this place by luck, as punishment for ruining the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult affair, the important tasks would go to her younger sibling.

At that moment, Gwisuk sent a telepathic message to her.

‘Leave this place first.’

‘I cannot face them alone.’

Gwisuk judged that her presence made no difference. He had to save at least her.

‘Once you get out, ask your younger sibling for help first.’

The moment Hwanyeo tried to answer, her figure vanished in an instant.

It was not that Hwanyeo left voluntarily, but that Gwisuk had forcibly ejected her from this place.

Even though she had disappeared, Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader showed no agitation.

“Do you know the method to break this place?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s calm question, the Pungcheon Cult Leader answered.

“What grand method to break it would there be? When the guests die and the owner dies, the inn closes its doors.”

Gwisuk’s expression completely stiffened. The Pungcheon Cult Leader knew exactly how to break it. If the occupants and himself died, this place would disappear.

It was a crushing defeat he was experiencing for the first time in his life. Rather, his heart was at ease. Because if his opponent was the Pungcheon Cult Leader, his death would not be in vain.

“But I will not go alone.”

Gwisuk’s demeanor changed. Killing intent and malice surged, and his appearance transformed as well. His eyes and ears were torn long, just as Hwanyeo’s had been. Blue ghost fire began blazing around his body.

Geom Mugeuk was certain that, when considering Ghost Arts alone, it would not be easy to find an expert as powerful as Gwisuk.

Geom Mugeuk took a step back.

“Fortunately, it seems it is the Cult Leader’s turn to fight.”

“Is it not your turn?”

“Did I not just face the Soul Subjugation Technique?”

“You simply got hit one-sidedly. That is not fighting.”

“There was a tremendous fight inside my heart.”

The conversation did not continue.

Gwisuk charged at the two. As he unleashed a palm strike, Gwisuk’s palm was also burning with ghost fire.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader did not dodge and met the palm strike head-on.

Kwaang!

As their palm forces clashed, both slid backward with a thunderous roar.

Gwisuk was pushed back much farther. The Pungcheon Cult Leader blew at the ghost fire clinging to his hand. The blazing ghost fire was extinguished.

The moment he saw that, Gwisuk despaired. The fact that the never-extinguishing ghost fire had been extinguished meant he could never defeat the Pungcheon Cult Leader through ordinary combat. An obvious gap in strength that could not be overcome no matter how he struggled.

‘In the end, that is the only option.’

The ghost fire rising from Gwisuk’s body blazed even more wildly. It looked as though it would burn him up at any moment.

Blood droplets began spurting from his skin. Hundreds, thousands of blood droplets spurted out and began filling the surroundings. They were no ordinary blood droplets. They were blood droplets imbued with vengeful ghost energy, containing the final essence of Gwisuk, an Illusion Arts expert. It was also a technique of mutual destruction where he poured out all his innate true qi to die together with his enemies.

“I will definitely pierce one person’s Body-Protecting Gang Qi. Cult Leader, choose. Will you stand before the Young Cult Leader, or behind him?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader did not hesitate.

He tried to stand proudly before Geom Mugeuk.

“Because this is my world.”

However, Geom Mugeuk was not one to place the Pungcheon Cult Leader before him either.

He was already unleashing his final move.

“I am sorry, but it is my turn.”

Nine Transformations Demonic Art, Second Form: Great Annihilation.

The evil ghost that appeared before him split into four.

Kwang!

With an eardrum-shattering explosion, the blood droplets were launched. There was no space to evade the thousands of blood droplets bursting in all directions.

At the same time, the four evil ghosts swept everything away and surged forward.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

The blood droplets flew toward the evil ghosts.

Pak-pak-pak-pak-pak-pak-pak!

He had been confident they could pierce even protective astral qi, but the droplets of blood could not pierce the evil spirits. The four evil spirits became shields guarding Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and charged toward Gwisuk.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwak!

The room was stained entirely with blood, but the path swept by the Great Annihilation Form remained clean.

In the place the evil spirits had swept through, only bloody water that had once been Gwisuk flowed.

Watching that sight, the Pungcheon Cult Leader returned the words Geom Mugeuk had said to him earlier.

“If I’ve done anything wrong, do go easy on me.”

* * *

Hwanyeo was staggering through the space she had created.

It was the very place where she had suffered that nightmare before. Perhaps this moment felt even more like a nightmare.

When she reached out, a bell descended from the ceiling, which writhed as if alive.

Ding, ding, ding!

She rang the bell urgently.

It was a signal telling the other side, where her younger sibling was, to receive the contact. Just as a sound would ring from the wind chime in the flower garden when the other party contacted her, a signal should be going to the other side as well.

After ringing the bell, she went to the wall and waited.

‘Hurry! Quickly!’

Just as she was waiting for the person in charge of communications to arrive.

She sensed a presence behind her and spun around.

She was startled out of her wits.

Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader were standing before her.

The fact that her space had been breached no longer even surprised her.

She could tell. Even Gwisuk had already been defeated.

When he had forcibly sent her out, she had expected him to use a method of mutual destruction. Yet even after he had used it, the two of them were standing here perfectly fine.

Then the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s gaze turned toward the wall behind her. His expression grew cold.

“Is that the Ten-Thousand-Li Transmission Wall? To make that, thousands of lives must have been sacrificed, no?”

Truly, when it came to illusions, there was nothing the Pungcheon Cult Leader did not know.

“If you come closer, I’ll kill myself!”

She knew they could not kill her because of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

At that moment, the Pungcheon Cult Leader said coldly,

“Die.”

“!”

“A disciple can simply be found anew.”

When he came out like that, even suicide was no longer easy.

As if he already knew perfectly well how to use it, the Pungcheon Cult Leader strode forward and began thrusting his head into the Ten-Thousand-Li Transmission Wall.

After looking inside for a moment, the Pungcheon Cult Leader pulled his head back out.

Then, in an instant, he rushed at Hwanyeo, suppressed her inner force, and seized her by the throat.

“Do you want to die!”

It was the first time Geom Mugeuk had seen him so enraged.

He looked as though he might snap Hwanyeo’s neck and kill her at any moment.

Calmly, Geom Mugeuk took hold of his arm and asked in a low voice,

“What is it, Cult Leader?”

“Do you know where that side is?”

From the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s mouth came a place Geom Mugeuk had never expected.

“It’s the Pungcheon Cult.”

Chapter 315: That's What Life with a Sword Is Like.
---------------------------------------------------
The fact that the person Hwan-yeo had been communicating with was in Pungcheon Church meant only one thing.

The Hwan King was in Pungcheon Church.

The enraged Pungcheon Church Leader shouted at Hwan-yeo.

“Tell me what you’re plotting, or I’ll snap your neck.”

But threats had no effect on Hwan-yeo. Though her inner power had been suppressed, she showed no fear whatsoever. She glared at the Pungcheon Church Leader with a look that said, Kill me if you dare.

Crack.

The Pungcheon Church Leader’s grip tightened around her neck. She had not only laid hands on his disciple but had also plotted against Pungcheon Church itself. It would not have been strange for him to break her neck on the spot.

“Church Leader, you mustn’t do this, if only for the sake of Pungcheon Church and the Seophon Demon Venerable.”

At my urging, the Pungcheon Church Leader let out a long breath and slowly removed his hand from her neck.

I quickly sealed Hwan-yeo’s speech acupoint, preventing her from biting her tongue and taking her own life.

“It seems she contacted them through that wall, so someone will likely appear soon.”

If word got out that Hwan-yeo had been captured by us, they would take countermeasures. We had to delay the news of her capture for as long as possible.

“Can you control her with the Seophon Soul Technique?”

The Pungcheon Church Leader shook his head.

“A master of illusion arts at this level cannot be controlled with the Seophon Soul Technique.”

Then we had no choice.

“Let’s get out of here first.”

If they looked this way and found no one, they would wait for another message. They might assume that something urgent had come up for Hwan-yeo. We could worry about the matter after leaving.

The moment the Pungcheon Church Leader swept his hand through the air—

The three of us, the Pungcheon Church Leader, Hwan-yeo, and I, were standing in the middle of the flower garden.

Hwan-yeo’s already rigid expression grew even more deeply furrowed. Seeing us enter and leave the space she had created so easily must have filled her with rage and self-loathing.

Jangho was waiting outside the flower garden with the Demon Army.

“Are you all right?”

I nodded in response to his question.

Jangho looked relieved to see that we had returned safely. Hwan-yeo and I had disappeared without warning, followed shortly afterward by the Pungcheon Church Leader, so he must have been deeply worried.

The Pungcheon Church Leader asked me,

“What do you intend to do?”

“We have to go to Pungcheon Church and smash everything to pieces. Let’s go. I’ll help!”

As though he had been waiting to hear those words, the Pungcheon Church Leader spun around, his sleeves fluttering.

He strode out through the door, then stopped and returned to the flower garden.

“What should we do first?”

He knew that we could not rush there immediately. He had merely been showing how impatient he was.

“First, we need to remove the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Technique cast on the Seophon Demon Venerable. We also need to meet Military Advisor Go and devise a plan for returning to Pungcheon Church—whether we infiltrate or return openly.”

As we had already experienced, these were enemies we had to approach with caution.

“As you saw, they are no ordinary opponents. This woman alone spent years posing as the owner of a flower garden and hiding there in order to cast the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Technique on the Seophon Demon Venerable. The enemy of the Martial Alliance had infiltrated it for ten years. Pungcheon Church is probably the same. If we act rashly, they’ll vanish beneath the surface and become a source of trouble for years to come.”

The Pungcheon Church Leader nodded. He was an old ginger who was no less shrewd than anyone else, so how could he not understand what I was saying?

“The matter concerning Pungcheon Church is my matter as well.”

At my words, the Pungcheon Church Leader calmed his excitement.

“Let’s take care of what needs to be done first.”

I felt that my relationship with the Pungcheon Church Leader had grown closer through this battle. I was beginning to see things I had never seen before and feel emotions I had never felt before.

I released Hwan-yeo’s sealed speech acupoint.

I precisely pressed the acupoints along her jaw so that she could not bite her tongue. She could speak, but she could not muster enough strength to bite down.

“Do you want to save your younger sister?”

A mocking smile appeared at the corners of Hwan-yeo’s lips.

“I know you have no intention of saving either me or my sister. So don’t waste your time. Just kill me.”

“If you somehow manage to stay alive, your backer might save you.”

Was she afraid of the person behind her? Or had her pride been wounded?

Hwan-yeo denied it.

“I planned everything alone. I take orders from no one.”

“If you have no backer, then what you’re doing makes no sense.”

“What do you mean?”

“You inherited the Blood Cult’s illusion arts, yet you went out of your way to get the Seophon Demon Venerable under your control? You even spent years pretending to be the owner of a flower garden for that purpose? Why? Did you want to become a Demon Venerable? Even if you did, you couldn’t become one that way.
