Or was it for money? Were you after my father? Why? If I assume you planned all of this alone, I simply can’t understand it.”

Hwan-yeo listened to me in silence.

“But if I assume that someone ordered you two to do it, everything becomes clear. They used you, masters of illusion arts, to bring even the Seophon Demon Venerable under their control. It makes sense, doesn’t it? Who else but you could have targeted the Seophon Demon Venerable? And they used your younger sister, who is also a master of illusion arts, to infiltrate Pungcheon Church. It all makes perfect sense, doesn’t it?”

She was unable to refute a single word.

The Pungcheon Church Leader, who had been listening, slipped into the conversation.

“When you act like this, you really are smarter than Goweol.”

“I know what I know because Military Advisor Go knows it too.”

“No, no. There’s something different about you.”

Then Hwan-yeo abruptly said,

“You’re a monster.”

She had looked into my heart before my regression. Perhaps those emotions from that time were still hidden somewhere inside me. Yes. Back then, I had been a monster.

“How can people capture monsters like you? A monster has to be caught by another monster.”

Would she cooperate in capturing the Hwan King? No. No matter how much she envied her sister or competed with her, she could not hate her enough to want her dead. If she had, she would not have lived the life she did before my regression. The two of them would have fought until one of them died.

Even if Hwan-yeo would not help us, I had to find some clue that might be of use. After all, dealing with the Hwan King would certainly be more difficult than dealing with Hwan-yeo.

That was when something caught my eye. Several newly arrived flowerpots had been left in a corner, not yet put away.

I immediately called for Jangho.

“There is someone you must capture.”

* * *

After returning to the church, the Pungcheon Church Leader went to see Goweol first.

When he learned that something had gone wrong in Pungcheon Church, Goweol was the person he had wanted to see most.

Even though the dependable Geom Mugeuk had been beside him, Goweol had come to mind first.

Yes, perhaps it was because he wanted to hear these words.

“They don’t know what Pungcheon Church means to you, Church Leader. Make sure they learn properly this time.”

And then came a moving addition.

“It is a precious place to me as well. Church Leader, don’t forgive them.”

The Pungcheon Church Leader was moved despite himself. He was grateful and sorry that Goweol called the place that would remind him of his shackles precious.

As he sank into thought with mixed feelings, Goweol poured cold water over his boiling emotions.

“Church Leader. Follow me and take deep breaths. Breathe.”

“What are you trying to do?”

“Just follow me.”

Goweol took exaggeratedly deep breaths, loud enough to be heard, and the Pungcheon Church Leader followed reluctantly.

“Do you think I’m a child? Why do I have to calm myself with deep breaths?”

“You are a child, aren’t you? When it comes to Pungcheon Church, your eyes turn upside down. You act like a child.”

“I do not.”

“All children say they don’t. They do it anyway, which is why they’re children.”

The Pungcheon Church Leader repeatedly took deep breaths along with Goweol. It was not the breathing that calmed him, but Goweol’s consideration in going this far for him.

“I’d be afraid someone might see us.”

“They’ll probably think you’re practicing some peerless martial art from beyond the borders.”

“Do you know how brilliantly I performed? You should have seen me!”

“I know even without seeing it. I know how well you must have done, Church Leader.”

“You didn’t even see it.”

“In a world where everyone else may fail to recognize you, I do. I know how magnificent you must have looked inside that illusion. So leave this matter to the Deputy Church Leader and support him from the side.”

The Pungcheon Church Leader suddenly shouted,

“It’s my matter.”

“That’s why I’m saying this. You won’t be able to look at it objectively. You already see the world however it suits you.”

“Then come with me and help me. If I act foolishly, nag me from the side. I have to go greet the fiends carved into the Thunder Demon Bell.”

Goweol shook his head.

“If the enemies use illusion arts and soul-seizing techniques, then people capable of opposing them should go. I’ll only get in the way. Give my regards to the fiends in the Thunder Demon Bell.”

“I refuse! One madman who talks to a bell is enough!”

Goweol gazed out the window into the distance, toward his homeland, and said,

“You’re getting excited again. Breathe!”

* * *

At the same time that the Pungcheon Church Leader was meeting Goweol, I was meeting with the Seophon Demon Venerable.

I told her that I had been afflicted by the Yin-Yang Soul Reversal Technique when I killed Sawujong.

“I didn’t know about it at the time either. I learned of it while pursuing the person behind it.”

I chose my words carefully so as not to offend her. No matter how young she was, Cheongseon held the status of a Demon Venerable.

I was especially careful not to wound her pride. The fact that the Seophon Demon Venerable had fallen victim to a soul-seizing technique could be a severe blow to her.

“It wouldn’t have worked with any other martial art, but since it was the Blood Cult’s demonic art, there was nothing you could have done.”

Fortunately, she accepted it sensibly.

The Seophon Demon Venerable was relieved and delighted.

I took her to the Pungcheon Church Leader, who was waiting in another room.

Inside were the Pungcheon Church Leader, Goweol, and Hwan-yeo, who had been put to sleep after her blood-sealing and sleep acupoints were subdued.

The Seophon Demon Venerable performed a deep bow to express her gratitude.

“I will never forget this kindness, Master.”

I gave substance to her vague feelings of gratitude.

“Do you know what the Church Leader said when he first learned of this?”

I imitated the Pungcheon Church Leader.

The Seophon Demon Venerable gazed at the Pungcheon Church Leader with a deeply moved expression. The Pungcheon Church Leader’s shoulders rose toward the heavens once more.

His eyes told me, More. Keep going!

The Pungcheon Church Leader waved his hands and said,

“I said it was nothing.”

Though he waved his hands as if telling me to stop, his fingertips told me, Just a little more!

“When the Church Leader snaps his fingers, the world changes! This time, I saw that world in all its glory.”

The Seophon Demon Venerable looked at him with sincere reverence. I was telling it half in jest, but to the Seophon Demon Venerable, it must have been the future she dreamed of.

She still had a long way to go. She was prickly and selfish at times. But none of us knew what she would be like ten years from now. All we could do was hope that she would grow into someone as magnificent as the other Demon Venerables.

“At the very least, I believe that no one in the martial world is luckier in their master than you. Take good care of that fortune.”

At my sincere advice, the Seophon Demon Venerable lowered her head in gratitude. Beyond her, the Pungcheon Church Leader wore a satisfied expression. Beyond him, Goweol was smiling again.

“Now then, let’s perform the ritual right away.”

Since no preparations were needed besides the people involved, we decided to conduct the ritual here.

The Pungcheon Church Leader woke Hwan-yeo. When she saw that the Seophon Demon Venerable had arrived, she seemed to sense her own fate.

The Pungcheon Church Leader seated the two women side by side.

An unusual, unidentified pattern had been carved into Hwan-yeo’s back. This was the Exorcising Spirit Seal the Pungcheon Church Leader had spoken of.

He said that if it were extracted, the Red Soul sleeping inside the Seophon Demon Venerable’s body would be released.

He had called it a difficult and dangerous ritual, but I was not worried. The ritual would be performed by the Pungcheon Church Leader, not anyone else.

He would handle it properly. Yes, he would handle it properly.

Trust was not born from words but from actions. Today, he had clearly shown what kind of person he was.

Before the ritual began, Hwan-yeo said,

“I would like to speak with the Deputy Church Leader for a moment.”

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader looked at me and asked what I intended to do.

I nodded, and everyone else withdrew, leaving the two of us alone.

“I didn’t know this morning. I never thought the end would come so suddenly.”

“That’s how life is when you carry a sword.”

“I have a favor to ask.”

She made an unexpected request.

“If you see my younger brother, tell him this. Tell him I didn’t betray him.”

By the time I delivered those words, he would probably be fated to die as well. I didn’t know whether this request would have any meaning.

“Do you believe I’ll keep my promise?”

“I do.”

“Why?”

“Because I saw into your heart. Anyone who has walked such a desperate path would keep their promises.”

“Weren’t you the one who called me a monster?”

Hwan-yeo stared at me with a complicated expression. I could feel both the rage that made her want to kill me and the desperation that made her want to beg me to spare her. But having looked into my heart, she would know. Nothing would move me.

Her final regret was this:

“…I should have lived my own life.”

She must have meant a life of her own, entangled neither with her younger brother nor with the power behind the scenes.

She said nothing more and closed her eyes.

“No matter how great they are, could they really be as great as us?”

Chapter 316: Because I Miss That Sandstorm Too.
-----------------------------------------------
The great ritual was in its final stages.

Purple energy extended from the left fingertips of the Pungcheon Cult Lord.

The purple energy moved as if alive and began to extract the Spirit-Subduing Seal engraved on Hwanyeo's back. Though it was a seal engraved like a tattoo, it was pulled from her body as if a nail were being drawn out.

Slowly, very slowly.

As if unwilling to tolerate even a moment's mistake, the Pungcheon Cult Lord focused his entire mind.

The moment the seal was completely extracted, the Pungcheon Cult Lord thrust his right hand over the Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate's head and let out a spirited shout.

Then red energy was pulled from her crown with a soft sound, as if a tree were being uprooted. It was the Red Soul embedded in her head.

The Spirit-Subduing Seal and Red Soul floating at the ends of both hands burst into flames and vanished.

As if on cue, both women collapsed with a thud.

“Hoooo.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord caught his breath and slowly withdrew his outstretched hand. The purple energy that had flared around his hand disappeared completely.

The great ritual had concluded safely.

Shortly after, the Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate, who had been collapsed, raised herself up.

The welcome news awaiting her.

“The Red Soul in your body has disappeared.”

“Ah! Thank you, Master.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord's body was drenched in sweat. For an expert of his caliber to be drenched in sweat, one could imagine how severe the mental exertion had been.

“You have saved my life.”

“Though I performed the great ritual, it was the Young Cult Leader who uncovered this scheme. Do not forget that.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord gave the credit to Geom Mugeuk. For the Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate, it was a good thing if the two were close, so she gladly vowed her loyalty.

“I will devote my loyalty to the Young Cult Leader, Master.”

The Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate's gaze turned toward Hwanyeo. Furious, she raised her hand high. She felt she would only be satisfied if she smashed that skull.

“There is no need. She is already dead.”

The moment the Spirit-Subduing Seal was forcibly extracted, Hwanyeo had perished.

“Cheongseon.”

“Yes, Master.”

“As you live on, you will peer into the hearts of countless people. You will manipulate countless people to your will. Each time, there is something you must look at.”

“What is that?”

“The hole in your heart. The higher your realm in the Soul-Devouring Art rises, the larger that hole will become.”

The Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate could faintly understand what her master was worried about.

“Then what should I do?”

Do not despise people. Do not despise the world. The Pungcheon Cult Lord, pondering what words to give, left the solution to his disciple.

“You must find how to fill that hole yourself. Do not forget that the hole exists, and look into it often. Keep looking, and you will find your own method.”

The Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate performed a deep bow right there. She was grateful for a master who cared for her so deeply.

“Should this foolish disciple ever fall into that hole, please pull me out, Master.”

Then the Pungcheon Cult Lord smiled and said.

“That is a request I should be making. Having raised you to this age, how large must my hole be?”

Deeply moved by the Pungcheon Cult Lord's open-hearted words, the Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate felt thrilled inside. Though there had been a vague distance between master and disciple, she felt they had grown closer through this matter.

“Go out and tell the Young Cult Leader to come in.”

* * *

I talked with Jangho at length for the first time in a while.

The demonic troops seemed surprised to see me conversing with him for so long.

How fun could talking with the taciturn Jangho be? I was putting on a show for the demonic troops. That I trust and cherish Jangho this much.

If he weren't someone I truly liked, I couldn't have conversed like this for the long duration of the ritual. It was my effort for him.

Thanks to that, I even heard that Jangho's childhood dream was to become a painter.

“When I retire later, I intend to try painting.”

Hearing that, I briefly left my seat.

“Wait a moment.”

Disappearing in a flash with rapid footwork, I returned and held out to him the tools painters use—paper, brushes, pigments, and so on. I had gone to Magachon and bought the finest ones.

“Don't put it off. Start from today.”

Jangho was flustered, having never expected me to go buy these. The demonic troops standing behind also wore surprised expressions.

“Try painting from tonight. Even just a rough sketch before bed. If you have nothing to paint, try painting those bulky subordinates, or that magnificent scar on your face.”

I know. What is needed for this is not time. Because when time becomes available, other tasks inevitably arise alongside it.

What is needed is not time, but an occasion. Such as painting tools forcibly given by the Young Cult Leader, for example.

Jangho bowed his head and respectfully received them.

“I will try painting from today.”

“I wonder if I should have bought a brush made of ten-thousand-year cold iron. It seems brushes might break in those rough hands of yours?”

At my jest, Jangho laughed pleasantly. He was the third person I wanted to see smile by any means possible. Father, Gwonma, and Jangho. I would keep making these taciturn men, who had lived their whole lives without knowing laughter, laugh.

Just then, the Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate walked out of the quarters.

From her expression alone, I could tell the result of the great ritual.

* * *

Only I, the Pungcheon Cult Lord, and Gowol remained in the quarters.

The Soul-Devouring Demon Venerate had returned, and Hwanyeo's corpse had been entrusted to the demonic troops for burial. Perhaps because she had left her final greeting to me, her face looked peaceful.

Now was the time to plan the next step: going to the Pungcheon Cult.

“Strategist Go, what would be best?”

While the ritual proceeded, Gowol had already been thinking about this.

“I recommend going to the Pungcheon Cult officially.”

“As the return of the former cult lord?”

“No. As an official visit from the Young Cult Leader of the Cheonma Divine Cult.”

He gauged the Pungcheon Cult Lord's reaction. The Cult Lord would want to return as the protagonist. Gowol had already thought that far ahead.

“If you return together with the Young Cult Leader, the Cult Lord's prestige will rise even higher. Because it will mean you were the one who made this visit happen.”

At those words, the Pungcheon Cult Lord's expression brightened.

“They will have infiltrated the leadership of the Pungcheon Cult. Therefore, you must go officially to move about freely. Now that you have become the Young Cult Leader, you also have the justification for a visit.”

It was a valid point. Sneaking in to infiltrate the Pungcheon Cult was no easy task.

“They will soon find out that Hwanyeo has disappeared, won't they?”

“Ignore it.”

“Ignore it?”

“Rather, if you move as if you know nothing, they too will be unsure. ‘They noticed our existence, yet they are making an official visit?’ Such doubts will arise.”

“What if they sense danger and disappear?”

“If the woman of the Flower Garden invested effort over several years, the same will be true for them. Would they give up so easily?”

It was a valid point. Hwanwang was someone whose pride reached the heavens. A figure whose ability matched his pride.

Gowol spoke to the Pungcheon Cult Lord.

“He was there since the time when the Cult Lord and I were there. We left without knowing.”

“!”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord's expression hardened, for he had not thought that far.

“He lay low watching for an opportunity, and moved in earnest when the Cult Lord left.”

I picked up where he left off.

“On this side, they moved when young Cheongseon became a Demon Venerate.”

“That is correct.”

“Very well, I shall follow your counsel. We will visit officially.”

I followed Gowol's intention without a second word. Was not the best way to empower a strategist to trust and follow him?

“I will squeeze whatever more I can from the person under interrogation and support you from as close as possible. Oh, and it is customary for a Demon Venerate to accompany the Young Cult Leader on an official visit. Given who the opponent is, you must carefully choose which Demon Venerate accompanies you this time.”

“Do you have someone in mind?”

“It must be a Demon Venerate strong in illusion arts.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord well knew who that was.

“It's Mabul. Mabul is strong in illusion arts.”

Traveling with Mabul? I did not mind, but the Pungcheon Cult Lord was the problem.

Originally, the Pungcheon Cult Lord had been closest to Mabul among the Eight Demon Venerates. In the past, it was Mabul who had brought the Pungcheon Cult Lord to the Cheonma Divine Cult.

But afterward their relationship soured, and I knew they were now in an awkward relationship.

Wondering what he was thinking, the Pungcheon Cult Lord stepped forward.

“I will go speak to Mabul myself.”

Yes, it was right to resolve it oneself.

“Then I shall first go receive Father's permission.”

Since it was an official visit, I had to report to and receive permission from Father.

We rose from our seats for our respective tasks.

* * *

Mabul was in the Great Dharma Hall.

A golden Buddha statue with a terrifying form looked down at him with cold eyes. In that place devoid of mercy, Mabul was chanting sutras alone.

The Pungcheon Cult Lord entered and sat behind him. When Mabul finished his chanting, he asked without turning around.

“What brings you here?”

It was awkward. In the past, regardless of their inner thoughts, they had faced each other with hearty laughter.

“Have you been well?”

“Could I have been well?”

Mabul was famous for supporting the Great Successor, so he could not have been well.

Mabul turned around and looked at the Pungcheon Cult Lord.

“As you can see, I am a dog that chased chickens.”

“That dog is in good spirits.”

It was not because the golden light emanating from Mabul's body shone unusually. Truly, Mabul's expression looked at ease, unlike before.

“What is the secret?”

“Would you not know?”

Mabul was asking if he did not know. How comfortable the heart becomes when one lets everything go. Come to think of it, both of them were people who had let go of their ambition for power.

An awkward silence passed for a moment. Then the Pungcheon Cult Lord abruptly asked.

“Don't I look like I've lost weight?”

Mabul made an incredulous expression.

“Did you come to boast about that?”

“I did. Just as your face has become at ease, I came to tell you that I too have become at ease.”

Mabul had already been feeling it. That the Pungcheon Cult Lord had changed greatly. He was not the man of old. Though he had smiled outwardly, he had been full of ulterior motives; now he somehow looked at ease.

“I have come with a request.”

“Speak.”

“Nasty fellows are scheming in the Pungcheon Cult. The Young Cult Leader will use an official visit as pretext to go with me. Come with us and lend your aid.”

“Why me?”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord conveyed his honest feelings.

“A Demon Venerate must accompany us, and among the Demon Venerates, you are the most comfortable to me.”

Mabul, who had been staring at the Pungcheon Cult Lord, refused in one word.

“I refuse.”

Rejected outright, the Pungcheon Cult Lord had nothing to say.

“Then it cannot be helped. Farewell.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord rose from his seat. As he tried to leave just like that, Mabul spoke. He had expected a barb.

“I am sorry for the rude words I said to you in the past.”

When the two had fought and their relationship soured, the Pungcheon Cult Lord had mocked him by treating him like golden shit.

“Why apologize suddenly? I will not follow you just because of that.”

“That is not why I apologize.”

“Then why?”

“There is something I learned while staying with the Young Cult Leader and Gowol. That the most important thing between people is apologizing. That one apology is more important than a hundred kind words. I only recently realized this. Having lived a life never apologizing, my apology comes late. I am sorry.”

Mabul was bewildered. Because the rude words back then had not come only from the Pungcheon Cult Lord.

Having said that, as he turned to leave, Mabul called out to him.

“Since you're here, have a drink of defeat wine at least before you go.”

“Is there alcohol in the dharma hall?”

“As if there is only alcohol. There are demons too, alcohol too, and if you rummage around somewhere over there, there will be corpses too.”

Mabul truly brought two bottles and handed one over.

The two men drank straight from the bottles.

The Pungcheon Cult Lord sat down so as not to look down on him.

Mabul gazed for a long time at the mural of demonic monks slaughtering, then revealed his inner thoughts.

“In the days when I strove to make the Great Successor the Young Cult Leader, that was all I saw. When I looked at a person, I thought only of whether they would help the succession struggle or not. Whatever happened, I thought only of whether it would be helpful or not. I was mad for it.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord listened in silence.

“I thought everything would be solved if the Great Successor became the Young Cult Leader. That even if I acted rudely or regretfully, I could apologize later and be understood. That's what I thought. Now I realize that if the Great Successor had become the successor, I would not have apologized to anyone.”

And now that Geom Muyang had not become the successor, he too apologized to the Pungcheon Cult Lord.

“I too was sorry then.”

The Pungcheon Cult Lord silently held out his bottle, and Mabul lightly clinked it before drinking.

“Let us call this not wine of defeat, but wine of victory. Then let us meet again.”

As the Pungcheon Cult Lord tried to go outside, Mabul asked from behind.

“When do we depart?”

Surprised, the Pungcheon Cult Lord turned around, and Mabul added as if to himself.

“I miss that sandstorm too.”

* * *

Father was in the training ground.

Amidst the heavy settled demonic energy, Father stood alone with the Cheonma Sword drawn.

The mere sight of his back holding the sword inspired fear that dared not be challenged.

Without turning around, Father asked.

“How far have you trained the Nine Transformations Demon Art?”

“I have mastered up to the second form.”

“Then try blocking this.”

Father quickly turned toward me.

The moment he pointed his sword at me, an evil ghost appeared before Father.

An evil ghost with a completely different appearance from mine. It was chilling just to look at; it was the evil ghost summoned by Father.

I could tell instinctively. That Father was about to unleash the Great Annihilation Technique.

Only the Nine Transformations Demon Art can block the Nine Transformations Demon Art!

An evil ghost appeared before me as well. In terms of appearance alone, mine looked scarier. Mine was fearsome; Father's was cold and ruthless.

Though they were evil ghosts born from the same martial art, they were merciless. Their eyes glared at each other with an intent to annihilate, terrifying to behold, and then.

Ssssssssk!

The two evil ghosts facing each other began to multiply.

I stopped at four, but Father's evil ghosts kept increasing.

The multiplied evil ghosts lined up before Father. The number created by Father, who had achieved great completion of the Nine Transformations Demon Art, was forty-four in total; the spectacle was truly blood-curdling.

Four against forty-four.

But he was not a father who would go easy on me.

BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM-BOOM!

Could I really block this?

Toward the evil ghosts surging forward to sweep everything away, my four evil ghosts charged forth powerfully.

Chapter 317: I Want to Fight More.
----------------------------------
It was forty-four against four, but in truth, it was a fight of four against four.

The evil spirits lined up in a row only pushed straight ahead. If these evil spirits had been a technique that turned and concentrated on us, Father would never have split them into so many in the first place. How could one possibly stop forty-four evil spirits?

That was what I had thought.

But the very instant the four evil spirits clashed with the evil spirits coming toward them—

I was startled.

The other evil spirits that had been rushing forward had stopped in place. Naturally, I had assumed only the four evil spirits would collide, and the rest would sweep past us on either side.

So he can stop them too!

Because my realm was still low, I had not known it could be used like this.

Then, surely not? They weren’t going to charge at us, were they?

Fortunately, the evil spirits remained where they stood.

The pressure was tremendous. Four evil spirits were fighting each other, and on either side of them, forty evil spirits stood menacingly.

Bang! Ba-ba-bang! Boom! Boom-boom!

The tremendous collisions continued.

They were evil spirits that swept away everything, yet they could not push each other back. They were like martial artists fighting on the very front line of a battlefield, with the resolve that if they were pushed back, everyone behind them would die. Every time swords and bodies collided, thunderous roars burst out and sparks flew.

Beyond the evil spirits, I could see Father. Though he had split them into forty-four and must have been consuming an enormous amount of inner force, Father was calm.

He was showing me now. This was what the Great Annihilation Form looked like when it had reached Great Completion.

And he was asking me. What will the Great Annihilation Form that you will one day complete be like?

The four evil spirits began to be pushed back. I poured my inner force into them to the utmost limit, but they could not withstand Father’s evil spirits, which had reached Great Completion. Just as no amount of firewood could overcome the heat of lava, I was not yet his match.

Did they know that if they collapsed, I would die? The four evil spirits held out to the very end before vanishing.

The choice I had to make now was to escape this area with Flash Step. I had to get far away, completely beyond the range of the Great Annihilation Form.

But I did not want to.

At that moment, my eyes met Father’s.

I will not run.

I drew up the Heavenly Demon Protective Art and raised my protective astral qi. On top of that, I invoked the fifth fist of the Thunderbolt Asura Fist, Vajra Asura, which temporarily made the body as hard as steel to protect it.

Come on, then! Let’s see if you can kill me even like this.

Kwa-boom!

The charging evil spirit struck me head-on. The impact traveled through my entire body, but I endured it. If I could withstand this first collision, then I could endure.

I blocked the sword the evil spirit swung down with the Black Demon Sword.

Its terrifying power made it feel as though my arm would be torn off, but I gritted my teeth and swung my sword.

Kaaang! Kaaang! Kang! Kaaang!

Sharp metallic sounds rang out one after another. The evil spirit’s attacks were fast, precise, and powerful. It was as if Father himself were striking down at me right before my eyes.

A continuous exchange of blows. It was a battle I could never have experienced if my opponent had not been Father. Father drove me to my limits, but I instead struggled to defeat the evil spirit.

Simply enduring has no meaning. I will destroy it somehow.

My attack, swift as lightning, struck the evil spirit. It felt as if I were hammering ten-thousand-year cold iron. The arm doing the hammering hurt more.

Kaaang! Kang! Kaaang! Kang!

At the final moment of the exchange, the Black Demon Sword pierced the evil spirit’s heart with perfect accuracy.

Puuuk!

Until now, it had felt as though I were striking metal, but this time, I heard the sound of flesh tearing.

Ssssss.

The next instant, the evil spirit began to vanish. As the evil spirit that had fought me disappeared, the other evil spirits vanished along with it.

The strength left my legs, and I sank to the ground.

I could tell. At the final moment, Father had withdrawn the Great Annihilation Form. That was why I had been able to pierce its heart.

So this is Father’s Nineflame Demonic Art.

Father slowly walked toward me.

I was breathing raggedly. Even though my entire body hurt as if I were dying, even though I had felt death draw right up to my nose, I looked up at Father and said,

“I want to fight more.”

Father smiled. It was not the sneer he always wore, nor was it the laugh that escaped when he could not hold back at my joking.

For the first time, I felt that he was smiling as one martial artist to another.

“Then you should stand up.”

With a groan, I rose from where I sat. I could feel Father’s heat. Seeing me refuse to dodge and block the Great Annihilation Form with my whole body had set Father aflame as well. He likely had not expected me to withstand it to that extent.

“Show me the third form of the Nineflame Demonic Art.”

Father meant that he would look over my third form. This was a fortuitous encounter only the Young Cult Leader could obtain, and a reward for the fighting spirit I had shown in wanting to continue.

I performed the third form of the Nineflame Demonic Art for the first time.

The third form of the Nineflame Demonic Art, Great Demon Wall.

The third form was not a technique in which evil spirits appeared. It did not use the sword either.

If the first form, the Annihilation Form, was a technique meant for a single person, then the second form, the Great Annihilation Form, was a technique meant for mass slaughter.

And the third form, Great Demon Wall, was a defensive technique that erected a wall of astral qi in front of oneself. It was fundamentally different from a sword wall formed with sword qi.

Once it was the third form, it was certainly difficult.

Even when I circulated my true qi according to the mnemonic I had learned, the Great Demon Wall did not form properly.

“Please give me a moment to replenish my true qi.”

Wondering if perhaps I lacked inner force, I sat cross-legged and circulated my true qi through one heavenly cycle.

But inner force was not the issue. The Great Demon Wall still did not rise easily.

“Why is it this difficult?”

“Because defense is harder than offense.”

Yes, that was true. One always feels that attacking is difficult, but in truth, defending is harder. The higher one’s realm becomes, the more keenly one realizes it.

I asked Father about the parts I did not understand, and Father explained them to me in detail. I gained in an instant an understanding that would have taken me months if I had trained alone.

I performed it again, then asked about the parts where I was blocked again. After repeating that process—

“It worked!”

Whoooong!

With a deep and majestic sound of wind, the Great Demon Wall finally rose.

It was a wall of astral qi flowing with a sky-blue hue, like the color of my sword aura.

Standing tall like a castle, it was a wall large enough to hide at least several dozen people behind it. The more I trained, the sturdier the wall would become.

I wanted to keep looking at it, but it consumed an enormous amount of inner force, so I could not maintain it for long.

Suddenly, I became curious about Father’s Great Demon Wall.

“I want to see Father’s Great Demon Wall.”

It would probably be a wall ten times larger than this, wouldn’t it?

For today, Father was in a good mood. Very well, I’ll show you, he seemed to say as he performed the third form.

Whoosh!

A more concise and lively sound of wind.

When I saw the Great Demon Wall that appeared before Father, I was startled.

It was a Great Demon Wall completely different from what I had imagined.

Father’s Great Demon Wall was small. It was truly the size of a shield that could hide a single adult body.

Seeing it, with white energy flowing over it like mist, I could tell.

That astral qi, concentrated and concentrated again, could not be broken by any attack. It was harder than diamond, harder than ten-thousand-year cold iron.

“The higher your realm rises, the smaller the Demon Wall will become.”

“And instead, it will become sturdier?”

Father nodded and said firmly,

“A Great Demon Wall that has reached Great Completion cannot be pierced by any attack.”

At those words, a question naturally came to mind.

“Not even by the Great Annihilation Form?”

“If both have reached the same Great Completion, then it will be decided by whose martial talent is superior. How pure their inner force is will matter as well.”

My heart pounded. The Nineflame Demonic Art always made my heart race like this.

“I want to see my Great Demon Wall after it has reached Great Completion. And with it, I want to block Father’s Great Annihilation Form.”

In Father’s gaze as he looked at me, I felt a heat even hotter than before.

He must have been imagining himself sweeping away my Great Demon Wall. Leaving aside the bond between father and son, we were martial artists who sought to reach the pinnacle of martial arts.

“I will repay the grace you showed me today by not being pierced on that day. I will become an opponent Father must fight with all his strength.”

It seemed that was the answer Father wanted. For the first time, he said such words.

“I will wait.”

I will come to you, Father. And Father, that will not be our final destination. You must go farther with me.

I walked out of the training ground with Father. I could feel that Father was in a good mood, and that put me in a good mood as well.

“Have you unraveled the secret of the secret coffer?”

“Not yet.”

I took the secret coffer from my robes.

“Pay your respects to Father.”

Seeing the eyes and mouth drawn on it, Father openly sneered. Yes, if you’ve met Father, you should see this smile before you go.

“What attempts have you made?”

“I haven’t tried anything. That must be why this fellow’s mind is at ease enough to be smiling like this, don’t you think?”

I put the secret coffer back into my robes. I had a vague feeling. That my fate with the secret coffer would simply come naturally. Until then, we would remain friends like this.

“And the Great Soul-Capturing Art ended well?”

As expected, Father knew all that was going on. The Heaven-Piercing Pavilion itself was an exceptionally capable organization, and Gowol also spared no effort in providing information to the Heaven-Piercing Pavilion.

“Cult Leader Pungcheon dispelled the soul-capturing art placed on Demon Venerable Seop. We dealt with the mastermind as well.”

Father’s aura turned cold. Since they had dared lay hands on a Demon Venerable of our cult, it was only natural that he would be displeased.

“It seems they are in league with the ones who schemed at the Martial Alliance before. We also discovered that they have infiltrated the Pungcheon Cult. I think I will have to go beyond the frontier with Cult Leader Pungcheon.”

Father nodded and said,

“Take the Demon Buddha with you.”

As expected, he left no gaps. Father, too, knew precisely that the Demon Buddha was strong against illusion arts.

“And when you return, let the three of us have a meal with your older brother.”

He was not simply telling me to eat. He meant that no matter how busy I was, I should look after my brother too.

“I’ll buy the meal!”

* * *

The next morning, we gathered in one place.

The group going to the Pungcheon Cult was decided: my guards, Cult Leader Pungcheon, and the Demon Buddha.

In any case, Father had told me to take the Demon Buddha, so even if he had said he would not go, he would have had to. But before that, it seemed his conversation with Cult Leader Pungcheon had gone well.

“With the Demon Buddha accompanying us, I truly feel reassured.”

“There must be many Demon Venerables you wanted to go with besides me.”

There was no point in denying it for no reason, since he would not believe me anyway.

“There were, of course. But our Cult Leader strongly insisted that he wanted to go with the Demon Buddha.”

As I said that, I looked at Cult Leader Pungcheon beside me. Cult Leader Pungcheon pretended not to hear and looked elsewhere, but I could sense his ears pricking up.

“He said the Demon Buddha would protect us better than the other Demon Venerables.”

The two of them were reserved and did not speak to each other, but they did not seem uncomfortable either.

Cult Leader Pungcheon and the Demon Buddha, huh.

It was a combination that both suited and did not suit each other.

At that moment, Jeogyeon came and reported.

“We are ready to depart.”

Just as we were about to board the prepared carriage, someone came to see us. Unexpectedly, it was the Poison King.

“Poison King! You came all this way because I’m going far away.”

At my delighted greeting, the Poison King passed by as if he had not seen me. In his eyes, there was only one person right now.

After giving Cult Leader Pungcheon only a perfunctory greeting, the Poison King went to the Demon Buddha. Surprisingly, he had come looking for the Demon Buddha.

“I heard you were leaving beyond the frontier.”

“I’m accompanying the Young Cult Leader to the Pungcheon Cult.”

“So I have a request.”

The Poison King took several sheets of paper from his robes.

“I only found out you were going last night, so I stayed up all night making these in a hurry.”

Indeed, both his eyes were bloodshot, and his hair was disheveled.

On the papers he held out, various kinds of poisonous herbs were drawn. Their appearance, of course, but also where they mainly grew and what characteristics they had were written in detail.

“These are poisonous herbs that grow beyond the frontier. If you happen to see any while passing by, I ask that you collect them.”

At that request, the Demon Buddha burst into hearty laughter. Truly, the Poison King was probably the only person who could treat him like this. In any case, it was the first time I had seen the Demon Buddha laugh so boldly.

“Very well. If I see them, I will be sure to dig them up for you.”

The Poison King’s expression brightened radiantly.

“Ah, this is a bit shameless of me, but…”

The Poison King took several more sheets of paper from his robes.

“I left these out because I thought I was giving you too many. But since you’re going anyway, perhaps these too. Ah, is it too much? I’ll pick just a few from these. This one is absolutely necessary…”

The Poison King agonized, choosing this one and then that one.

At that moment, the Demon Buddha took all the papers the Poison King was holding.

“Give them all to me.”

“You will?”

“Wouldn’t that give me a better chance of digging up even one more?”

I could feel it. Right now, the Poison King was genuinely happy. When it came to poisonous herbs, he was a sincere man.

“No, why aren’t you asking me?”

“The Demon Buddha is someone who sees what you cannot. Go and don’t get in the way.”

If he came at me like this, I couldn’t just endure it. I shouted at the Demon Buddha.

“You’re being used! Once he has no more poisonous herbs he needs, he’ll abandon you just like he abandoned me!”

While flipping through the papers with the poisonous herbs written on them, the Demon Buddha said to me,

“Will the day ever come when the Poison King has no need of poisonous herbs?”

At those words, I saw the Poison King make a peculiar smile. The Poison King at this moment was the Poison King who had come out from his own world into reality. I like the Poison King who stands around this boundary line.

“I will return.”

Instead of telling him to be careful, the Poison King said,

“Be sure to dig them up and come back.”

The Poison King turned to leave, and our eyes met.

“Aren’t you going to say goodbye to me?”

“You’ll obviously return safely.”

Just how much he trusted me was contained in that word, obviously.

“Since you’re out already, why don’t you come with us?”

“No. I detest sand and dust.”

“You just gave him a mountain of papers telling him to dig in that very sandstorm.”

Without looking back, the Poison King scurried away.

Watching him go, the Demon Buddha smiled faintly, folded the drawings of the poisonous herbs, and carefully tucked them into his robes.

“When did you become so close with the Poison King?”

It occurred to me that Cult Leader Pungcheon might perhaps be just a little jealous. After all, he must have thought he was the one closest to the Demon Buddha.

“We are not close.”

“Then why are you granting him a request like this?”

“I’m going all the way beyond the frontier even though I’m not close with you either.”

At the chilly answer, Cult Leader Pungcheon said a word too.

“Do not change too much as a person.”

“Look who’s talking.”

With these two bickering even before we had departed, it was now time to set out.

“Now then, let us go pull up some hidden poisonous herbs.”

Chapter 318: The Answer Is Not Here.
------------------------------------
It had been two days since we left the Central Plains and entered the frontier lands.

“Sandstorm!”

At Jeogyeon’s shout from outside, I quickly stuck my head out the carriage window.

Whooooooooosh!

A tremendous sandstorm was blowing in from far away.

Like a massive tidal wave, a wall of red sand was surging toward us. It was so terrifying that one might think this was the sort of sight we would see at the end of the world.

“West! Run at full speed!”

At my order, the carriage changed direction and began to race.

As if this was hardly his first time experiencing such a thing, the Pungcheon Cult Leader sat with a relaxed expression.

The Demon Buddha and I, on the other hand, watched nature’s wrath with startled hearts, inwardly preparing for the possibility of being swept up in the sandstorm.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, the Demon Buddha, and I could somehow endure even if the storm caught us, but the horses, the carriage, and of course the guards might be blown far away. Even if we had to abandon the horses and the carriage, we had to save the people from harm somehow.

Whooooooooosh!

As I watched the massive wall of sand drawing closer and closer, a thought suddenly came to me.

I want to block that storm by raising the Third Form of the Nine Flames Demonic Art, the Great Demon Wall.

It was said that the higher one’s realm in the Great Demon Wall rose, the smaller the demonic wall became.

But I found myself wishing my demonic wall would be different. I wished that my Great Demon Wall, having surpassed Ten-Star mastery and reached Twelve-Star Great Completion, would be large enough to block that sandstorm.

I imagined the shield of Ten-Star mastery growing larger and larger until it became a colossal wall. I imagined it becoming an immense fortress wall that would not collapse before anything.

Fortunately, the carriage narrowly escaped the sandstorm’s area of influence. It was thanks to our quick judgment and sprinting away.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader said to me,

“Well? How does the frontier taste?”

“It’s spicy from the very beginning.”

We watched the sandstorm sweep past behind the carriage, devouring everything in its path.

I wondered how people could live in a place like this, but after traveling for a while, we saw a father and son passing by while driving livestock.

The young boy helped with his father’s work, herding the animals with a long stick. They were people who had known in advance that a sandstorm was coming and knew where they needed to move.

We stopped the carriage for a moment and watched them travel on.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader abruptly asked,

“Have I ever told you that I didn’t want to leave the frontier? I keep forgetting things these days.”

“What does it matter whether you have or haven’t? You can just tell me again.”

“I don’t want to. Saying the same thing again and again makes me seem like a senile old man, doesn’t it?”

“I’ll take it as a display of your humanity.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s gaze returned to the father and son driving their livestock in the distance.

“Gowol once said this to me. We sang endlessly of advancing into the Central Plains, advancing into the Central Plains, but had we ever truly thought about it seriously? I couldn’t deny those words then. To be honest, I liked living here. There’s an atmosphere here that you can’t feel in the Central Plains. Ah, of course, I admit it. It was probably because I lived without any worries. After all, sandstorms like that don’t strike the Cult Leader’s Hall.”

At that moment, the Demon Buddha suddenly spoke.

“It’s because you’re a country bumpkin.”

When the Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at him, the Demon Buddha continued.

“What useless reasons are you tacking on when a country bumpkin says he likes his hometown? Takes all the charm out of it.”

His rebuke was closer to hurt than scolding. He was asking why the man had to add at the end that he felt that way only because he had lived carefree as the cult leader. He could simply say it proudly. I like this place, and I didn’t want to leave.

The two of them had been like this throughout the journey here. They would go all day without saying a word, then suddenly engage in a war of nerves over something trivial. And at times like this, they would also care for each other.

He did not show it outwardly, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader was on edge and tense. If I could sense it, then of course the Demon Buddha could as well.

The reason the Demon Buddha picked unnecessary fights and said things like that just now was likely to ease the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s tension.

Someone who did not know him might wonder, Does the Demon Buddha have that kind of consideration? They might find it unexpected, but that was not the case. Among the Demon Venerables, the Demon Buddha was a deeply considerate man who valued loyalty.

That was why I had thought of him, who had remained loyal to my elder brother and kept his faith, as a small giant.

“Now then, let us set off again.”

The carriage began to speed toward the place from which the sandstorm had come.

* * *

Many things happened on the way to the Pungcheon Cult.

A band of bandits charged at us, trusting only in their numbers, and lost their lives, and several of the escort martial artists suffered stomach pains after drinking unfamiliar water.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader found medicinal herbs nearby and fed them to them. As he did, he said that young people these days were pathetically weak, and made the difficult-to-believe claim that when he was young, he had been perfectly fine even after drinking water mixed with blood and poison.

As always, the Demon Buddha threw in a remark.

“Why are you nagging so much? They say when you get old, you should close your mouth and open your purse.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was not someone who would obediently shut his mouth.

“It’s because I’m lonely, lonely! What good is knowing everything at my age? None of the young ones ask me anything. What this herb is, what effects it has, when I learned about it.”

Knowing that it was not merely a joke, I looked at him and smiled.

That was not the only hardship of the journey. Jeogyeon lost the way and wandered several times. There were many places where the map and the actual terrain were different.

If anything, the Demon Buddha knew the roads better than the Pungcheon Cult Leader did.

“Isn’t this your territory?”

“That is precisely why I know it even less. My subordinates carried me around, so how would I know the roads?”

It was the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s subtle protest as someone with a poor sense of direction.

Another problem with traveling through the frontier was where to sleep. As we went deeper into the frontier, it became difficult to find inns.

“We will have to camp here tonight.”

Jeogyeon got down from the carriage and began preparing camp.

The guards divided their roles and moved in perfect order. While keeping watch over the surroundings, they lit a bonfire, dressed the game they had hunted, and prepared dinner.

I personally took care of the sleeping places for the Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Demon Buddha. I laid soft grass on the ground and placed smooth animal hides over it.

When he saw me paying attention to their bedding, the Demon Buddha said,

“Don’t worry about us. Take care of your own sleeping place.”

“I’m young, aren’t I? I’d be fine sleeping on a bed of gravel. Now, try sitting down.”

“You only look young on the outside. There are times when you feel more senior than that directionally challenged fellow over there.”

When the Demon Buddha teased him, the Pungcheon Cult Leader deliberately wriggled his backside like a child.

“It’s comfortable. Very comfortable.”

The Demon Buddha also looked pleased with the bedding.

Then I made mine even softer than the two of theirs. Watching this, the Pungcheon Cult Leader wore an expression of disbelief.

“Yours is practically a soft tiger hide, isn’t it?”

“I’m the Young Cult Leader, am I not?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader shook his head as if dumbfounded, while the Demon Buddha looked as though he had expected as much. For the first time in a while, the Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Demon Buddha were of one mind.

“Did you see that shameless expression?”

“You said you could sleep on a bed of gravel!”

I lay down on the soft hide and laughed, then asked the Pungcheon Cult Leader,

“What kind of person is the next cult leader?”

“He is someone I chose as my successor from the very beginning and raised as such. A trustworthy man.”

Of course, he must be trustworthy. But with the Illusion King involved, there was no way to know whether that trust would remain intact. The bastard would surely be somewhere near the new cult leader.

“It is up to you, Cult Leader, to find him. You know the Pungcheon Cult better than anyone.”

I believed in him. There were not many people who could abandon the position of Pungcheon Cult Leader for the sake of friendship with someone. I believed in what made him special.

“Do not trust me too much. As you know, I am indecisive and emotional.”

“That is why I like you. I do not like people whose convictions are too rigid.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at me with an expression that seemed to say, What sophistry are you about to spout now?

“I like people who can change according to the moment, people who do change. People like you, Cult Leader. I like people who, when they lack confidence, can say outright that they lack confidence. Isn’t that better than pretending to know, pretending to be confident, and causing a huge accident for no reason? And you have already shown me, haven’t you? What kind of person you are in fields you know well, and what kind of person you are in your own world.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at the Demon Buddha and shook his head.

“When I am with the Young Cult Leader, even not having rigid convictions can feel this good!”

Thus the night in the frontier only deepened.

* * *

At last, we arrived at the Pungcheon Cult.

When we reached the main gate, a grand welcoming ceremony awaited us.

Hundreds of martial artists were lined up, and a long red carpet had been laid out. It was an exceedingly dazzling reception.

Before them, the new Pungcheon Cult Leader, So Baekta (昭伯打), had personally come out.

“I greet the Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk gave a cupped-fist salute as he greeted him.

“You have worked hard coming such a long way.”

So Baekta was wearing the colorful and splendid traditional attire unique to the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and his face was painted in various patterns used for illusion arts, all in vivid primary colors.

Was it because of that? Seen favorably, one could sense a meticulousness in him, as though he did not wish to reveal even the slightest bit of himself at their first meeting. Seen unfavorably, there was an indescribably eerie feeling about him.

“I have been looking forward to this, as rumors say the Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult possesses the greatest talent in history.”

“Rumors tend to be exaggerated.”

“It seems the rumor that you are the greatest handsome man was not exaggerated at all.”

Contrary to his appearance, he was skilled at conversation.

After greeting Geom Mugeuk in that way, he greeted the Demon Buddha next.

“I greet the Demon Venerable.”

“I greet the Cult Leader.”

“I paid my respects to you when you visited last time.”

“I remember.”

At that time, even the Demon Buddha had not expected So Baekta to ascend to the position of cult leader so quickly.

Lastly, So Baekta greeted the Pungcheon Cult Leader. He ran over and clasped his hands tightly.

“Master, it has been a long time.”

“Have you been well, Cult Leader?”

Now that he had become the cult leader, the Pungcheon Cult Leader also observed propriety toward him.

They say that a position makes the man, and the disciple he met after so long was far more confident and composed than before. So Baekta was one of the disciples he had raised from childhood, and he possessed the highest martial skill among them, as well as deep loyalty toward him. That was why he had long ago marked him as his successor.

“I wanted to see you so badly that I could not wait in the Cult Leader’s Hall and came running out like this.”

Contained within those words was the implication that if the Pungcheon Cult Leader had not come with them, he would have received Geom Mugeuk in the Cult Leader’s Hall. It would be one thing if the leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had visited, but this was the Young Cult Leader’s visit.

“Now, please come in.”

The three of them walked together behind So Baekta.

As he walked, he introduced various buildings of the Pungcheon Cult to Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Buddha. He showed a cheerful and bright side, but for some reason, the Pungcheon Cult Leader felt a strange sense of incongruity from it.

‘Was he always like this?’

The So Baekta he knew had been calm and reticent.

In that way, the three of them entered the Cult Leader’s Hall.

At that moment, the Pungcheon Cult Leader flinched in surprise. The interior had changed greatly from when he had been cult leader. The throne chair was set higher and had been replaced with an even larger one. The color of the walls had been repainted, and the stone statues, paintings, decorations, and everything else were different.

“The interior has changed a great deal.”

“My subordinates suggested it would be better to change it, so I accepted their advice.”

“You did well. New wine should be put into new wineskins.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader showed no sign of it at all, but Geom Mugeuk could sense it. The Pungcheon Cult Leader was not in a good mood.

He could understand his feelings. It was a position he had handed over even though, originally, it had not yet been time to pass it on. Yet no sooner had So Baekta taken that position than he had freely changed the Cult Leader’s Hall like this. How could that feel pleasant?

On top of that, So Baekta went one step further.

After entering the Cult Leader’s Hall, So Baekta went up to the throne chair and sat down. As a result, he was looking down at the three of them.

Of course, his words remained courteous.

“For a long time, your cult leader’s family did not visit our cult. Yet now the Young Cult Leader has come like this, so it is truly an occasion to commemorate.”

There was a barb in those words, saying that until now, you people had not come to see us.

“Is that not all thanks to the former Cult Leader here?”

So Baekta’s gaze turned toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“He is a treasure to our cult.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader smiled as he looked at him, but just as Geom Mugeuk expected, his mood was very poor.

The other party was the Young Cult Leader, and he himself was the cult leader, so he could receive him while sitting in the throne chair. But today, was he not here as well? They could have spoken from the guest seats below, yet So Baekta deliberately looked down from up there.

A few formal words were exchanged with Geom Mugeuk. After they spoke of hoping that the peaceful relationship between the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Pungcheon Cult would continue for a long time, So Baekta said,

“You must not have recovered from the fatigue of your journey, so please rest today and let us speak tomorrow. Tomorrow, we will hold a banquet to welcome the Young Cult Leader.”

“Thank you for your welcome.”

Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Buddha left the Cult Leader’s Hall first.

Now only the Pungcheon Cult Leader and So Baekta remained in the hall.

Then So Baekta descended from the throne chair.

“Please do not be angry, Master. Because the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult was here, I deliberately put on a show of authority.”

“Do you think authority is established by sitting in a high seat and looking down?”

A silence flowed for an instant. Soon, So Baekta lowered his head.

“This disciple’s thinking was shallow.”

When he lowered his head, the Pungcheon Cult Leader said,

“No. I did not say that to reproach our cult leader. Raise your head. The leader of the great Pungcheon Cult does not lower his head carelessly.”

So Baekta raised his head with a bright face. At times like this, he did resemble the disciple from before.

“I am so happy you came, Master. Please rest well before you leave.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader slowly left the Cult Leader’s Hall. He could not easily adjust to the changed hall.

* * *

When he returned to the guest hall, I sensed something unusual from the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s expression.

“How was it?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader expressed the sense of disharmony he had been feeling all along like this.

“Something is subtly different. I cannot tell whether he changed naturally after becoming cult leader, or whether I feel that way because I suspect there is something behind him.”

I asked carefully,

“Could he have been subjected to a soul-capturing art?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader firmly shook his head.

“That is not it.”

If the Pungcheon Cult Leader said it was not, then it certainly was not.

“Then from now on, there is one thing you must be sure to uphold.”

I told the Pungcheon Cult Leader the first principle I had established after my regression.

“Look at that person clearly. Do not look roughly—look properly. But you must cast aside your preconceptions as you look. My disciple was originally like this and that, he was originally this kind of person. Throw away all such thoughts.”

If he could look properly in that way, he would be able to find it.

“The answer does not lie within you, Cult Leader. Do not fall into the trap of thinking you must find something in your own heart. The answer lies in the person you are looking at.”

Chapter 319: It's Always Because of the Young Cult Leader.
----------------------------------------------------------
That night, the Fengcheon Cult Leader was pacing the courtyard, unable to sleep.

Geom Mugeuk’s words kept circling in his ears—look at the other person properly, without prejudice.

He had failed to look at his disciple, So Baekta, without prejudice.

He had focused only on the differences. Had he always had such a personality? He didn’t think he had been like this before.

Without looking at him properly, he had let himself be swept along by emotion.

‘He changed the Cult Leader’s Hall so completely? If he had any respect for me, he wouldn’t have done that.’

But if he considered it coldly, respect for him and changing the Cult Leader’s Hall were separate matters. He might have changed it after deciding it was no great matter, or there could have been some other reason.

Yes, Geom Mugeuk was right. The answer was not in his own heart, but with So Baekta. He should not have looked at the changed Cult Leader’s Hall; he should have looked into So Baekta’s eyes.

As he wandered the front yard amid such thoughts, the Demon Buddha walked out, brightening the darkness with golden light.

“Which of the three cult leaders is keeping you from sleep?”

“Three cult leaders?”

“Is it our Young Cult Leader? Or the current leader of the Fengcheon Cult?”

“And the remaining cult leader?”

The Demon Buddha stared intently at the Fengcheon Cult Leader. Only then did the Fengcheon Cult Leader realize that the last cult leader the Demon Buddha spoke of was himself, the previous cult leader.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader laughed self-deprecatingly and said,

“The problem is always because of the last cult leader.”

At that, the Demon Buddha offered a different answer.

“No. The reason we cannot sleep is because of the Young Cult Leader.”

How could he not know?

If not for the Young Cult Leader, this very gathering today would not have existed. That So Baekta would still be in the position of staring only at the Cult Leader’s Hall, thinking, ‘When will I become cult leader?’

The thought sent a chill through him.

Gowol would still be bound inside the Cult Leader’s Hall. No, since he had possessed the key to his shackles, by now he might have left him.

And he himself would be leaping up and down, issuing orders to kill Gowol without ever knowing what kind of man he truly was. The thought of himself living such a life horrified him.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader sat down on a rock. It was to meet the Demon Buddha’s gaze.

If not for the Young Cult Leader, that Demon Buddha, too, would probably have been living a different life.

If it had not been the present Young Cult Leader, but another, would the Eldest Young Master have become the successor?

Then the Demon Buddha said something unexpected.

“To be honest, I have been enjoying myself lately.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader was startled. The words “You’ve gone mad” almost slipped out without his realizing it.

To think the word “enjoying” would come from the Demon Buddha’s mouth. Yes, he was human too, so he could feel enjoyment. But he had never expected him to reveal that feeling to him.

“I am enjoying this feeling of living a life other than my own. Had the life I lived until now been too tedious?”

A life of laying down the power struggle, a life of coming all the way here to help the Fengcheon Cult Leader, a life of receiving stacks of paper covered with drawings of poisonous herbs—he was certainly living a life different from before.

“I had never once thought, ‘What would it be like to live a different life?’”

Listening to the Demon Buddha, the Fengcheon Cult Leader suddenly said,

“Come to think of it, I don’t believe I have ever looked at you properly.”

Even when they had once gotten along like friends, even when they had fought with harsh words over the succession, and even now. Not once, it seemed, had he truly seen him properly.

The Demon Buddha was not at all offended. It was because he, too, sympathized with the meaning contained in the Fengcheon Cult Leader’s words. And so the Demon Buddha easily reached his conclusion.

“That is why all of this is the Young Cult Leader’s fault.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader nodded with a smile.

“Yes, it is always because of the Young Cult Leader. Because of our insolent, clever Young Cult Leader who keeps adults from sleeping.”

These days, the moments he found most amusing were when he and the Demon Buddha spoke ill of Geom Mugeuk. Whenever they cursed Geom Mugeuk, he was always the most excited.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader looked up at the night sky. The moon beyond the frontier looked even more desolate and lonely. Then, in that instant, he caught himself.

‘There I go again. Again, I am looking for the answer in my own heart first.’

He looked up at the moon again. Looking again, the moon beyond the frontier and the moon of the Central Plains were the same. Yes, even while looking at the moon, he would practice. The answer was in that moon.

* * *

The next day, before they went to the welcoming banquet hall, there was a strategy meeting.

“By now, the one behind this side will have lost contact with the woman in the flower garden. Naturally, he will think she was dealt with by us. There will be two possible reactions. He will either lie low, or retaliate.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader and the Demon Buddha did not show any sign of fear. Naturally so, because whoever the enemy was, they did not think he would dare take revenge against the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, the Demon Buddha, or the former leader of the Fengcheon Cult.

But Geom Mugeuk knew. The opponent was the Illusion King, who had lost Hwanyeo. No one could know how he would act. And so he awakened the two men’s vigilance.

“As you saw through the woman in the flower garden, they are people who use the demonic arts of the Blood Cult, which were thought to have been lost. We don’t know what may come out, so you must not let down your guard.”

The Blood Cult was certainly effective on the Fengcheon Cult Leader. The moment he heard those words, his gaze grew intense. Moreover, the Fengcheon Cult Leader was a man who believed in the conspiracy theory that there were those trying to revive the destroyed Blood Cult.

“Cult Leader, please closely observe whether anyone attending today’s banquet has perhaps fallen victim to the Soul-Capturing Art.”

To the Demon Buddha, he gave a different request.

“They may try to approach not me or the Cult Leader, but exploit a blind spot and approach you, Lord Demon Buddha. Please keep that in mind and watch for anyone suspicious who comes near you.”

Geom Mugeuk rose from his seat and spoke with vigor.

“Now then, let us go. The war begins now.”

Together with the two men, he left the guest hall.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader and the Demon Buddha walked side by side on either side of Geom Mugeuk. Here, beyond the frontier, they were his wings.

* * *

At the welcoming banquet hall, not only the Five Great Elders of the Fengcheon Cult but also all its principal experts had gathered.

Not everyone in the Fengcheon Cult was an expert in illusion arts. There were also pure martial artists who did not use illusion techniques. Elder Hou Yang, who was now greeting the Fengcheon Cult Leader warmly, was one such person. He was an expert swordsman counted among the Three Great Swordsmen Beyond the Frontier.

“It has been a long time.”

Now that he could no longer call him Cult Leader, Hou Yang felt awkward about what to call the Fengcheon Cult Leader.

“Have you been well?”

“Thanks to you, I have been well. It seems you have lost a great deal of weight.”

At those words, the Fengcheon Cult Leader smiled with satisfaction. These days, those were the words he most liked to hear.

“Your face also looks peaceful.”

“Thank you. You look very well yourself.”

In the past, he would naturally have thought this:

Because our relationship was good before, of course he must be friendly toward me now.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader gazed at him calmly.

Was that truly so?

Now, that question came to him with weight.

This was what Geom Mugeuk had meant.

Past relationships, personality, reputation, pleasing words, or slips of the tongue. Do not drag such things in and judge through assumption. Look properly at the gaze and expression with which that person looks at you now, and listen properly to the words he speaks now.

“I am sorry I did not value and appoint you to more important positions.”

“What do you mean?”

“I respected you, as someone who came from among pure martial artists. I was grateful that you devoted yourself to our cult. And so I had intended to treat you better, but I kept putting it off and lost the chance. I am sorry.”

He apologized to him. Apologizing like this was also a change that had come after meeting Geom Mugeuk. He had apologized to Gowol, apologized to the Demon Buddha, and apologized to Hou Yang. Yes, he would apologize to them all.

He could feel Hou Yang’s bewilderment. The man could not hide the emotion of wondering what scheme he had in mind.

Yes, the relationship between Hou Yang and himself was exactly this much.

A distance that caused bewilderment: ‘Why is this man suddenly acting so kindly?’ This was the distance between him and himself. The vague sense of closeness he had felt earlier was an emotion that had existed only in his own heart.

When he made the effort to look carefully, things he had never seen before began to appear.

“I only said it because if not today, I may not have another chance to tell you.”

“Thank you for having thought of me that way.”

With Hou Yang at the head, the other elders also came to greet the Fengcheon Cult Leader.

After exchanging greetings with each of them, the Fengcheon Cult Leader asked,

“But I do not see Elder Xing.”

At that, Hou Yang spoke cautiously.

“Elder Xing stepped down from his position on his own.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader was startled. Elder Xing was a man named Xing Ye, the head elder among the Five Great Elders. He had also been the person the Fengcheon Cult Leader had cherished most.

“What was his reason for stepping down?”

“He said he wished to rest now.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader recalled what Elder Xing had said when he stepped down from the position of cult leader and left.

—I shall serve the new Cult Leader until this life of mine runs out.

—I leave believing only in you.

A man like that stepped down because he wanted to rest? That could not be. If he had stepped down, there had to be some reason.

In the meantime, people also came to greet Geom Mugeuk.

A chance to personally meet the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was something that might not come even once in a lifetime. And so they all introduced themselves, trying somehow to leave even the slightest impression.

At that moment, one woman offered a bold greeting.

“If I had known the Young Cult Leader was this handsome, I would have escaped beyond the frontier long ago.”

She was a middle-aged woman with an open, amiable face. Unlike the people of the Fengcheon Cult, who exuded a rough and bewitching air, she had kind eyes.

“I am Gu Shu.”

“I am Geom Mugeuk.”

Geom Mugeuk knew who she was. She was one of the Five Great Elders, a woman who had followed the Fengcheon Cult Leader devotedly.

Then the Fengcheon Cult Leader walked over and said,

“If you fall for the Young Cult Leader, you really may attempt to escape beyond the frontier.”

Gu Shu smiled brightly and greeted the Fengcheon Cult Leader.

“Like our elder brother?”

“Elder brother?”

At the word “elder brother,” not only the Fengcheon Cult Leader but everyone nearby was startled.

“Now that you have stepped down as Cult Leader, I ought to call you elder brother. Do you not remember? I said that if you stepped down from the position of Cult Leader, I would call you elder brother.”

To be honest, he did not remember. Since she was not the sort of person to make up such a thing, he realized anew just how little he had properly listened to what others said.

“I thought it was a joke.”

“I have never once joked about it.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader merely blinked as he listened, but Geom Mugeuk could feel it. She respected the Cult Leader.

“Young Cult Leader, before you return, I hope I will have the chance to treat you to a cup of wine.”

“I would welcome that anytime. How could I miss the opportunity to learn wine and life from a beautiful older sister who has weathered every hardship?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s glib reply, Gu Shu burst into a bright smile and chided the Fengcheon Cult Leader, telling him to be at least half as charming as the Young Cult Leader.

If there were elders with such refreshing personalities, there were also elders like Wang Xiao, who only exchanged greetings and then quietly observed.

Laughter came from the side.

When Geom Mugeuk turned, he saw the Demon Buddha sitting on the edge of a table, talking with people. Because he had met many people in his time, he had a knack for leading conversations.

At that moment, Cult Leader So Baekta entered the banquet hall.

As he entered, the martial artists of the Fengcheon Cult all paid their respects in unison and welcomed him. The pattern that had been on So Baekta’s face yesterday had been completely erased.

Was that why? The bewitching atmosphere he had given off now felt pure today.

So Baekta attended to Geom Mugeuk first.

“Today is a day for you, Young Cult Leader, so please eat and drink to your heart’s content.”

“Today is the first time I have learned that the wine beyond the frontier is this delicious.”

“It is a special wine prepared for an honored guest.”

Next, he exchanged greetings with the Demon Buddha, and lastly, he attended to the Fengcheon Cult Leader.

“Master, did you sleep well?”

“Thanks to our Cult Leader’s consideration, I slept comfortably.”

He deliberately used the phrase “our Cult Leader” so everyone would hear. No matter where or how the one behind this had infiltrated, the Fengcheon Cult Leader felt a sense of responsibility to protect So Baekta, and the Fengcheon Cult.

“Cult Leader.”

“Yes, Master.”

“Returning to the cult after so long, I suddenly wish to see the Yin Thunder Bell. May I?”

“Of course. I will escort you.”

It was also because he wanted to be alone with So Baekta, and he truly did want to see the Yin Thunder Bell.

Leaving the banquet hall, So Baekta led the Fengcheon Cult Leader toward the Cult Leader’s Hall.

‘The Yin Thunder Bell was not in the place I went to yesterday.’

Though puzzled, the Fengcheon Cult Leader silently followed behind So Baekta.

Upon entering the Cult Leader’s Hall, So Baekta climbed up and sat in the grand preceptor’s chair, then infused inner force into the place where one rested one’s hand.

Then the walls on all sides began to rise.

Ruuuuumble.

Behind the walls stood display cabinets, and within them the sacred treasures of the Fengcheon Cult were kept.

“Since I was changing the interior anyway, I changed the way the sacred treasures were stored as well.”

“It is an extremely intricate mechanism. Impressive.”

Of course, it did not suit the Fengcheon Cult Leader, who had lived for the pleasure of looking at those sacred treasures.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader slowly moved his steps and looked over the treasures. Seeing them after so long filled him with fresh emotion. He had taken all of these with him to the Divine Cult.

The Yin Thunder Bell was placed in the center behind the grand preceptor’s chair.

“It truly has been a long time.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader’s gaze deepened as he looked at the Yin Thunder Bell. It had been so very long since he had seen the evil spirit engraved upon the Yin Thunder Bell.

‘Gowol told me to send his regards.’

Standing beside him, So Baekta said in a low voice,

“Do you remember what you said to me when you left the cult?”

In that instant, the Fengcheon Cult Leader flinched.

‘What did I say when I left?’

At that time, he truly had been out of his mind. All he had thought was that he had to finish matters quickly and return to Gowol.

But even so, he did not remember those words?

It was the same with forgetting what Gu Shu had told him earlier.

This was not a question of whether his memory was good or bad. This was a question of his attitude toward life. An attitude of being intoxicated solely by his own emotions, of not looking properly at others, of not listening properly to what others said. That was why he forgot everything.

To him, reality… must have been a world inferior even to the world inside an illusion.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader, who had been looking at the Yin Thunder Bell, turned his head and looked at So Baekta.

So Baekta calmly repeated the words the Fengcheon Cult Leader had said to him that day.

“I could not achieve my dream, but you must be sure to achieve yours.”

Upon hearing those words, he remembered. Yes, he had said those words to him. What else could a person leaving say? He had meant it as a wish that So Baekta would remain for a long time and die as the Cult Leader.

So Baekta also turned his head and looked at the Fengcheon Cult Leader. Their gazes met with the evil spirit of the Yin Thunder Bell between them.

“I intend to achieve that dream.”

“What is your dream, Cult Leader?”

Somehow, he felt words that should not be spoken were about to come out. And that premonition was accurate.

“The unification of the martial world.”

“!”

Yes, that much was still fine. For the leader of a great power like the Fengcheon Cult, it was a dream one could have at least once.

The problem was what came next.

“I believe I shall now take the first step.”

Chapter 320: My Successor Said So
---------------------------------
It felt strange.

The disciple speaking of unifying Murim before him felt like a stranger.

This was the disciple to whom he had handed the position of cult leader, believing he would protect the Pungcheon Cult better than anyone.

So much so that he had checked again to see if the boy had fallen under a Soul Subjugation Technique. But he hadn’t. Not one Soul Subjugation Technique was affecting So Baekta.

What if it was a technique he didn’t know? If such a Soul Subjugation Technique existed, that person would already rule Murim.

He was taking the first step toward the unification of Murim? You crazy bastard! Did he even know what kind of path that was?

Holding back the urge to shout like that by force, he treated the boy with the courtesy due a cult leader.

“What do you mean?”

“It was a joke, Master.” That was what the Pungcheon Cult Leader had hoped to hear. Yes, let’s laugh it off with a hearty chuckle.

But So Baekta spoke calmly, his face devoid of any trace of a smile.

“I suppose I must start by unifying the outer Murim.”

The Pungcheon Cult was not the only one in the outer regions. Though not comparable to the Central Plains, many sects here vied for supremacy.

In the outer regions, the Pungcheon Cult held a position akin to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult in the Central Plains. Naturally, there were forces occupying positions comparable to the Murim Alliance or the Saedo Alliance.

“Surely you do not mean to start a war?”

“It will be impossible to subjugate them without shedding blood.”

On top of that, So Baekta pushed even further.
