When his words ended, So Baekta’s desperate cry flowed out through the closing door.

“Do not come, Master! You mustn’t come…….”

The door that had swallowed the two of them shut with a thunderous roar.

Chapter 325: At Times Like That, This Is What You Say.
------------------------------------------------------
The two of them were inside the painting.

It was a painting that looked exactly like real people, and the movements were vivid as well.

When the Pungcheon Cult Leader was looking at Sobaekta, I saw Hwanwang.

It was the same Hwanwang I had seen before the regression.

Hwanwang, so you were here at this time.

Inside the painting, Hwanwang took Sobaekta and disappeared through the door.

Mine and Mabul's gazes naturally gathered on the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

The Cult Leader's eyes deepened as he gazed at the painting. He must be feeling the ominous aura beyond that door.

"Impressive."

It was a word acknowledging Hwanwang. Yes, of course. Because it was a world created by Hwanwang. Hwanwang was a greater master than Hwanyeo. That was why he was also the one who ultimately made a name for himself in the world.

"It's dangerous. I'll go alone."

At those words, I spoke with a smile.

"I wondered who the disciple took after."

"What do you mean?"

"If you truly wanted to go alone, you wouldn't have said it was dangerous, would you?"

It meant the same as when Sobaekta shouted not to come while being dragged away earlier. It was a more burdensome plea than begging to be saved. Just like saying 'it's dangerous, so I'll go alone.'

"Even if I said I was leaving you behind, you aren't people who would listen to me anyway, are you?"

"We came together, so we finish it together."

There was no way I would send the Pungcheon Cult Leader into that painting alone.

"Ready?"

"Ready."

Mabul nodded along with me.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader recited an incantation before the painting.

Huuuuuuung.

With a deep reverberation, we stood before that door.

We had entered the painting in an instant.

The spectacle of illusion arts performed by a master who had reached the pinnacle was truly art.

Gigantic beasts gathered from all directions, baring their teeth.

But perhaps because the imposing aura radiating from the Cult Leader was frightening, they didn't dare charge.

On the other hand, they acted as if they would pounce on me and Mabul at any moment.

Mabul strode toward them. Though they bared their teeth and growled, they couldn't charge at Mabul either.

Mabul reached out and stroked a beast's head. Surprisingly, the beasts remained still.

It was the moment I confirmed firsthand just how strong Mabul was against illusion arts.

Meanwhile, the Cult Leader stood before the door.

He closed his eyes briefly to compose his mind, then placed his palm against the door.

As the Cult Leader chanted an incantation and slowly pushed, the door began to open with a loud sound.

We entered shoulder to shoulder.

Beyond the door, a long corridor awaited us. It was an endless corridor that felt as though once we passed through it, we would never be able to return.

"When I saw his face, he resembled that woman in the garden too much. They are probably twins or siblings."

As I walked, I informed the two that Hwanwang and Hwanyeo were blood-related.

"Then this place is even more dangerous."

At Mabul's words, the Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded. He must have sensed it too. Since he was the one who killed her, one of the two would not be able to leave alive.

"You don't seem nervous at all."

"With the two of you here, why would I be nervous?"

This time, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked Mabul.

"Why aren't you nervous?"

"With you two here, you'll figure it out."

This time, I asked.

"What about the Cult Leader?"

"Me? ...Because I'm here. Damn! I feel like I'm the only one getting the short end of the stick!"

I laughed cheerfully at the Pungcheon Cult Leader's joke. But I could feel it. He was definitely more nervous than when he faced Hwanyeo last time. There seemed to be some kind of aura that only masters of illusion arts could feel.

We continued walking down that long corridor like that.

"This illusion art was also refined in the Blood Cult."

"I've felt it before, but the Cult Leader seems to have profound insight into Blood Cult martial arts as well."

"Because we are the Blood Cult."

Because the very origin of the Pungcheon Cult was the Blood Cult.

"Long ago, following defeat in the war against the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, all of the Blood Cult's leadership died. The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult knew they couldn't annihilate the Blood Cult. Because killing all of those spread throughout the Beyond would require too great a sacrifice. So the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult wished for a new Blood Cult."

"That is the Pungcheon Cult."

"Yes. In the early days, it is said that those who wanted to preserve the Blood Cult as it was and those who wanted to discard the name Blood Cult clashed fiercely. But since the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult supported the side that wanted to establish the Pungcheon Cult, it was ultimately reborn as the Pungcheon Cult. For a while, the Pungcheon Cult with a pro-Heavenly Demon tendency continued, but at some point, they broke free from that sphere of influence."

Over that long history, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult also underwent many changes.

"This bastard is someone who has properly inherited Blood Cult demonic arts. There is undoubtedly a force trying to rebuild the Blood Cult."

"You mean a secret society?"

"It won't be a secret now. They've kidnapped the Pungcheon Cult Leader and even drawn in the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult."

Meanwhile, we arrived at the end of the corridor.

Three doors awaited us.

The three doors emanating a mysterious aura seemed to be saying exactly this: depending on which door you choose, your life or death will be decided.

"Which way shall we go?"

The Pungcheon Cult Leader's eyes deepened as he gazed at the three doors.

"It's a trap. If we go together through one, we will be scattered; only if we enter separately will we meet."

"Then let's go separately."

"I'll take the center."

The Pungcheon Cult Leader chose the center door, and Mabul and I each entered the left and right doors.

When we opened the door and entered, there was another corridor.

As I walked down the corridor and looked back, the door I had entered through had vanished. There was no going back now.

When I exited through the door at the end of the corridor like that, neither the Pungcheon Cult Leader nor Mabul were visible.

I realized in an instant. That the Pungcheon Cult Leader had tricked us.

He intended to face Hwanwang alone. Those three doors were a message Hwanwang had left for the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Come alone, leaving the two behind.

Because this fight was between the Pungcheon Cult Leader and Hwanwang. Because the Pungcheon Cult Leader wanted to protect me and Mabul.

The place I entered was a banquet hall. Dancers were dancing, and musicians were playing cheerful tunes. Young women held bottles of alcohol and beckoned me to come.

Just the atmosphere here lent weight to the earlier speculation.

Stay here and rest.

I looked around for a way out. But the bluish light was not easily visible.

Since this space was also created by illusion arts, there had to be a way to break it somewhere.

Then a man in the banquet hall walked up and spoke to me.

"Please rest comfortably. If there is anything you wish for, simply tell me anything."

"Where is the way out?"

"The door only opens when the time comes. It will not open before then."

Shhhk! Sruk!

I cut the man down with a single slash.

The fallen man vanished with a popping sound, turning into black smoke.

Because of that, the music stopped, and everyone froze in their movements, staring at me. Naturally, they were not people. They were evil spirits formed of demonic energy.

I looked around at them and asked.

"Raise your hand if you know how to get out of here!"

Instead of raising their hands, they charged from all directions.

* * *

In contrast to Geom Mugik, who had entered the banquet hall, the place Mabul entered had a brutal atmosphere.

Pitch-black darkness. It was a place where you couldn't see an inch ahead. Even the usual radiant light emanating from his body couldn't illuminate the surroundings.

Mabul could feel it. That something was here.

He quickly formed a mudra with his fingers.

Revolving Dharma Light Seal!

In an instant, a golden radiance burst from Mabul's body.

At that moment, he could see that place.

Dozens of strange beasts shielding their eyes from the light.

Reacting to the light, they all howled at once. It was a piercing, ear-splitting wail that felt as though it would rupture eardrums.

A new mudra formed in Mabul's hands.

Slaying Dharma Repentance Seal!

The next moment, golden energy surged and erupted from Mabul's palms.

Kwaeeeeeeeeek!

The tremendous energy surged like a wave, sweeping away all the strange beasts there.

Puk! Peok! Puk! Puk! Puk!

The strange beasts vanished, turning into black smoke.

Mabul strode forward and opened the door to the next room.

It was a plaza-like place more than ten times larger than the previous room.

And the evil spirits filling that place all turned to look at Mabul. Red eyes and torn mouths. It was truly a sight that made him want to close the door again.

But Mabul raised one corner of his mouth and entered. Rather, he shut the door tightly, as if to ensure no one could leave.

The evil spirits shrieked and charged at Mabul from all directions. There were so many that they trampled one another.

Amid that chaos, a new golden mudra was forming.

* * *

Hwanwang and Sobaekta entered a spacious main hall.

That place was filled with stone statues and murals on all sides, massive monuments covered in incomprehensible patterns. It was surely Sobaekta's first time there, yet it felt familiar.

"Where is this place?"

Sobaekta faced him calmly. Cursing and acting hostile was not a wise choice. He had to calmly find an opportunity.

"Is this not the place where you are every day?"

"Surely not? The Cult Leader's residence?"

It was different from the current Cult Leader's residence, and different from the Cult Leader's residence in Master's time as well.

"It's the Blood Cult's Cult Leader's residence."

"Yes. This is the true Cult Leader's residence."

Hwanwang's eyes were filled with emotion as he looked around that place. He truly respected and loved this place.

"This is where the true demons of the Beyond lived. Not pitiful ignorant brats like you."

The words 'ignorant brat' stabbed at his heart. But now was not the time to act like an ignorant brat.

"Why did you bring me here?"

"To use you as a hostage."

Sobaekta's heart sank, but he spoke nonchalantly.

"Those are truly nonsensical words."

"Because your master is not someone you can deal with stylishly."

Sobaekta could tell. That Hwanwang truly acknowledged his master. He had cursed so much when he was with Sobaekta. Now that he thought about it, those curses were ultimately curses directed at himself.

"Master will not come."

"He will come."

"He does not cherish me that much."

"If that were not the case, he wouldn't have rushed all the way to the Beyond. The Young Cult Leader came to kill me, but your master came to save you."

Sobaekta, who had been blank for a moment, abruptly spoke.

"Come to think of it, my life seems better than yours."

Hwanwang silently stared at him, who had suddenly changed.

"You're cowardly, have no pride, and are even despicable? You're probably just a faithful dog who only listens to someone's orders."

Sobaekta charged fearlessly.

"You always groveled to me. 'Cult Leader, have you arrived? Did something good happen today? You are surely the one who will lead the new martial world, Cult Leader. Cult Leader.' Licking yesterday, licking today. If I told you to lick, you'd probably have licked the soles of my feet."

When Hwanwang reached out his hand, Sobaekta went flying.

His internal energy already suppressed, Sobaekta was helplessly caught in his grip. The only thing he could fight with was his mouth.

"What are you so scared of that you suppressed my internal energy? Isn't this your hell? You coward! Lick my ass!"

Despite the pouring ridicule, Hwanwang didn't get angry; rather, he smiled as he looked at Sobaekta.

"Unexpected. To think you cherish your master this much."

Hwanwang saw straight through Sobaekta's inner thoughts. He realized Sobaekta was trying to provoke him into killing him.

"If you want to die that badly, dash your head against the horn of that stone statue and kill yourself. Do you truly have the courage for that?"

Hwanwang threw Sobaekta before the stone statue.

Sobaekta looked at the stone statue with trembling eyes. The horn growing from the monster's forehead was as sharp as a spear.

As he gazed at the sharp horn, he felt death approaching.

Then, like a kaleidoscope, memories of being with his master when young came to mind.

Come to think of it, Master particularly cherished him. He acknowledged that Sobaekta was born with talent, and even praised him to his fellow disciples, telling them to learn from Baekta. The day he was praised by Master, he was so happy he couldn't sleep.

On the day of the Blood Spirit Ceremony, when the Yin Thunder Bell was struck, he would call Sobaekta to strike it together.

As he did so, Master would stroke Sobaekta's head and say, "You will inherit my position."

Just because Master failed him once after doing well ten times, he resented and denied Master. You foolish, ignorant fool! If someone does well ten times and fails once, they're still someone who did well nine times.

Sobaekta, reproaching himself, squeezed his eyes shut.

'I cannot be a burden to Master any longer.'

He recklessly hurled his face toward the horn.

Thwack!

He thought he would die like this, but the stone statue's horn crumbled like soft clay.

From afar, Hwanwang clapped and laughed. He had changed the horn with illusion arts.

"This is why I can't kill you. How could I kill such a precious hostage when you think of your master this much?"

Sobaekta hurled his body toward the wall.

But before his head hit the wall, he flew into Hwanwang's grasp.

Hwanwang gripped Sobaekta and looked down at him.

"If your master dies, it will be because of you."

It was a horrifying thought just to imagine. He tried to bite his tongue, but no strength entered his jaw.

"...Please kill me."

At that very moment, someone entered that place and spoke in a clear, ringing voice.

"At times like that, instead of asking someone to kill you, you say this."

The one who strode in was none other than the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

"If Master dies, it's because of you. If Master dies, it's because our master's martial arts were weak. Master's death has nothing to do with me. And to bastards like that who toy with people's hearts, you say this: Get the hell out of here, bastard!"

To Sobaekta, who was looking at him with eyes filled with emotion, the Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke calmly.

"I will teach you again, one by one, from now on."

Chapter 326: It's Because You Don't Know Him.
---------------------------------------------
Sobaekta turned to the Illusion King, about to speak.

To the man who had toyed with his heart.

Why don’t you get lost, you bastard!

The Illusion King, sensing the sign of it, spoke a step ahead of him.

“I’ve already heard it, so don’t say it.”

The Illusion King was not at all tense at the appearance of the Pungcheon Cult Leader. Of course, that did not mean he had let his guard down. Using Sobaekta as his shield, he stood behind him and said,

“Welcome, Cult Leader. Please understand the circumstances, unsightly as they are.”

He was using his disciple as a hostage and acting like a coward, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader could feel that the Illusion King was truly strong. His skill was one thing, but the man was also the sort who would do anything to achieve his objective.

“It’s fine. Isn’t being that frightened your way of showing me respect?”

They were in a relationship where they would kill each other, yet they treated one another courteously. No—because the other was someone they would kill anyway, they could afford to be courteous.

Unlike Geom Mugeuk or the Demon Buddha, nothing had blocked the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s path on his way here.

Indeed, the Illusion King had invited only the Pungcheon Cult Leader to this place.

“Experts always die because of pride. If the Young Cult Leader or the Demon Buddha had come with you, it would have been troublesome to deal with.”

“When there are too many people, it only makes a fight distracting. You haven’t experienced the Young Cult Leader, so you don’t know. Before the fight even began, you’d get so furious you’d smash your own head against that wall.”

As he said that, the Pungcheon Cult Leader leisurely looked around with his hands clasped behind his back.

“So this was the Blood Cult’s leader hall. Oh, was this blood demon originally shaped like this?”

The blood demon painted on the wall was quite different from the form passed down in the Pungcheon Cult.

“This is where the true Blood Cultists lived.”

“How would you know, when you never lived there? If anything, I think it would’ve been even more of a mess than it is now. Wouldn’t this great hall have been swarming with things mad with thirst for blood?”

The corners of the Illusion King’s eyes creased slightly.

At his reaction, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked slyly,

“You’re on that side, aren’t you? The secret society trying to revive the Blood Cult?”

“Calling it a secret society makes it sound far too grand. At first, yes.”

“And now?”

“The revival of the Blood Cult is not my ideal.”

Only then did the Pungcheon Cult Leader turn his gaze to his disciple.

“That is how it should be. Even a man like him is trying to find his own dream, isn’t he? Let the Central Plains conquest you’ve heard about since childhood drift away just like that.”

Sobaekta was still staring at his master, wrapped in agitation. He feared his master would die because of him; that was all he could think about.

“Are you all right, Baekta?”

When his master called his name and asked if he was all right, Sobaekta’s heart surged. There were so many things he wanted to say, but he did not know what he should say. The only words that came to mind were these.

“Do not submit to him because of me. I am not worth that much.”

The Illusion King watched with interest to see what answer the Pungcheon Cult Leader would give.

“Do you know what the Young Cult Leader said when I told him it was dangerous to enter the painting here, so I’d go alone?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader brought up Geom Mugeuk.

“The Young Cult Leader said this: that was me begging him to come with me. So if you’re worried about me, you should say the opposite. Please save me. If you’re my master, then you have to save me. Save me! Even if you die, save me! Like that. Then wouldn’t I get so disgusted that I’d leave you to live or die?”

He said it like a joke, but it struck Sobaekta’s heart.

‘That’s right. The more dignified I act, the greater the burden on him to save me.’

When that thought reached him, Sobaekta repeated exactly what his master had said.

“Please save me. Even if you die, Master, you must save me.”

At those brazen words, the Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded as if he had been waiting for them.

“All right, I’ll save you.”

Sobaekta was flustered, since that had not been what he had meant to hear.

‘That is not it, is it, Master?’

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s eyes said this:

It’s fine. Leave it to me now, my disciple.

Because of that, Sobaekta could not simply stay still.

He quickly turned, trying to slap the Illusion King across the cheek at least.

But the Illusion King caught his hand and stopped him.

Sobaekta glared at him and made one final effort.

“You bastard, you don’t have to get lost. Instead, kill me and fight my master properly. You won’t be able to fight to your heart’s content while worrying about me either. I’m a hostage who will hold you back too. So kill me and pour out everything you have.”

The Illusion King stared silently at Sobaekta, then spoke to the Pungcheon Cult Leader beyond him.

“This is why he weighs on your mind.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded. There was no need to hide his true feelings from the Illusion King, a man who must have weathered every sort of hardship.

“When I was right beside my disciple, nothing happened, but once we were apart, a trigger like this appeared.”

That was why triggers were so frightening.

Meeting Geom Mugeuk had completely changed his life. His relationship with Gowol had changed, and in the end, his very life had changed as well.

By coming beyond the borders this time, his relationship with his disciple had also changed greatly.

Like this, when it came to changing the relationships between people, a trigger sometimes came before effort. Just as he had become enemies with this Illusion King.

“I know that you killed my elder sister.”

“I did.”

“How did she die?”

“She died when the Jeryeongin left her.”

As if he understood what had happened, the Illusion King nodded silently.

“Originally, I was supposed to go to the Demon Cult, and my sister was supposed to come here. But she insisted. The Demon Cult mission must have looked bigger to her. She was like that since childhood. She always wanted to beat me somehow. She never once did, though.”

No sorrow or mourning could be felt from the Illusion King. The Pungcheon Cult Leader could tell. This was a man who lived only for himself.

“If I kill your disciple, that would make things even.”

“It’s no use trying to shake my composure like that. I’ve already sensed that you are not foolish to that extent.”

It was a counterblow containing the implication that the Illusion King could not defeat him in a straightforward fight, but the Illusion King was not shaken either.

“He is a disciple who would cast away his life like a blade of grass for his master, so you must save him.”

“I’ll try.”

At that moment, Sobaekta did not let go of the thread of hope. He was watching only for an opportunity. For his master, he would even cling to the man’s trouser legs and drag him down. If that could save his master, he would not mind dying.

And if by even the slimmest chance he survived, he would live the rest of his life for his master.

It was then.

Swaaaek!

Thud!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader thrust his hand into the empty air to his left.

Then blood began dripping from the space where there had been nothing.

A corpse fell with a thud, blood flowing from its neck. It was one of the Illusion King’s subordinates, who had approached while hidden, using the gap in their conversation to attempt a surprise attack.

Wiping the blood on his hand against the man’s clothes, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked,

“By the way, why did you put the wind of unifying the lands beyond the borders into my disciple?”

“Why, for your disciple’s dream, of course.”

For an instant, Sobaekta flinched, but as if to show him that this was how one dealt with such a situation, the Pungcheon Cult Leader said calmly,

“Thanks to you, my disciple truly found his dream. I should be grateful, rather.”

If it had been his former self, he would not have been able to respond so flexibly. He had met Geom Mugeuk, seen a new side of Gowol, argued and struggled—and in the process, he too had grown.

In a flash, the Pungcheon Cult Leader vanished and reappeared.

Puuuk!

A bloodied hand burst out of the empty air.

He had caught another enemy who had been approaching while concealed. Instead of condemning the ambush,

“I lost weight recently, so my movements have gotten faster.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was even bragging about his weight loss to the Illusion King.

Looking around to see if there were any others hidden, the Pungcheon Cult Leader walked toward the Illusion King as if it was time to fight in earnest. Before that, he asked about Geom Mugeuk.

“The Young Cult Leader?”

“He’ll be fine.”

“No, I meant, did you properly lock him up?”

“He is trapped in a place he cannot escape. Even if he breaks through that place, there are three more checkpoints he can never break through.”

“Only three?”

“Only?”

“I’m telling you, you don’t know that man.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader released his aura.

Swaaaaaaaaa.

A powerful ghostly energy unlike anything experienced before spread in all directions, and blue flames began to burn from both of the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s hands.

“Then I’ll have to hurry. So I can show off when the Young Cult Leader gets here.”

* * *

“Ah! This isn’t right.”

I opened the door with great momentum, then closed it again.

After dealing with all the evil spirits in the banquet hall and finding the method to break through, I found the next door.

Where had the method been? It had been hidden beneath the geomungo the musician had been playing. It was fortunate I’d had experience finding hidden breakthrough methods several times; for someone searching for the first time, it would not have been easy at all.

The problem was the next room.

When I opened the door a moment ago, a massive snake was coiled in a room as large as a plaza.

The Blood-Venom Great Python.

It was the Blood-Venom Great Python, called the king of venomous snakes. Around the Blood-Venom Great Python swarmed thousands upon thousands of poisonous snakes.

Just from opening the door for that brief moment, the poisonous fumes and fishy stench were overwhelming. The poison was so vicious that if an ordinary expert had merely opened the door, he would have been poisoned and forced to circulate his qi to recover. More importantly, though, I absolutely hated snakes.

“Cult Leader, fight well. I trust you and will turn back.”

Of course, I could not do that. If it were only a matter of catching the Illusion King, I could trust the Pungcheon Cult Leader. But now there was the variable called Sobaekta. Who knew what dangerous situation his affection for his disciple might create?

I had to get there as quickly as possible and create the variable called “Geom Mugeuk.”

Suddenly, I remembered when the Pungcheon Cult Leader had come to save me when I was trapped in an illusion. I remembered the hellscape that had stretched beyond the open door. The Pungcheon Cult Leader had broken through that path to save me.

Yes. Even if every snake in the Central Plains is there, I’ll break through.

I drew the Black Demon Sword and opened the door again, stepping inside. The poisonous snakes nearby lunged toward me.

Shik! Shiiik!

I swung the Black Demon Sword and cut them down. As the snakes burst apart, poisonous fumes sprayed out.

The other snakes began moving all at once. They were no ordinary snakes. They sprang up, whipping their bodies through the air as they rushed at me.

The next instant!

A single form was unleashed.

The Nine-Flame Demonic Art, Third Form: Great Demon Wall.

A massive wall of ganggi rose before me.

Thud! Thud! Thuuud! Thud!

The snakes that collided with the wall of ganggi burst apart. Poisonous fumes spread thickly. That poison provoked every venomous snake in the room.

The Blood-Venom Great Python raised its massive body and charged at me.

Boom!

With an explosive roar, the Great Demon Wall trembled. The Blood-Venom Great Python, wounded by the ganggi, began to thrash. Because of that, smaller snakes were crushed to death in chaos.

The Blood-Venom Great Python spewed venom.

Chiiiiiiik!

This atrocious venom, which would melt anything it touched, was truly horrifically toxic. Poison mist billowed thickly. If I had not been immune to all poisons, there would have been no way to endure it.

The venom began melting the Great Demon Wall.

A tremendous amount of internal energy was consumed to maintain the Great Demon Wall.

And in this fight, I made a new attempt for the first time.

With the Great Demon Wall still raised, I unleashed another form.

An evil spirit appeared before the Great Demon Wall. I had unleashed the Second Form: Great Annihilation.

Ssssssss.

The evil spirit split into six. In the meantime, my accomplishments in martial arts had advanced, and the number it could split into had increased from four to six.

“Go!”

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa!

The six evil spirits swept through the snakes and pushed forward.

Puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puk!

Every snake in their path was cut apart.

Their target was the Blood-Venom Great Python straight ahead.

Could they really take it down?

The Blood-Venom Great Python, which had been melting the Great Demon Wall, sprayed poison at the evil spirits. Drenched in venom, the evil spirits pushed straight ahead.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa!

Puaaaaaak!

I saw it. The six evil spirits severing the Blood-Venom Great Python’s body.

The head side and the tail side, cut into two, began to writhe in agony. As it thrashed, the surrounding poisonous snakes were crushed to death.

The writhing Blood-Venom Great Python charged at the Great Demon Wall with its severed body.

Kwaaaang!

The Great Demon Wall shook violently, but with a body cut apart, it could do nothing to the Great Demon Wall.

Thud!

The Blood-Venom Great Python collapsed to the floor and twitched one last time before disappearing with a bang, bursting into a terrible cloud of poison. Then all the other poisonous snakes also began to vanish.

Puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puk!

Normally, even after defeating them, one would not be able to pass through this room filled with poison mist.

But I crossed it and opened the door behind where the Blood-Venom Great Python had been, stepping inside.

Astonishingly, beyond the door was the leader hall.

And the moment I saw the scene spread out there, I froze. The place was filled with traces of a bloody battle, broken on every side, corpses strewn about.

My gaze went to the grand preceptor’s chair in the distance.

Someone familiar was sitting there.

Astonishingly, it was the Pungcheon Cult Leader. He was covered in blood, his head drooping.

The corpse lying before the chair was none other than Sobaekta. In the end, he had failed to protect his disciple.

At the thought that the Pungcheon Cult Leader might be dead, my heart began to pound madly.

I launched myself toward him. Had I ever been this shocked in recent days?

After landing before the grand preceptor’s chair, I called him.

“Cult Leader.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader did not move.

“Cult Leader.”

Then the Pungcheon Cult Leader laboriously opened his eyes and looked at me.

“……I couldn’t kill him.”

“Cult Leader.”

“……I’m sorry.”

The life faded from his eyes, and his head was about to droop again.

“Cult Leader!”

Just as I was about to support him—

Shiiik!

Puuuk!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s hand, which had reached out to seize my vital point, had stopped in front of my chest. Conversely, my ten-thousand-year cold iron dagger was buried in his heart.

Black energy swirled in his two eyes, and pitch-black ghostly energy flowed out.

“……How did you know?”

He had been a fake Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“The Cult Leader I know wouldn’t have said ‘I’m sorry’ as his last words. He’d have said, ‘I’m dying because of you. Hand over my divine artifact, Young Cult Leader! Try living well without me and Gowol!’ That’s the kind of person the Pungcheon Cult Leader is.”

The moment my faith was conveyed, the fake Pungcheon Cult Leader’s body burst and scattered into black smoke. Sobaekta’s corpse, which had been lying collapsed, also burst into smoke and began to disappear.

I believe.

“That man could get stabbed to death while drinking in Maga Village. But he won’t die inside an illusion here. This is his world.”

I strode toward the door behind the grand preceptor’s chair.

A moment ago, I had felt what it would be like if the Pungcheon Cult Leader died. Even though part of me suspected it was a trap, my heart had pounded as if it would burst. I absolutely could not let the scene I saw here become reality.

With the Black Demon Sword drawn, I threw the door open and leapt inside. It did not matter what waited beyond it. Just as you came to save me—

‘This time, I’m coming!’

Chapter 327: You Have Already Saved Me.
---------------------------------------
Corpses formed a mountain.

It was not a figurative expression. The corpses of evil spirits truly filled the space, and seated atop them was Mabul, eyes closed as he chanted Buddhist sutras.

Thud thud thud thud thud thud thud!

The evil spirits began disappearing one after another in clouds of black smoke. Since they had all been dealt with almost simultaneously, they vanished together.

Once all the evil spirits had disappeared, a door came into view beyond them.

Mabul hurried toward it.

He had never expected to find himself worrying like this, but he was genuinely concerned about the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Weren’t they friends? If they had remained as they were in the past, close only in words and laughing heartily together, he would never have taken such a risk to save him.

But lately, he had come to understand him. When the Pungcheon Cult Leader had first said he was quitting, Mabul had thought, What kind of idiotic thing is he doing? Now, he understood him.

Was that why?

He did not want the Pungcheon Cult Leader to die in a place like this.

When Mabul opened the door and entered, he found himself in a wasteland.

Whoooooosh!

A sandstorm blew in and engulfed him.

“Damn it.”

He was sick to death of this wretched illusion and this wretched sandstorm.

How was he supposed to escape from this endless wasteland?

Then something caught Mabul’s eye.

It was a flower blooming in a crack between the rocks. Mabul stood before it for a long while, looking down at the flower.

Whoooooooosh.

Just as an ominous sound seemed to come from behind him, the surroundings suddenly darkened as a shadow fell over him.

When Mabul turned around, a gigantic evil spirit of truly enormous proportions was looking down at him. It was an evil spirit formed from clumped sand.

Salbeop Hoein!

Golden qi surged from both of Mabul’s palms and shot forth.

Screeeech! Boom!

The qi struck the evil spirit squarely in the chest.

The next moment—

Whirrrrrr.

The sand around them swirled upward, then quickly filled the hole.

Screeeech! Boom!

The second blast of qi pierced a hole through the evil spirit’s face, but the result was the same. Sand instantly filled the hole.

Whoosh!

When the evil spirit extended its hand, sand poured forth with a rattling roar.

It was no ordinary sand. Sharp qi whirled around it, capable of tearing the body to pieces at the slightest touch.

Mabul threw himself aside to evade the attack.

Kra-boom!

The ground where he had been standing was gouged deeply, sending dust billowing into the air.

As Mabul retreated backward, the giant evil spirit slowly approached, as though intending to stomp him into the ground.

Mabul swiftly formed hand seals with both hands.

Demon Palm Annihilation Seal!

The next moment—

A shadow appeared around the evil spirit.

The evil spirit turned its head backward.

Above its head, a gigantic Buddha’s palm—far larger than the evil spirit itself—was striking down at it. Golden radiance poured from the palm.

Kuwaaaang!

“Kueeeeeeeek!”

Letting out an ear-splitting shriek, the evil spirit scattered into sand.

As Mabul pressed his palms together in prayer, the golden hand turned into a mass of light and vanished into the sandstorm.

Only the enormous imprint of a palm remained deeply embedded in the ground where the giant evil spirit had been.

A door appeared where the giant evil spirit had disappeared.

As Mabul was about to walk toward it, he saw the flower he had noticed earlier lying on the ground.

He picked it up and stared at it for a moment before tucking it into his waistband.

Then he strode into the next door.

* * *

When the Pungcheon Cult Leader imbued both hands with blue energy, the Hwan King hid behind Sobakta and said,

“You are not my opponent.”

Clank, clank.

The iron doors on either side of where the Pungcheon Cult Leader stood opened, and a master of illusion emerged from each one.

The Hwan King introduced them.

“They are the Blood Guardians who protect the Blood Sect.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader replied,

“Imitations made by copying the Blood Guardians. Listen, and don’t forget. The Blood Sect is an organization that has already vanished.”

After finishing his words, the Pungcheon Cult Leader extended a hand.

Whirrrrrrrr!

Amid the sound of fierce wind, one of the Blood Guardians was sucked into the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s grasp.

It was the same technique the Hwan King had used earlier when pulling Sobakta toward him. The two techniques were similar, yet different.

Within the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s grasp, the man’s body began twisting without managing to resist even once.

“Grrrrrk.”

Demonic energy flowed from the Blood Guardian’s eyes. It was not the aura of a human being.

Crack crack crack crack.

His entire body twisted like laundry being wrung out, and he died.

“How long did it take to create this place?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader could tell. The space within this painting had not been created solely through the power of the man standing before him, the Hwan King.

It was a preparation laid over hundreds of years in pursuit of the Blood Sect’s resurrection—a culmination of the Blood Sect’s illusion arts.

“It has existed since the time I first joined this organization.”

“You abandoned that long history and pursued your own dream.”

“You know as well as I do, don’t you? That as one goes through life, the wind sometimes blows in unexpected directions.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded and was about to answer when another Blood Guardian suddenly rushed toward him.

The instant he lunged for the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s vital points—

Screech! Thunk!

With the sharp sound of wind, something invisible pierced through his torso. The charging Blood Guardian collapsed on the spot.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader made a motion as if pulling something from the man’s body. Though nothing could be seen, judging by his actions, it seemed to be a long spear.

“How about it? I’d like to test myself against your Formless Qi.”

The Blood Sect, too, had possessed techniques that used invisible qi as a weapon, and so did the Pungcheon Cult.

Naturally, the Hwan King refused. If he were the sort of person who would accept such an offer, he would never have taken Sobakta hostage.

“That would be discourteous to those waiting for you.”

Clank, clank.

The iron doors on both sides opened again, and this time two Blood Guardians emerged from each.

“How many Blood Guardians did you have again? I read about it a long time ago, so I can’t remember.”

Judging by the Hwan King’s relaxed smile alone, there were likely many of them prepared. The fact that they emerged in groups instead of all at once was meant to exhaust the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s internal energy as much as possible.

“Isn’t it a waste to throw them away like this?”

“They were created for that very purpose.”

The cold-hearted Hwan King had no reason to worry about the Blood Guardians being expended.

The Blood Guardians, for their part, charged forward without knowing fear.

Screeeech!

Invisible Formless Qi flew from the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s hand.

Splurt!

The Blood Guardian at the front was driven into the wall. Since the spear could not be seen, he appeared to have died suspended in midair.

The remaining three Blood Guardians charged forward while simultaneously striking with their palms. Palm force filled with demonic energy flew toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

The spot where the Pungcheon Cult Leader had been standing was shattered, but he was already gone.

The Blood Guardian in front had his waist broken, the one on the left took a palm strike to the chest, and the last had his throat pierced.

The iron doors opened again, and this time four Blood Guardians emerged from each side. They knew no fear. Within this illusion, the Blood Guardians were nothing but killing weapons that carried out orders.

“I take back what I said before. I take back what I said about the old Blood Sect having been more bloodthirsty and savage than it is now. You’re worse.”

At the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s mockery, only a cold sneer appeared on the Hwan King’s lips.

The eight Blood Guardians who emerged this time attacked differently from those before them. They scattered in all directions, then began chanting in unison.

Red lines extended from their fingertips, forming a red web throughout the surroundings.

Like a net from which there was no escape, it pressed in on the Pungcheon Cult Leader and tightened around him.

“It’s dangerous, Master!”

Sobakta shouted in alarm, but there was nowhere to escape.

Slice.

At the moment the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s body was about to split into more than a dozen pieces, he vanished from that place.

He reappeared behind the Blood Guardian who had been attacking from the east.

Whirl.

Slice.

The blue energy in both of the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s hands became long lines and severed the Blood Guardian’s neck.

Before he could fall, another blue line from the Pungcheon Cult Leader cut through the air.

Thud!

The forehead of the Blood Guardian in front was pierced, and he collapsed.

Whirrrrrrrr.

The red and blue lines became entangled in the air.

But the red lines could not catch the blue ones. The blue lines slipped between the complicated tangles.

Whenever the blue lines drew taut, pieces of the Blood Guardians’ bodies were sliced away. The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s movements, using lines of qi, displayed the ultimate essence of illusion martial arts.

The Hwan King had naturally expected them to withstand an attack of this level, so he remained at ease even as the Blood Guardians died one after another.

More than anyone, he knew how much internal energy each of the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s techniques consumed. No matter how profound the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s internal energy was, he could not deal with all the Blood Guardians.

The iron doors opened again, and this time eight Blood Guardians emerged from each side.

* * *

Flap, flap.

The moment a blue butterfly settled on a Blood Guardian’s shoulder—

Sssrrrk.

The Blood Guardian slowly collapsed and died.

The butterfly that had felled the last Blood Guardian vanished from the air with a pop.

It was the moment the final Blood Guardian fell—the thirty-second of all those who had emerged from both doors.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader still stood with his hands behind his back, looking relaxed and aloof.

But Sobakta could hear it: his master’s breathing, which had previously been inaudible. His master was tired.

His master had been fighting an exceedingly difficult battle. The Blood Guardians were threatening enough, but the real problem was the Hwan King. While the Blood Guardians fought, he launched surprise attacks and kept up pressure, threatening his master. Whenever his master tried to sweep them all away with a grand illusion, the Hwan King invariably stepped in to interfere.

Master…

If Sobakta had not been there, his master would have tried to kill the Hwan King first by any means necessary.

Now, thirty-two Blood Guardians had emerged from each chamber. Sixty-four in total.

A fierce battle unfolded between the Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Blood Guardians. They charged at him like a swarm of bees, and he confronted them by drawing upon every martial art and illusion technique he possessed.

Blood Guardians were sent flying in bloody heaps beneath the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s palm force.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader evaded their attacks with illusions. Each time he unleashed a martial art, Blood Guardians fell as corpses, but his internal energy continued to be consumed in equal measure.

Boom! Boom!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, driven backward as he blocked the rain of palm techniques with protective qi, was besieged by the Blood Guardians’ attacks. To that was added the treacherous Hwan King’s assault.

Enduring the impacts, the Pungcheon Cult Leader swung both palms. Blue qi swept through the space, smashing several more Blood Guardians apart.

Amid the fierce battle, the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s voice reached Sobakta through sound transmission.

―Three, two…

He counted without saying anything else.

But even without being told, Sobakta knew what his master wanted.

―One.

Sobakta tried to slam the back of his head into the Hwan King standing behind him with all his might.

Because the Hwan King had been concentrating all his attention on the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s battle, the headbutt nearly struck him squarely in the face.

At the instant he took a step back to evade it—

Screeeeeech!

Sobakta lowered his body, and the qi released by the Pungcheon Cult Leader swept through the space above him.

Kuwaaaang!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader rushed toward them.

It was an overly reckless attack, and he was struck repeatedly by the Blood Guardians. But he endured with his protective qi and flew toward Sobakta.

Just as the Pungcheon Cult Leader was about to escape into the Spatial Transposition Illusion Art together with Sobakta—

Whirrrrrrrr.

An invisible cord pulled Sobakta away first.

The Hwan King, who had retreated, had snared him before them.

The surprise attempt to rescue Sobakta had failed, and the Blood Guardians’ attacks poured down upon the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Boom! Boom! Bang!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader once again focused on his battle with them.

And when he had finally dealt with all the remaining Blood Guardians, his breathing was ragged, and blood trickled from the corner of his mouth. He had overused his internal energy and suffered internal injuries.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader had a premonition that he might die here. In front of Geom Mugeuk, he had loudly declared that this was his world. But everyone had limits.

Wiping the blood from the corner of his mouth, the Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at Sobakta. He remembered the boy when he had still been small—the boy who had looked up at him with bright, clever eyes.

“For some reason, I took a liking to you. Even among all those disciples, you stood out. Baekta. Even if I could go back and do it all over again, I would still make you my successor.”

Tears Sobakta had held back throughout the battle streamed from his eyes. An inexplicable grief welled up inside him. Now he could do better. If only he could survive, he could truly do better now.

Then the Hwan King whispered to Sobakta,

“You were right. Now is the time to concentrate on the battle.”

The Hwan King had sensed that it was time for him to step forward in earnest.

When the final door opened, sixty-four Blood Hand warriors would emerge from each room. Once they joined the assault, the Pungcheon Cult Leader would be finished.

“Watch closely. Watch how your master dies. Then I’ll destroy your dantian, sever the meridians in all four limbs, cut out your tongue, and throw you into the prison. I’ll make sure you spend the rest of your life remembering what happened today.”

Sobaekta was in despair. It was fine if he himself suffered. What he could not bear was his master dying because of him. I’m sorry, Master. I’m truly sorry.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke to his disciple.

“Where have you ever heard of the Pungcheon Cult Leader shedding tears in an illusion? Wipe them away.”

Sobaekta wiped away his tears.

“And what did I tell you to say when you met someone like that?”

Sobaekta turned toward the Illusion King and said,

“You cowardly son of a bitch! Why don’t you get the hell out of here?”

Sobaekta saw the apology in his master’s eyes. His master was sorry that he could not save him. How could he ever repay such a great kindness?

“Master, you have already saved me.”

He had saved the soul more precious than life itself.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader beckoned for the Illusion King to come out.

The Illusion King slowly took several steps forward, then stopped where he stood.

“Still, I’ve been waiting the longest. Don’t you think you should at least let me get some fresh air?”

He was not one to take risks until the very end. With sixty-four Blood Hand warriors waiting behind each iron door, there was no need for him to go first.

Just as he gazed at the iron door with a satisfied smile—

A faint sound came from beyond it. It was the sound of something colliding, accompanied by vibrations.

The sound grew louder and louder.

Boom! Rumble! Boom! Crack! Crunch!

It seemed that screams had mingled with the sound of the wind, and then the noise gradually subsided.

Silence descended.

Clang.

The door opened, and light spilled out.

The person who strode through, radiating a golden brilliance, was Mabul. Behind him, the corpses of the Blood Hand warriors who had been waiting there lay scattered across the floor.

At his appearance, the expressions of the Pungcheon Cult Leader and Sobaekta brightened. The Illusion King’s expression, on the other hand, hardened coldly.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s gaze fell on the flower tucked into Mabul’s waistband. Yes, saying thank you was not enough. No gratitude in the world would ever be enough. And so he grumbled,

“You had time to pick flowers, did you? Your friend was having the fight of his life over here.”

The word friend came naturally, and the word friend was accepted just as naturally.

“Still, I think I got here earlier than the Young Cult Leader.”

“The Young Cult Leader is always late. He only comes running like the wind when he needs something, ordering me to hand this over or do that. He really knows how to make a person’s blood boil.”

Mabul nodded in agreement.

And that was when—

Kwakwakwakwakwakwang!

A sound like something being swept away rang out.

Kkwaaang!

The door opposite the one Mabul had emerged from was smashed apart, and something shot out through it.

They were the six demons of the Great Extinction Formula.

Not even a corpse remained in the corridor the six demons had pushed their way through.

Everyone held their breath at the sight of them. They were a split from the most terrifying-looking of the four demons of the east, west, south, and north, and the sight of the six demons standing in a row was enough to overwhelm everyone present.

Mabul pressed his palms together and bowed his head toward them. He was expressing his respect for the Nine-Fire Demon Technique.

As the six demons slowly vanished, Geom Mugeuk walked out, dragging the Black Demon Sword, blood dripping from its blade. His clothes were torn, and he was covered from head to toe in wounds. It was easy to imagine how difficult the passage he had crossed before reaching this place must have been. It was clear how desperately he had fought his way through.

The burning gazes of Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader met in midair. What need was there for words?

“In any case, our Cult Leader only badmouths me whenever I’m not around.”

Chapter 328: Try Living Earnestly and Run Away.
-----------------------------------------------
Hwanwang looked at Geom Mugeuk with an expression of utter disbelief.

‘He passed through all those gates?’

The Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke to Hwanwang.

“Didn't I tell you? It's because you don't know this man.”

Ma Bul's gate had been difficult as well, but he had been certain that the gate blocking Geom Mugeuk was absolutely impassable. Especially when he thought of the gate with the Blood Poison Great Net.

“One cannot pass through without being immune to all poisons.”

He had thought Geom Mugeuk wouldn't even attempt it because of the poison. That was why he had judged he could keep the Little Cult Leader tied up without killing him.

Geom Mugeuk stood side by side next to the Pungcheon Cult Leader and spoke.

“I'm close with the Poison King.”

Ma Bul also stood side by side next to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“I'm also close with the Poison King.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader felt an exhilaration he had never experienced in his life as he looked at the two people standing solidly at his left and right. Just moments ago, he had been prepared to die. How could he possibly express this overwhelming emotion in words, when the ones who had rushed to save him from that desperate situation were Geom Mugeuk and Ma Bul?

The emotion that washed over him after the storm of joy was this:

‘The Little Cult Leader has come!’

He had a feeling that if it was Geom Mugeuk, he might somehow be able to rescue So Baekta. Wasn't it precisely because he was that kind of person that they had tangled together singing, “Second Young Master, Second Young Master, Little Cult Leader, Little Cult Leader”?

‘Little Cult Leader, I beg you.’

As if to show that his joy and faith were by no means misplaced, Geom Mugeuk's next action swept the Pungcheon Cult Leader up in even greater passion.

Geom Mugeuk secretly placed his hand on the small of his back and sent a stream of inner force to begin treating his internal injuries.

‘You truly…!’

The Pungcheon Cult Leader could tell for certain. This man, Geom Mugeuk, was a different breed of person entirely. The consideration of worrying about his internal injuries at a time like this, and the boldness of channeling inner energy while standing—it was something an ordinary person couldn't even think of in this situation.

Thanks to this, the Pungcheon Cult Leader's internals grew comfortable and his complexion improved.

Finally, Geom Mugeuk's telepathic message flew to him.

—Even if we let him escape, do not overexert yourself no matter what.

To Geom Mugeuk, capturing Hwanwang was important, and rescuing So Baekta was important, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader's safety was the most important of all.

Hwanwang had completely hidden his body behind So Baekta. So Baekta was still a hostage, but his expression was incomparably bright.

‘With those two here, Master will be safe.’

His heart was as heavy as it was joyful. If the three of them attacked together, how could Hwanwang endure? The problem was himself.

Geom Mugeuk comforted him as his face reddened with shame.

“Our cult's Soul-Devouring Demon Lord fell victim, and even the Martial Alliance Leader's grandson was taken. There is no need to be ashamed at all. It is because they approached with such meticulous care.”

Hwanwang brought his fingers to So Baekta's neck and threatened them.

“Leave at once. Unless you want your disciple to die.”

His fingers looked as though they would pierce So Baekta's throat at any moment.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“We have disciples in our cult as well, so wouldn't we be fine without one or two?”

Before the Pungcheon Cult Leader could answer, Hwanwang spoke first.

“Bluffs won't work. I already know how much the Cult Leader cherishes his disciple.”

Hwanwang was growing impatient. The three people standing side by side before him were individuals he couldn't afford to let his guard down against even in one-on-one combat.

What little fortune he had was that the Pungcheon Cult Leader had suffered internal injuries, and the other two had likely expended much internal energy as well. They had rushed here in haste, so they wouldn't have had time to recover their internal energy.

But even so, facing three people alone was no easy task. Even if the sum of their internal energy was less than his, how could that possibly be a favorable situation for him? His opponents were absolute masters from whom anything could emerge even with just a handful of inner force.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader showed an unexpected reaction to Hwanwang's threat.

“Given who our opponent is, let us say our farewells in advance. I'm sorry I couldn't save you.”

Geom Mugeuk and Ma Bul looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader. They could feel it was not some scheme to save his disciple. He was truly bidding farewell to his disciple.

So Baekta felt his master's heart in the gaze directed at him. These words were enough for him.

“If I am reborn, I will live a life where I will surely become Master's disciple again.”

With the two of them acting this way, Hwanwang's expression instinctively stiffened.

The less value So Baekta had as a hostage, the higher the probability that he himself would die.

If the Pungcheon Cult Leader weren't here, he might be able to escape using illusion arts. But because the Pungcheon Cult Leader was here, even that was no easy feat. In such a situation, he must not rashly leave this space. His martial arts could demonstrate far greater power in this space than fighting outside. What he trusted was the legacy of the Blood Cult—namely, this very place.

“Will you cowardly rush me three against one?”

The one who responded to Hwanwang's words was So Baekta.

“You coward! Hiding behind my back and that's what you say? Kill me and then kill yourself too. Pathetic lives, both of us, let's die together!”

Ignoring his words, Hwanwang looked at Geom Mugeuk. At the look that said the decision was his anyway, Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“Don't look at me. Honestly, the person you hold hostage is not important to me. If I were going to take a hostage, I would have taken one of these two here…….”

He turned his head toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Right when Hwanwang's gaze naturally turned toward him as well—

Geom Mugeuk's figure flashed as he charged toward Hwanwang.

*Swoooooosh.*

That very moment when it seemed he wouldn't attack was a calculated surprise attack. It was an attack that showed he didn't care if So Baekta died.

Because he hadn't expected an attack at this moment, Hwanwang was caught off guard.

Instinctively, he tried to kill So Baekta but gave up. Geom Mugeuk's movement was too fast—so fast that he felt at least one of his own arms would be severed. If his instincts had worked properly, he should have used So Baekta to block rather than dodging.

The moment Hwanwang vanished.

Geom Mugeuk embraced So Baekta and charged backward.

*Shh-shh-shhk!*

He thrust his sword toward the direction he was descending. It was because he considered the possibility that Hwanwang had concealed himself there.

Then the Pungcheon Cult Leader shouted.

“Danger!”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader unleashed a palm strike. The target of his palm force was So Baekta's shadow.

Hwanwang, who had been hiding within So Baekta's shadow, had been about to rise and launch a surprise attack on Geom Mugeuk.

*Bang!*

The shadow vanished, and So Baekta's shadow returned to normal.

Geom Mugeuk quickly undid So Baekta's sealed internal energy. Only then was he able to use his internal energy. It was thanks to Geom Mugeuk's bold decision that they succeeded in rescuing So Baekta.

But there was no leisure to share the joy of rescuing him.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader sent a signal to everyone that Hwanwang was concealed in this space.

Hwanwang's concealment technique was demonstrating truly peak-level skill. The Bloodwind Shadow Concealment Technique he used was a martial art at the pinnacle of Blood Cult concealment techniques. Judging by concealment skill alone, he was a match for Hwi-ui, his father's bodyguard.

Geom Mugeuk sent a telepathic message to Ma Bul.

—I request light!

Ma Bul quickly formed hand seals.

“Dharma Light Manifestation!”

Light erupted from Ma Bul's body and illuminated the space. In that fleeting instant, Geom Mugeuk unleashed the Divine Eye Technique. And he saw. That there was a place where light reflected differently from elsewhere.

*Shhhhhhriek.*

*Thwack!*

Blood sprayed from midair as Hwanwang revealed himself.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader charged in to drag him down, but he hid again by a hair's breadth. However, the Pungcheon Cult Leader thrust his hand into the air once more and pulled him out.

*Pow-pow-pow-bang!*

The revealed Hwanwang and the Pungcheon Cult Leader exchanged palm techniques in midair.

Hwanwang was not pushed back by the Pungcheon Cult Leader at all. Rather, because the Pungcheon Cult Leader was in a state of internal injury, he was at a disadvantage in terms of inner force.

Therefore, Geom Mugeuk flew to his aid. Ma Bul came before So Baekta and protected him. Because Hwanwang might target him again.

Hwanwang mustered all his strength and unleashed twin palm strikes.

*Kwaaang! Kwaaaang!*

Hwanwang, who had pushed Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader back, unleashed a hidden trump card. If he held back his secret technique, he would die without ever getting to use it.

*Whooooooosh.*

Red mist like blood seemed to envelop Hwanwang's body, and when the mist disappeared, his form had changed as well.

His skin had transformed to resemble red steel. His eyes had also turned bright red, emitting a ferocious blood aura.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader recognized the martial art Hwanwang unleashed and shouted a warning.

“Be careful! It's the Ironblood Heaven-Shattering Art!”

The Blood Cult's demonic arts had many factions and branches. The demonic art Hwanwang inherited was of the Blood Spirit Technique lineage, and the Ironblood Heaven-Shattering Art was the final secret technique of the Blood Spirit Technique that pulled up all the inner force and blood aura in the body to make it like steel for a set duration while simultaneously unleashing several times one's usual strength. Moreover, because it was being unleashed in this space, its power was even greater.

*Swoooosh!*

Hwanwang charged at Geom Mugeuk without hesitation.

*Pa-pa-pa-pa-pang!*

Hwanwang rushed forward, throwing two fists in succession. He was so fast and powerful that there was no leisure to use a sword. Raising his protective gang qi, Geom Mugeuk faced him with fist techniques.

As it was Hwanwang's final secret technique, the shockwaves that flooded his entire body each time the fists clashed were tremendous.

Geom Mugeuk unleashed the Vajra Asura, the defensive form of the Thunderclap Asura Fist, and even the Heavenly Demon Body Protection Art. He was hard-pressed just to defend, let alone attack.

An invisible weapon flying from the Pungcheon Cult Leader's hand embedded itself precisely in Hwanwang's back.

But though Hwanwang staggered once, he did not stop his attack. He poured everything out at Geom Mugeuk.

*Boom-boom-boom-boom!*

Ma Bul was not idle either.

“Killing Wheel Seal!”

Gang qi whirled. Just before being struck, Hwanwang dodged to the side.

Then the gang qi flew directly toward Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk also dodged in the opposite direction.

*Kwa-kwa-kwang!*

The place where they had been standing was swept away and sent flying.

Hwanwang immediately flew at Geom Mugeuk once more. Hwanwang, using the Ironblood Heaven-Shattering Art, was keeping up with the movements of the Wind God Steps.

Hwanwang was targeting only Geom Mugeuk. He knew. That among these four, Geom Mugeuk was the most threatening.

If the Nine Transformations Demonic Art unfolded, it absolutely could not be blocked by Blood Cult demonic arts. That was why he was trying to kill Geom Mugeuk first by any means.

So Baekta also watched for an opportunity to attack, but because Geom Mugeuk and Hwanwang were tangled in close combat, he could not recklessly launch an attack.

Ma Bul looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader. His intent could be seen in his gaze.

The moment the Pungcheon Cult Leader began chanting an incantation!

Hwanwang tried to sink down somewhere but sprang up. A black hole had formed beneath his feet. It was not a trap to make him fall. Its purpose was to make him halt his attack for a very brief moment.

That fleeting instant was sufficient.

The very moment Geom Mugeuk leaped backward to create distance!

Ma Bul's hand seal was completed.

“Demonic Palm of Annihilation!”

A massive palm descended upon Hwanwang.

*Kwa-aaaang!*

But Hwanwang raised both hands and blocked it. Both Ma Bul and Hwanwang pulled up their inner force. Just when the side pressing down and the side resisting were tautly matched! The situation Hwanwang feared most unfolded.

Having bought himself distance and time, Geom Mugeuk unleashed the First Form of the Nine Transformations Demonic Art: Annihilation Form.

*Srrrk! Srrrk! Srrrk! Srrrk!*

Four demonic ghosts appearing from the four cardinal directions—east, west, south, and north—sliced through Hwanwang.

*Craaaaack!*

A sound like shattering ice rang out, and the red skin on his body became broken pieces of metal and began to crumble down with a clatter.

The one who revealed himself from within was Hwanwang. Standing with a pale face, blood trickled from the corner of his mouth.

The combined attack of Geom Mugeuk, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and Ma Bul moments ago had been perfect, as if they had practiced it countless times, yet it was a combined attack performed through instantaneous resourcefulness. With opponents like Geom Mugeuk, Ma Bul, and the Pungcheon Cult Leader, the fight could never proceed according to his will.

*Srrsst!*

Despite his severe injuries, he vanished suddenly, and three doors formed in that spot.

His illusion art skill had truly reached the pinnacle—it was admirable even as one watched with one's own eyes.

Geom Mugeuk spoke seriously as he looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“This time, you must not go alone.”

“I wouldn't go even if you pushed me from behind.”

“Which door must we take?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader fell into thought for a moment, then pointed at the third door.

“That one.”

The four people leaped side by side toward that door.

Once they disappeared, something astonishing happened.

*Srrrsst.*

Hwanwang revealed himself there once more. Hwanwang quickly dispelled all three doors he had summoned.

Only then did he vomit a bowlful of blood. He had suffered severe internal injuries when the Ironblood Heaven-Shattering Art was broken.

But on Hwanwang’s face was a smile of triumph.

“No matter which door you enter, you’ll end up at the final remaining gate of this place. It will take at least one shichen to get out.”

It was Hwanwang’s deception. Earlier, they had entered through three doors. He might not have fled through those doors at all, but he had dazzled them with the possibility by presenting three.

First, he would get out of this place and flee far away, to somewhere they could never find him. Next time, I will have my revenge!

Just as he was about to recite the incantation and leave this place—

Puuuuuk!

A single sword pierced his heart.

When Hwanwang lifted his head, Geom Mugeuk was standing before him. Beside him were the Pungcheon Cult Leader, Mabul, and So Baekta.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader said to Hwanwang,

“I saw through your trick.”

While pretending to enter the door, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had taken the three of them into the Space-Time Transposition Art. Just as Geom Mugeuk had deceived So Baekta with the Space-Time Transposition Art before, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had deceived Hwanwang.

Just then, as Hwanwang’s lips twitched as if he meant to say something, Geom Mugeuk’s unwavering attitude toward villains was no different even for Hwanwang. There was no need to listen to some curse a villain left behind before dying.

Shiiiiik!

Slice!

Without hesitation, Geom Mugeuk pulled out the sword lodged in his heart and struck off his head. Hwanwang’s head, eyes wide open, rolled across the floor. At last, Hwanwang had been eliminated.

“First, let us leave this place.”

When the Pungcheon Cult Leader recited the incantation, the four of them were standing before the painting.

At the thought that they had finally escaped from that wretched painting, everyone let out a light sigh.

So Baekta prostrated himself before the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“Master.”

All kinds of emotions surged through him. He truly had not thought he would survive. He was deeply grateful to his master, who had risked his life to come save him. He was grateful to Geom Mugeuk and Mabul as well. After conveying his thanks to everyone, he spoke his true feelings to his master.

“Inside the illusion art, I vowed countless times. If I survived, I would live for you, Master. Just say the word, whatever it may be. For the rest of my life, I will live according to your will.”

At that, the Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke in a gentle gaze and calm tone.

“Do you think that is what I want? If I were that sort of person, would I have abandoned the position of cult leader and left?”

“……Master.”

“You must live for yourself. If you live as the Pungcheon Cult Leader, then you live for the you who became cult leader. If you run away as I did, then you live for the you who fled.”

This was the last thing the Pungcheon Cult Leader would say to him. It was also something he said to himself.

“Even if you run away, live with all your might before you do.”

So Baekta could not lift his head.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader helped his disciple to his feet.

Watching the two of them, Geom Mugeuk crumpled Hwanwang’s painting and placed it on his palm.

Fwoooosh.

Looking up at the ashes that burned in the Samadhi True Fire and rose into the air, Geom Mugeuk said,

“No longer believe in the secret society working for the revival of the Blood Cult. From today on, it is a conspiracy theory and a rumor.”

Chapter 329: I Bet My Wrist There’s No Such Poison Herb
-------------------------------------------------------
If reviving the Hyeolgyo wasn’t the goal, then what on earth had it been for?

Sobaekta wondered about that.

“Why did that man instill in me the ambition to unify the frontier?”

It wasn’t just Sobaekta—the Pungcheon Sect Leader and Mabul as well wore expressions of incomprehension. Naturally, their gazes gathered on Geom Mugeuk.

“Why are you looking at me?”

“Aren’t you our Young Cult Leader who sees through all the secrets of the universe?”

It had been nothing more than a quip from the Pungcheon Sect Leader, a ‘surely you don’t know this too,’ but Geom Mugeuk saw right through it.

“I do have a guess.”

He called it a guess, but in truth, he was certain.

“It is because of Sinung Cheolbang.”

While everyone made puzzled expressions, Sobaekta spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

“That is a place where our cult secretly had weapons prepared.”

“I know. I have already been there with your master. We even uncovered the large underground workshop.”

Sobaekta’s face reddened. Sinung Cheolbang was also a decisive part of a shameful past.

“He seems to have wanted to grow Sinung Cheolbang into the greatest forge in the frontier through war.”

It was a conjecture based on the fact that in his previous life, Sinung Cheolbang had actually become the greatest forge in the Central Plains.

“The war probably lasted quite a long time. Sinung Cheolbang would have secretly sold weapons to the forces opposing us as well.”

Because nothing brought greater wealth to a forge than a prolonged war.

“Investigate it later. If there is evidence that Sinung Cheolbang made contact with other forces in the frontier, then my guess is correct.”

Sobaekta had a feeling those words were true. Because while stoking his ambitions, Hwanwang had constantly emphasized thorough preparations for war through Sinung Cheolbang and said they had to be prepared for a long war. In the end, he had been thoroughly used by that bastard.

“Since the subject of Sinung Cheolbang has come up, there is one more thing that remains to be checked.”

The Pungcheon Sect Leader guessed what Geom Mugeuk wanted to check and sent a telepathic message.

—I’ll do it myself.

The Pungcheon Sect Leader gazed into Sobaekta’s eyes and asked calmly.

“What happened to Elder Seong?”

At first, the Pungcheon Sect Leader had been half-convinced; now, he believed Sobaekta hadn’t killed him. During this hostage ordeal, he had been able to recall why he had chosen Sobaekta.

And so he was certain. Although Sobaekta had been bewitched by Hwanwang and dreamed vain dreams, at least he wasn’t someone who would commit such an act.

“What do you mean? Has something happened to Elder Seong?”

“He is currently missing.”

Just from Sobaekta’s reaction, they could tell he hadn’t killed Elder Seong.

“It couldn’t be! Was it his doing?”

Geom Mugeuk was more relieved than the Pungcheon Sect Leader. If Sobaekta had killed him, the Pungcheon Sect Leader would not have been able to forgive his disciple.

And no matter what punishment he inflicted on his disciple, the Pungcheon Sect Leader would have suffered an irreparable wound to his heart.

Sobaekta berated himself with a gloomy face.

“Ah! If something has happened to Elder Seong, it is because of me. If I hadn’t gotten entangled with that man in the first place, Elder Seong would not have left the cult. I didn’t even know Elder Seong had gone missing.”

The Pungcheon Sect Leader comforted his disciple.

“His fate has yet to be determined, so do not blame yourself too much.”

The four of them left Hwanwang’s quarters in that manner.

Just as they were heading toward the Cult Leader’s hall, a martial artist came and delivered a letter before departing. It was a letter from Gowol. The one who capped off this entire affair was Gowol.

“Strategist Gowol has found out where Elder Seong is.”

At those words, not only the Pungcheon Sect Leader but even Sobaekta jumped in surprise.

“Is he alive?”

“Yes, he is alive.”

“Where is he?”

From Geom Mugeuk’s mouth, that same place was mentioned once more.

“Sinung Cheolbang.”

* * *

The Pungcheon Sect Leader and Sobaekta swept through Sinung Cheolbang together with the experts of the Pungcheon Sect and safely rescued Elder Seong, who had been imprisoned underground.

During that process, all the experts connected to Hwanwang lost their lives.

Those who had worked without knowing anything were not killed but were scattered. Just as Hwanwang had vanished, Sinung Cheolbang also disappeared from Murim.

While the Pungcheon Sect Leader and Sobaekta were reuniting with Elder Seong, I was wandering nameless mountains of the frontier with Mabul, searching for poisonous herbs. Because from now on, the matters of the Pungcheon Sect were theirs to resolve.

The medicinal herbs growing in the frontier were different from those of the Central Plains.

Even when it wasn’t what he was looking for, Mabul carefully examined any new herb he saw. Just as Dokwang had organized them, he too drew herbs on paper and wrote down their characteristics.

He thought that perhaps he did this work because he truly enjoyed it, but on the other hand, he felt that perhaps he had immersed himself in herb gathering to protect himself, because he didn’t think he could endure unless he focused on something like this. I couldn’t know what was truly in his heart.

“Could this be the herb?”

At my shout, Mabul came to check.

“It’s identical. We finally found it! Dokwang, just you wait! I’m returning with the herb I found! The herb found by me, not Mabul!”

Mabul had memorized all the herbs in the drawings, so I was the one holding the paper to compare. Mabul compared the poisonous herb on the paper in my hand with the one I had found.

“It looks similar, but this isn’t it.”

“Jealousy and obstruction won’t work. Step away from the herb!”

“It should have five mouths, but this one has six, doesn’t it? Look here. Didn’t I write it down especially? Ones with six leaves are commonly found.”

“Even this commonly found one was hard to find! Isn’t it too difficult? We’ve already been at it for an hour and haven’t found a single one.”

“If it looked like it could be found easily, I wouldn’t have asked for help.”

“Oh, my back.”

Mabul bent his waist again to scan the grass and spoke.

“Stop exaggerating. That’s the same waist that was perfectly fine even after being beaten by the Cheolhyeol Patcheonsul.”

“Is the waist that works the same as the waist that fights? I can’t find any more! I can’t do it!”

Geom Mugeuk flopped down on a rock.

“And you’re doing so well while an adult is working.”

“The sky looks nice from here. Lie down for a moment.”

The drifting clouds were peaceful. This brief rest felt so precious. Of course, I couldn’t rest alone while Mabul was working.

I sprang up and spread the papers with the poisonous herbs drawn on them around me.

“I’ve never seen ones that look like this in my life. Dokwang is trying to pull a fast one on us! I’ll bet my wrist that this herb doesn’t exist.”

At that moment, as if by magic, Mabul held up a herb high.

“Found one!”

Comparing the herb Mabul brought with the one drawn on the paper, they were identical.

“So it really did exist.”

“Which wrist was it?”

“I’ll have to bet with you after I’ve achieved the Geumgang Bulgoe.”

Mabul placed the herb on the rock and rushed off to dig again.

“You just dug one up, so rest a bit before digging again!”

Without even pretending to hear me, Mabul busily surveyed the surroundings.

Sitting on the rock, I silently watched him gathering herbs. Come to think of it, Mabul was someone who was always busily moving about on those short legs of his. Whether when trying to make his older brother the successor, or now while gathering herbs, he was a person who was diligent by nature.

“Found another one!”

Mabul dug up yet another herb. It too was one of the herbs on the paper.

“You rigged it, didn’t you? You hid the herbs here in advance, didn’t you? You rigged it to target my wrist!”

It was astounding how he could find poisonous herbs so well.

“Isn’t that precisely why Dokwang asked me?”

This time, when he tried to go dig again, I forcibly held him back to make him rest.

Mabul pretended he couldn’t win, shook off his dirt-covered hands, and sat on the rock to rest for a moment.

“Your coming with me this time has truly been a great help.”

“I merely played the role of a folding screen.”

“A folding screen that protected our lives.”

After a brief pause, Mabul asked.

“Say, Young Cult Leader.”

“Yes.”

“Who are the ones behind this matter?”

When speaking with Sobaekta earlier, Mabul had heard that the Soul-Devouring Demon Venerable had been attacked at their cult, and the Alliance Lord’s grandson had fallen victim at the Murim Alliance.

“A certain organization is secretly entrenched and trying to take root in the important organizations of Murim. We do not yet know which organization it is.”

“Does the Cult Lord know as well?”

Gowol had been sharing all information with the Tongcheon Pavilion. Naturally, he knew that a certain organization was operating in secret.

“Since the Chief Strategist knows, Father likely knows as well.”

At that, Mabul nodded with a relieved expression. This must be the faith the Demonic Venerables had in his father.

“They are ones who have brought in someone who has legitimately inherited the Hyeolgyo’s illusion arts. Be careful going forward.”

“Are you worried about me?”

“If you protect our cult well, won’t I be able to live this kind of life?”

As Mabul set out to search for herbs again after the brief rest, I shouted to him.

“Please count just one of these as found by me! That way I can brag too! Please!”

Pretending not to hear, Mabul bent his waist and went deeper in.

* * *

When we returned after gathering the herbs, Sobaekta was waiting for Geom Mugeuk at the Cult Leader’s hall.

“I have something to tell the Young Cult Leader, which is why I waited.”

With Elder Seong having returned safely as well, he wore a bright expression. Compared to when I had first met him, the difference was so stark he could have been a different person. This must have been his true self all along.

Sobaekta slowly looked around the Cult Leader’s hall.

“I could feel your master’s confusion when he first saw this place. He must have been surprised because I had it completely renovated. In truth, I aimed for that too.
