Separate from having lived longer, and separate from what kind of life I had lived, there exists a different kind of strength in being a father.

I want to become a father like my father.

Though to others I spout all manner of words—flattery, banter, and jokes—I want to be the kind of father who shows it silently through action only to his son. Though I had never desired children in my life, at this moment, I feel greed.

Father stopped ahead and looked back at me. At his gaze asking if I wasn't coming, I strode toward him.

"I'll visit you every day, massage your shoulders, play Baduk, and give you plenty of pocket money. And... let's go together! I wondered who the Asuras took after, leaving like that. Father!"

* * *

When I arrived at the Asura of Extreme Evil's quarters, he was sitting alone in a snow-white room. Even though it was his birthday, he was like that.

"Lord Asura!"

"Young Cult Lord!"

The two eyes within the eye holes of the Asura of Extreme Evil, who had turned toward me, were smiling widely. Was it empathy through gaze alone? Just looking at those eyes of the Asura made me emotional.

"I saw you standing on the stone platform yesterday."

"I wanted to greet you from afar, thinking you must be tired from the long journey. How did it go?"

"I handled it well and returned. With the Wind Heaven Cult Leader and the Demon Buddha there, nothing could go wrong even if they tried."

Perhaps the Asura of Extreme Evil regretted not being able to fight alongside me.

"Oh, happy birthday!"

"Thank you."

"If I had arrived two days later, I would have missed it."

"It's not such a big deal."

"It's an important day. The day you were born."

Then the Asura said something unexpected.

"Actually, I dislike celebrating my birthday."

"But outside, it's a festival?"

The Asura walked to the window and looked outside. I stood beside him. The area around the Asura's quarters was decorated even more splendidly. Faceless Guests had gathered in front, making a commotion as if it were their own birthday.

"We didn't used to, but I told them to enjoy themselves to their hearts' content today under the pretext of my birthday. I also told them to share the gifts that arrived from outside."

The Asura of Extreme Evil was showing a changed side from before as well.

I took out what I had brought from my bosom and said.

"But this gift cannot be shared."

The Asura recognized the gift I handed him at a glance.

"This is!"

His two eyes trembled.

"It was gathering dust in the warehouse, so I brought it thinking you might need it."

What I had taken from the Heavenly Demon Treasury was an armguard worn on the wrist. Its value was apparent at a glance; on one side was embroidered the image of a black dragon roaring, and on the other, a tiger soaring into the air.

The Black Dragon Soaring Tiger Armguard.

Known to be invulnerable not only to blades and swords but also to sword qi, this item was counted among the finest wrist guards. Especially for one like the Asura of Extreme Evil, who did not use swords or blades, it was a treasure absolutely necessary for defense in urgent situations.

For a moment, the Asura's gaze as he looked down at it deepened.

"No one in the world would call the Heavenly Demon Treasury a warehouse."

The Asura looked at me.

"Do you know how precious this is?"

"I know that something so precious is gathering dust in the treasury."

The Asura stared at me and spoke.

"Didn't you say relationships last longer when they're light as a feather? Didn't you say you didn't want to build an impregnable fortress with me? Didn't you say you wanted our relationship to be as light as a feather, comfortable until death? If you do this, what will you do for my next birthday? And what must I do for you in return?"

I looked at the Asura of Extreme Evil in silence for a moment.

"Can you not think of it this way? In my previous life, that Young Cult Lord committed a sin against me, and in this life, he is trying to repay it."

"But you already repaid it when you saved my life."

"Then perhaps I committed two sins."

"Young Cult Lord!"

"Lord Asura!"

If I were to fall into danger, he would be the first to run to me anyway. Someone who would gladly throw away his life.

Therefore, there was no need to worry that this gift would burden him. Whether given or not, he would act the same.

"Please accept it, Lord Asura."

Our gazes intertwined in the air. In the end, he could not refuse my earnest wish.

"Very well."

“Ah, what a relief. If you had refused to the end, my father would have teased me about this for the rest of my life.”

Geugak Soma rolled up his sleeves and put the Black Dragon Flying Tiger Gauntlets on both hands. They suited Geugak Soma, with his white mask, far too well.

“This is the most precious birthday gift I have ever received.”

“If anyone happens to break the record, please be sure to tell me. I can’t stand coming in second, you see.”

His eyes, deep within the mask, conveyed his gratitude.

“What will you do if the other Demon Venerables find out?”

“They’ll look forward to what I’ll give them on their own birthdays.”

“And if you can’t give them anything?”

“They’ll think I must like Soma-nim the best.”

Geugak Soma laughed out loud.

It was Soma’s pleasant laughter, heard for the first time in a long while.

* * *

After leaving the Valley of Villains, I went straight to Maga Village.

I meant to go to Pungryu Tavern, both to see Jo Chunbae and to have a meal.

Maga Village, which I had returned to after so long, was no different from when I had left.

The bustling marketplace was crowded with people coming and going.

Peddlers calling for customers, an old man pulling a cart and shouting for people to move aside, women browsing goods, men grabbing each other by the collars, lovers walking hand in hand with smiles on their faces, children running about, and even housewives shouting at them not to run.

After returning from a life-or-death battle, this scene certainly looked different.

Only by realizing that the everyday life that seemed so natural might not be natural at all could one find happiness within it.

When I arrived at Pungryu Tavern, I was so surprised that I stood there blankly for a moment.

The doors of Pungryu Tavern were closed. It was the first time. The first time this place had ever closed its doors. Was he ill? Or had something happened?

Just then, someone came up and spoke.

“Young Cult Leader, I sell fruit down there.”

He was someone I had often seen while coming and going through Maga Village. He was also close to Jo Chunbae.

“The owner of Pungryu Tavern left with his wife a few days ago, saying something had happened at his in-laws’ home.”

I knew that Jo Chunbae and his wife shared a particularly deep bond.

“Do you happen to know what happened?”

“I didn’t hear the details, but from what I caught, it seemed someone in her family had gotten entangled with a martial artist.”

At the words entangled with a martial artist, my heart sank coldly. Most of the time, such things did not end well. On top of that, the fact that he had closed the tavern and left in such haste meant the situation was that urgent.

Of all times, it had happened while I was away from the cult. Ian had also gone far away, so Seo Daeryong and Jang Ho probably had not been holding their tavern gatherings lately either.

“Do you know where his in-laws live?”

After hearing the location of his in-laws’ home, I immediately launched myself forward.

With Swift Steps, I left Maga Village in an instant. The best outcome would be for me to arrive first, and the next best would be to arrive before it was too late.

I hoped it was nothing serious, but if he had been caught up in something dangerous, what I had to trust in was the tavern owner’s experience. After all, he had gone through all manner of things while running a tavern here.

“Tavern owner, just hold on until I get there!”

Swift Steps, pushed to the extreme, began to surpass its limits once again.

Absolute Regression, Chapter 335

Chapter 335: I Run a Tavern in Front of the Demonic Cult
--------------------------------------------------------
When they were nearly home, Yang Ren caught hold of Jo Chun-bae’s sleeve.

“Dear.”

“What is it, wife?”

“You just go back. I’ll handle this matter. Go back and tend to the business.”

He had married into a poor household with only a widowed mother and two daughters, and had taken on the role of a son. Many things had happened in the family over the years. Each time, her husband had stepped forward to settle them. It was Jo Chun-bae who had even arranged her younger sister’s marriage.

But they had never once faced a matter involving a martial artist like this. She could not shake off her unease. She was afraid something might happen to her husband.

“How do you intend to handle it? Stop saying useless things and let’s go.”

Jo Chun-bae resumed walking.

“It’s been a long time since I last came here. I should have visited more often.”

With the tavern to run, visiting his mother-in-law had never been as easy as he wished. Fortunately, his sister-in-law and her husband lived near his mother-in-law’s house, so he had done what he could as a dutiful son by sending them money for her living expenses.

When the two arrived at the house, their mother-in-law, Madam Yang, hurried out to greet Jo Chun-bae.

“Son-in-law Jo!”

Her old hand, little more than skin and bone, looked pitiful.

“It must have been hard getting here.”

She always looked after her son-in-law before her own daughter. She had been that way ever since the day they married. It was also her own particular way of loving her daughter.

“Mother!”

Yang Ren embraced Madam Yang. In the time they had been apart, her mother had grown far too thin.

Her younger sister, Yang Shan, was at the house. Her eyes were so swollen from crying that she was almost unrecognizable.

“Sister-in-law.”

“Brother-in-law.”

The moment she saw Jo Chun-bae and Yang Ren, she burst into tears.

Yang Ren went over and embraced her younger sister.

They had heard the general details through a messenger. Yang Shan’s husband, Jong Hak, worked as a porter for the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency. He had apparently been caught stealing goods during an escort mission.

“What about Jong Hak?”

“He’s being held at the escort agency right now. The escort warrior said that they would cut off both his hands in front of everyone…”

Yang Shan could not finish speaking and burst into tears.

Jo Chun-bae and Yang Ren were shocked.

“They’re going to cut off his hands?”

Jo Chun-bae had known that this was no ordinary matter before coming.

Escort agencies punished insiders who stole escort goods with excessive severity. There were already plenty of bandits outside; if people within the agency began laying hands on the goods as well, they would be unable to cope.

That was why he had closed the tavern and hurried here without stopping.

But they intended to cut off both his hands? It was the harshest punishment of all. What on earth had he tried to steal?

“Sister, Brother-in-law, you know he isn’t the kind of person who would steal someone else’s belongings, don’t you? Someone framed him.”

They knew. The Jong Hak they knew would never do such a thing. There had to be some other circumstance behind it.

Desperate, Yang Shan pleaded with her brother-in-law.

“Brother-in-law, you run a tavern in front of the Demon Cult, don’t you? Don’t you know anyone in the Demon Cult? If someone from the Demon Cult stepped forward, couldn’t they clear my husband’s name?”

Someone he knew? He knew many. He knew the Young Cult Master, the Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion, the Demon Lord, the Chief of the Ghost Shadow Unit, and even the Demon Venerables. He even knew the Heavenly Demon.

But there was no one among them who could help with this matter. They were merely honored guests he had come to know through the Young Cult Master. Ordinarily, they were people he could not even meet eyes with.

The person he would feel most comfortable asking for help was Geom Mugeuk. He was an extraordinarily special person, after all. But Geom Mugeuk had gone beyond the frontier and was not there.

Nor could he ask the low-ranking demons who frequented the Pungnyu Tavern to accompany him on the several-day journey to his hometown and resolve the matter. He was not close enough to any of them for that. And even if one of them were willing to help, this was not something a low-ranking demon could resolve.

When her husband fell deep into thought, Yang Ren scolded her younger sister.

“What are you talking about? Asking whether he knows someone? What are you going to do if this makes the matter even bigger?”

“No, it’s nothing. I’m sorry, Sister. I only said it because I was frustrated. Don’t worry about it, Brother-in-law.”

“It’s all right, sister-in-law. Anyone would feel the same.”

Who could blame her for saying such things at a time like this?

Jo Chun-bae rose from his seat.

“I’ll go to the escort agency.”

Madam Yang clasped Jo Chun-bae’s hand and shed tears.

“I’m only causing you trouble.”

“Please don’t say that. It concerns your son-in-law. Wait here. I’ll settle it and come back.”

Jo Chun-bae went outside. Yang Ren followed him.

“I’ll go with you, dear.”

“It’s all right. Wait here.”

“I’m sorry.”

“Stop saying you’re sorry.”

If he had to speak of someone being sorry, he was more sorry that she had spent her entire life suffering after meeting him. She was the wife who had trusted him and endured hardship at his side even when he had not possessed a single copper coin.

“Be careful. Don’t overdo it, no matter what. Jong Hak is important, but you’re more important, all right?”

It pained her to say such a thing about her younger sister, but those were her true feelings. She could not even imagine a life without her husband.

* * *

Jo Chun-bae approached the escort warrior guarding the entrance to the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency and bowed politely.

“Greetings.”

“Who are you?”

“I’m Jong Hak’s brother-in-law.”

The escort warrior’s expression stiffened slightly, indicating that he knew Jong Hak.

“You can’t see him right now.”

“I know. It must be hard standing guard in this cold. Here, please accept this first.”

He handed the man food and liquor wrapped in paper. He had stopped by the tavern to purchase them on his way here.

“I can’t accept something like this.”

The delicious aroma pierced the air. Jo Chun-bae had bought the dishes most favored by martial artists at the tavern.

“Keep them for when your shift ends, then go inside and enjoy them. I’ll leave them behind here.”

Jo Chun-bae placed the liquor and food behind a pillar. Knowing martial artists well, he acted smoothly, leaving the man no chance to refuse.

“I’d be grateful if you could just tell me whether he’s safe. We’re family. I need to know whether he’s alive or dead.”

Faced with Jo Chun-bae’s earnest plea, the martial artist could not bring himself to refuse that much.

“He isn’t dead yet, so don’t worry.”

“I heard they’re going to cut off his hands.”

Having both hands cut off was little different from being killed. Unless the wounds were quickly staunched and treated on the spot, he would die on the way to a physician. Even if he survived by some miracle, what would become of the rest of his life?

“If he committed a crime, he must be punished.”

“That’s right, of course. Then when will the punishment be carried out?”

“In three shichen. We’ll call the villagers and carry it out in front of them.”

Three shichen? Jo Chun-bae’s vision went dark. There was far too little time.

“Now, you should leave.”

“Escort warrior, please let me see his face at least once.”

Jo Chun-bae discreetly showed the draft he had prepared inside his sleeve. It was a thirty-tael draft.

It was far too much money merely to show a man’s face for a moment. Jo Chun-bae knew that if one intended to use a bribe, it had to be an amount difficult to refuse for it to have any effect.

“There are other people guarding the place besides me.”

He meant that the money was not enough.

“If you make this possible, I’ll give ten taels to the men guarding the place, and thirty taels to you. Please, let me see his face.”

The escort warrior opened the gate and briefly looked inside.

“Follow me. Give me the money first.”

“I’ll give it to you once we get there.”

The escort warrior looked displeased, but greed had already won him over.

Jo Chun-bae followed him inside.

After speaking with the escort warrior guarding the front of the warehouse, he beckoned to Jo Chun-bae, who was standing behind a tree.

“Only for a very brief moment.”

Jo Chun-bae handed him the forty taels he had brought, then entered the building.

Jong Hak lay there, bound tightly. His face and body were a mess, as though he had been tortured.

Even when Jo Chun-bae shook him awake, he did not regain consciousness easily.

“Brother-in-law.”

After being shaken awake several times, Jong Hak finally opened his eyes.

“Brother-in-law? Brother-in-law!”

“Are you all right?”

“Brother-in-law!”

Tears streamed from his two eyes, filled with injustice.

“I didn’t steal anything. I was framed!”

When his voice began to rise, Jo Chun-bae covered his mouth.

“Shh! But why would they frame you?”

“I saw something I shouldn’t have seen.”

Just as he was about to say more, the escort warrior entered and dragged Jo Chun-bae away.

“You have to go. The man taking over my shift is coming.”

He followed the escort warrior back to the entrance of the escort agency.

“If you breathe a word about what happened today, you’ll die.”

It was a threat not to reveal that he had given a bribe.

“Now get out of here.”

Jo Chun-bae left the escort agency.

His heart was heavy. If Jong Hak had really stolen something, he might at least have been able to prevent the severing of his hands by spending money on a bribe as he had just done. But what if he had been caught up in a conspiracy?

Jo Chun-bae hurriedly hired a carriage and went somewhere.

It was a branch of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult about an hour away from his in-laws’ home by carriage.

It was a small branch office, but standing at the entrance was a demon with a far more menacing appearance than the man who had guarded the escort agency.

The demon swept his cold gaze up and down Jo Chun-bae’s body. Before he could ask anything, Jo Chun-bae respectfully introduced himself.

“I am Jo Chun-bae, the owner of a tavern in front of the main altar of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

“And?”

“I wish to meet the branch master.”

“For what reason?”

“There is something I must tell him in person…”

Swish.

Without saying another word, the demon drew his sword.

“Good heavens!”

Jo Chun-bae cried out. He thought the man was going to stab him, but fortunately, the blade merely came to rest against his throat.

“What business does a tavern owner have with the branch master?”

“My younger sister’s husband has suffered an injustice. I wish to ask the branch master for help.”

“You’re insane. Aren’t you going to get lost?”

The words I was sent here by the Young Cult Master rose to Jo Chun-bae’s throat, but he could not bring himself to say them. No matter what, he could not dare to trade on the name of the Demon Cult’s Young Cult Master. Even if Geom Mugeuk forgave him, the other demons would not.

He considered giving the money he had prepared, but judging by the atmosphere, it was obvious that they would take his money and still refuse to let him see the branch master.

Yes, this was normal. This was the relationship between a tavern owner and a demon—being ignored was the least of it, and the demon could even threaten him by pressing a blade to his throat. It was only because the Young Cult Master was a peerless figure that he had been able to become the owner of a special tavern.

Jo Chun-bae returned home.

Yang Ren, who had been waiting anxiously in the yard, ran over. She was relieved that her husband had returned safely, but he had brought devastating news with him.

“They’ll cut off Jong Hak’s hands in an hour.”

Yang Ren’s heart already ached at the thought of how much her mother and younger sister would suffer.

Jo Chun-bae silently embraced his sobbing wife. She did not see the resolve that flickered in his eyes.

* * *

People gathered at the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency.

The escort agency intended to cut off Jong Hak’s hands in front of the villagers. It was the established law of the escort agency.

Since Jong Hak’s Blood-Sealing Acupoint and other acupoints had been sealed, the way he was dragged out obediently made it appear as though he was meekly admitting his crime.

The person overseeing the proceedings was Escort Chief Kong Can.

“Will you admit your crime?”

With his acupoints sealed, Jong Hak could neither answer nor shake his head.

But the people did not know that.

Among the murmuring crowd stood Jo Chun-bae’s family. They had paid a physician to bring him along. Even if Jong Hak’s hands were cut off, they intended to treat him somehow on the spot and at least save his life.

“Since he has admitted his crime, we will cut off both his hands as a warning to all!”

At the cry, Madam Yang staggered as though she might collapse, while Yang Shan wailed.

“Husband! Husband!”

Jong Hak looked at his wife with eyes filled with injustice and sorrow. He had never committed a crime in his life. He had never imagined that he would be framed like this and lose his hands. Evil always happened in an instant.

Just as Kong Can drew his sword to cut off Jong Hak’s hands, Jo Chun-bae stepped forward.

“I am that man’s brother-in-law.”

Everyone’s eyes focused on him.

“We have heard nothing at all about this incident. I believe you should at least explain what happened.”

Yang Ren, as well as Madam Yang and Yang Shan, was startled when Jo Chun-bae stepped forward. They were afraid that he, too, might suffer for it.

“The investigation has already been completed. He tried to steal escort goods, and he is receiving the punishment for it.”

Kong Can cast him a threatening look that clearly said, Do you want to be taught a lesson as well? But Jo Chun-bae had resolved to save his brother-in-law somehow.

“What exactly was he caught stealing? It isn’t as though the stolen goods cannot be recovered. Cutting off both his hands merely because he was caught trying to steal something is far too harsh, in my opinion.”

Yang Ren’s hand, clutching Jo Chun-bae’s arm, trembled violently.

“Harsh treatment?”

Gong Chan’s gaze turned fierce.

Jo Chunbae was not frightened. What kind of eyes had he seen at the tavern of pleasure?

Jo Chunbae instinctively realized that reasoning with his opponent based on his reaction would not solve anything. In the end, there was only one method left to him.

“Even the Demonic Cult does not handle matters this way.”

At the mention of the Demonic Cult, the surroundings fell silent.

“I know masters from the Demonic Cult. Please bear with this until they come and mediate.”

Gong Chan’s expression had completely hardened. The Demonic Cult carried a sense of terror and oppression. If one became entangled with them, an entire escort bureau could be massacred without a trace.

“Who in the Demonic Cult do you know?”

“I know the Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion.”

In an instant, the surroundings froze. The residents might not know, but the escorts knew exactly what position the Pavilion Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion held.

“I also know the Demon Lord.”

At the mention of even the Demon Lord, the surroundings fell silent. He did not bring up the Demon Venerable or the Young Cult Master. If he went too far, it would have no effect.

Just then, an escort who knew about Jonghak’s family history came over and whispered into Gong Chan’s ear.

“That man runs a tavern near the Demonic Cult.”

“A tavern?”

“It’s a shabby, small-scale tavern.”

A mocking smile flitted across Gong Chan’s face.

“You merely sell liquor, yet you dare use the Demonic Cult to threaten us?”

Gong Chan was angry and humiliated at the realization that he had been frightened for a moment.

“If I send word, they will come.”

But the moment he heard that Jo Chunbae was merely a tavern owner, Gong Chan stopped listening to him altogether.

“Drag him over here!”

Then his sister-in-law, Yang Seon, cried out.

“No! My brother-in-law only said that because he wanted to save my husband! Please forgive him!”

Yang Seon was seized by terror, afraid that her brother-in-law would be dragged away and harmed as well.

In the end, Jo Chunbae was dragged out too.

With his blood paths subdued, Jonghak could not even move his head. All he could do was watch the situation with a devastated heart.

The man who had made him happiest after he married was Jo Chunbae. He had no idea how reassured he had felt knowing that man was there. He was terribly sorry that Jo Chunbae had to suffer because of him.

“You threatened this escort bureau by hiding behind the Demonic Cult, so I will cut out your tongue.”

Yang In and Yang Seon shouted.

“No!”

“Please, forgive him!”

Madam Yun threw herself to the ground and pleaded.

“My son-in-law—please forgive him just this once! Punish this old woman in his place!”

Trembling with fear, Jo Chunbae spoke to Gong Chan.

“Give me time to confirm what I said. For the sake of the Yellow Dragon Escort Bureau as well, do not act in haste.”

Gong Chan roared.

“Shut up! You vile bastard, still trying to threaten us to the very end. I’ll cut out your tongue first.”

“If you lay a hand on me, the Young Cult Master of the Demonic Cult will not let you off!”

He had hoped to frighten Gong Chan somehow, but it only had the opposite effect.

“The Young Cult Master of the Demonic Cult? You really are completely insane. Let’s say, for argument’s sake, that you do know the Young Cult Master. Do you truly believe that the Young Cult Master of the Demonic Cult would come all the way here to help you?”

He shouted as he said this, but it was useless. The escorts seized Jo Chunbae by both arms. They truly intended to cut out his tongue.

That was when something pure white descended onto Jo Chunbae’s shoulder.

He raised his head and saw that snow had begun to fall. Everyone looked up at the snow.

Why did it have to snow at a time like this?

They said the fate of man was such that one could not see even an inch ahead. He had never imagined that his tongue would be cut here at the Yellow Dragon Escort Bureau. But he did not regret it. He could not simply stand by and watch that kind-hearted brother-in-law have his hand severed unjustly.

Still, he could not help being afraid. Would he survive if his tongue were cut out? He would have to stop the bleeding to live.

In the distance, he saw the face of his wailing wife.

I’m sorry. I’m so sorry that this husband of yours is powerless.

Behind his wife, he saw Madam Yun and Yang Seon sobbing.

Mother-in-law, I’m sorry. Sister-in-law, I’m sorry.

Just as Gong Chan was about to put his hand inside Jo Chunbae’s mouth—

Whoooosh.

There came the sound of a fierce wind, and then—

Bam!

Something that had flown down from the sky crashed into the training grounds as if it had been hurled there.

People screamed, while the escort chief and escorts drew their swords, throwing the training grounds into chaos.

Everyone’s eyes turned toward that spot.

What had flown in was a person. He lay spread-eagled in the middle of the training grounds, panting for breath. His hair was disheveled, and his entire body was drenched in sweat. The only sound in the courtyard was his rough breathing.

When Jo Chunbae recognized who he was, he was stunned. A smile slowly spread across his bewildered face, and soon he was beaming.

Then his emotions surged, and his eyes filled with tears. He clenched his teeth, afraid he would burst into tears, but a sob escaped him anyway.

With everyone watching, the man lying on the training grounds gazed up at the sky and said,

“Master, the first snow is falling.”

The Unrivaled Regressor

Episode 336

Chapter 336: I Like the Food My Son-in-Law Makes.
-------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk lay there for a while, gazing up at the falling snow.

Except for the time he had spent replenishing his inner energy, he had run all the way here without resting for even a moment.

Thanks to that, his Swift Footwork had broken through the limits of the tenth-stage mastery and entered the eleventh stage. He had taken his first step toward the twelfth-stage mastery. Just how fast would Swift Footwork be once he achieved the twelfth stage?

Of course, what pleased him even more was the fact that he had arrived in time. Geom Mugeuk thanked the heavens. Thank you. He also thanked Jo Chunbae. Thank you for holding on so well.

As though answering his gratitude, the snow continued to fall softly upon Geom Mugeuk’s face, hands, and body.

After lying there for a moment and gazing up at the falling snow, Geom Mugeuk rose to his feet.

Even then, everyone there was staring at Geom Mugeuk, who had suddenly fallen from the sky.

Geom Mugeuk smoothed his disheveled hair. Everyone wore surprised expressions at the sight of his younger-than-expected face.

Geom Mugeuk looked toward Jo Chunbae. Tears were streaming down his cheeks.

‘That man is the standard for my demonic path. When tears no longer flow from that man’s face, I will know that my demonic path is being properly established.’

Jo Chunbae could barely get the words out.

“How… how?”

He truly had never even imagined that Geom Mugeuk would appear here.

Looking at him, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“I came to see the owner.”

The moment he heard those words, Jo Chunbae was overcome with emotion once more.

“I went to have a drink, but you had already closed.”

Jo Chunbae was deeply moved by the fact that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had rushed all the way here for his sake.

‘Young Cult Leader!’

He could not think of what to say. He was simply grateful—so very grateful. He did not need to say anything. His tears and expression were already answer enough.

Gong Chan, the escort captain who had been about to cut out Jo Chunbae’s tongue, stepped forward.

“Who might you be, young hero?”

If he was acquainted with the tavern owner, then he ought to be insignificant. Yet the young man’s appearance had been far too dramatic.

At first, they had thought he had fallen from the sky. The impact should have badly injured even a master, but he looked perfectly fine on the outside. How was that possible?

The problem was that the young man did not appear to have practiced martial arts at all. He could not possibly have reached the Returning to Simplicity realm at such a young age.

Rather than answer Gong Chan’s question, Geom Mugeuk walked over to Yun, Jo Chunbae’s mother-in-law, who was lying facedown on the ground.

“Please rise, Mother. Your sons-in-law will be all right now.”

Geom Mugeuk helped Yun to her feet, then helped Yangin and Yangseon up as well.

“Allow me to introduce myself properly. I am Geom Mugeuk, Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Silence descended upon the place.

The instant Gong Chan heard those words, he was so shocked that he nearly stopped breathing. The escorts around him were too stunned to say anything.

Clang.

One of the escorts dropped his sword in his astonishment, but no one paid him any attention.

If Jo Chunbae had not previously said that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult would never let them off, they would all have thought they had misheard or that the young man was insane.

Yet the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult whom Jo Chunbae had spoken of had truly dropped out of the sky.

Naturally, Gong Chan did not believe him.

“You’re lying! There’s no way the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult would come here alone looking like that!”

His clothes were filthy with dust, his entire body was drenched in sweat, and even his hair was disheveled. No one would have called him the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult at a glance.

Geom Mugeuk explained himself to the three women.

“I ask for your understanding regarding my disheveled appearance. I heard that the family of someone I love and respect had fallen into danger, so I rushed here as quickly as I could.”

As though the person he loved and respected was none other than that man, he looked toward Jo Chunbae.

Ordinarily, he would have handled the matter without revealing his identity.

But he had revealed himself today for Jo Chunbae’s sake. He wanted to show his family that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had rushed here like this to save him. He wanted to show his wife, his mother-in-law, his sister-in-law, and his brother-in-law. He wanted to show them all.

It was Geom Mugeuk’s consideration and courtesy toward Jo Chunbae, who had always welcomed him with the same unchanging warmth.

Because Jo Chunbae understood his feelings, he clenched his molars once more and restrained the emotions surging within him.

“Mother, I like the food and liquor my brother-in-law makes more than anything else. Even my father, the Heavenly Demon of our cult, goes there to eat his food and drink his liquor. He is truly precious to me—and to our cult as well.”

Yun was so shocked and moved that she did not know what to do. She looked once at her son-in-law, then once at Geom Mugeuk. She looked once at her daughter, then once again at her son-in-law.

Not knowing what else to say, she could only repeat, “Such an honored person, such an honored person.”

In truth, she did not know what it meant for someone to be the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. She merely sensed that he must be an extraordinarily important person.

Jo Chunbae’s wife, Yangin, on the other hand, was genuinely astonished.

She had heard her husband talk a great deal about the Young Cult Leader. But deep down, she had thought he was exaggerating. The Young Cult Leader may indeed have come to the tavern, but was he really that close to her husband?

Her husband had said that the Young Cult Leader treated him like a friend. No way, surely? Yet that very impossibility had now become reality before her eyes.

“We pay our respects to the Young Cult Leader.”

Yangin tried to prostrate herself again, but an invisible force lifted her back to her feet.

Her younger sister, Yangseon, pleaded,

“Please save us. Please, save my husband and my brother-in-law.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled to reassure the three women, then turned back toward Jo Chunbae. Though he had been speaking with them, Geom Mugeuk had released his qi in several directions, allowing him to perceive even the slightest movements behind him.

Until then, two escorts had been gripping Jo Chunbae by both arms, while Gong Chan held a dagger in his hand. They were confused, wondering whether he truly was the Young Cult Leader.

“He’s a fake!”

Gong Chan came to his own conclusion. There was no doubt that Jo Chunbae and this young man had plotted this scheme to save Jonghak. He must have fallen so clumsily into the training yard because he lacked the skill to land properly. Now that he thought about it, everything fit.

“This was supposed to end with only your tongue and hands being cut off, but now it looks like your head will be cut off too.”

He looked as if he might stab Jo Chunbae in the neck at any moment, and the three women screamed.

Geom Mugeuk thrust both hands forward at once.

First came the astonishing result produced by his left hand.

Whoooosh!

The two escorts holding Jo Chunbae were sent flying backward. A fierce, invisible force had blown them away.

It looked as though they had simply been flung aside, but it was in fact a move that displayed the pinnacle of martial arts. In that brief instant, their hands gripping Jo Chunbae had been forcibly opened, leaving Jo Chunbae exactly where he was while sending only the two escorts flying. Even the two escorts rose without suffering a single injury.

His right hand displayed an even more astonishing feat than his left.

Ting!

A sharp metallic sound rang out.

Everyone’s eyes turned toward the dagger in Gong Chan’s hand. The dagger had broken.

Gong Chan started violently and threw the broken dagger onto the ground.

Yes, he could understand using palm force to send the escorts flying. But breaking a dagger through Airborne Object Control?

Others might have assumed that he had thrown a concealed weapon to break it, but Gong Chan alone knew the truth. Nothing had flown through the air. The dagger had simply broken.

When Gong Chan, who had been distracted by the broken dagger, raised his head, Geom Mugeuk was already standing right in front of him. The instant their eyes met, an inexplicable terror overwhelmed him, and his body froze.

Only then did he understand.

‘He really is the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult!’

Even so, he still could not comprehend it. The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had personally come to save a tavern owner? Looking like this? Alone? Why in the world?

When Geom Mugeuk took another step forward, Gong Chan shrieked in fright.

“Aaagh!”

Geom Mugeuk merely lightly subdued his blood points. He neither beat him nor tried to intimidate him. Once the truth of this matter came to light, only those who would live and those who would die would remain. He already had a feeling which side Gong Chan belonged to.

“Did you not hear me? I am the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Geom Mugeuk’s manner was calm and courteous. It was not out of consideration for Gong Chan, but for Yun, Jo Chunbae’s mother-in-law, and for his wife and sister-in-law, Yangin and Yangseon.

Geom Mugeuk looked around. Though he had not released any demonic qi, the overwhelming presence he possessed caused the escorts’ hearts to shrink.

The escorts threw their swords onto the ground. Then they lowered their heads, not daring to meet his gaze. Even if he was a fake Young Cult Leader, it did not matter. They could not bring themselves to challenge someone with such skill.

Only then did Yun, Yangin, and Yangseon truly realize that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had come.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the nearby escorts.

“Bring us five comfortable chairs.”

Without a word, the escorts hurried off to fetch them.

Geom Mugeuk released the acupoints sealing Jonghak’s voice and movement.

“Thank you. Thank you so much.”

As Jonghak expressed his gratitude, Geom Mugeuk smiled.

“Thank your elder brother instead.”

Jonghak’s heated gaze turned toward Jo Chunbae. Even before the Young Cult Leader had arrived, Jo Chunbae had stepped forward to save him.

“I truly made the right choice in this marriage.”

At Jonghak’s words, Jo Chunbae and his family laughed.

After speaking, Jonghak coughed painfully.

Geom Mugeuk quickly seated him, placed a palm against his chest, and infused a thread of inner energy into him.

His body had been badly damaged by the brutal beating, but fortunately, none of his injuries would leave him crippled. With some time to recuperate, he would regain his health.

After soothing his internal injuries, Geom Mugeuk led him over to where his wife was standing.

“Darling!”

“I thought you were going to die!”

“I’m sorry.”

“No, it’s all right. Thank goodness. Thank goodness.”

The family embraced Jonghak in one place and wept.

All the villagers watched the scene. Geom Mugeuk had deliberately not sent them away. They needed to witness Jonghak clear his name. Even after he left, these people would have to continue living here.

That was why Geom Mugeuk handled the matter with care. Without allowing emotion to take the lead, he proceeded calmly, steadily, and decisively.

Among the escorts watching were some who knew the character of Jonghak, the porter. They found the situation strange. It was strange that Jonghak would have tampered with the escort goods, and they also felt that the punishment of cutting off both his hands was far too severe.

In the meantime, the escorts returned with the chairs.

They arranged the chairs side by side and seated Jo Chunbae and his family.

“Please sit comfortably here.”

“I’m fine.”

“No, please sit down and make yourself comfortable.”

He seated Yun in the most comfortable chair, then had the rest of the family sit as well.

Once Jo Chunbae sat down, his heart began to pound with a different kind of tension.

Just then, his wife Yangin, seated beside him, quietly took his hand. Her hand was trembling as well. Jo Chunbae struggled to calm his own fear. The people he had to protect were beside him.

Yangin mouthed the words,

‘You were wonderful, dear.’

Jo Chunbae gripped her hand more tightly. He was glad that his life had been saved, but he was even happier that he had been able to protect his family. He was also deeply pleased to have earned the approval of his wife and her family.

‘Thank you, Young Cult Leader.’

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Jo Chunbae.

“Now, let us hear what happened.”

Jo Chunbae calmly explained the situation.

“My younger brother-in-law works as a porter for the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency here. During this escort journey, he was accused of being a thief and nearly had both hands cut off.”

Jo Chunbae told him everything he had heard when he met Jonghak. Geom Mugeuk was the sort of person who could predict ten things from hearing one, so he understood the gist even from the rough explanation.

“So it was not because your younger brother-in-law tampered with the escort goods. This whole mess began because he happened to see something he should not have seen.”

Geom Mugeuk asked Jonghak,

“What did you see?”

Jonghak looked at Gong Chan and said,

“By chance, I saw Chief Escort Gong secretly meeting some man. The look in his eyes when he saw me was so sharp that it frightened me. I thought the matter had passed, but after we returned from the escort journey, I had become a thief. No matter how I think about it, that is the only reason I could have been framed.”

“Did you know the man Chief Escort Gong met?”

“No. I had never seen him before. But he had a large mole on his forehead.”

That mole must have been the root of the trouble. The story had not become that he had met someone, but rather that he had met someone with a mole.

Everyone’s eyes turned toward Gong Chan. He could do nothing but glare at Jonghak with a hardened expression. He was regretting it.

‘I should have disguised it as an accident and killed him there.’

The reason he had been unable to do so was that he had been required to report to someone within the escort bureau. They had told him never to handle even the most trivial matter alone, but to report it.

What happened afterward had also been a problem.

He had suggested making it look as though a band of bandits had invaded and killing the entire family. But Jonghak had said that if they were going to kill them anyway, they should make it serve the escort bureau’s interests. If they cut off his hands and failed to stop the bleeding, he would die in no time. That way, they could both reinforce discipline within the escort bureau and kill the man. He had thought it would kill two birds with one stone.

Geom Mugeuk approached Gong Chan and asked,

“Why did the matter of you meeting someone become grounds for cutting that gentleman’s hands? Who on earth was it? To be honest with me, and I’ll spare your life.”

His unspoken words were: I’ll still cut off both your hands and your tongue.

Since he did not know what had been omitted, Gong Chan’s eyes wavered.

It was at that very moment.

The door of the escort bureau opened, and someone stepped outside.

“Stop for a moment.”

The middle-aged man who appeared had a massive build and an intense gaze. He was none other than U Sochu, chief of the Hwangnyong Escort Bureau. For the chief of a mere escort bureau, he possessed an impressive air of authority.

“I am U Sochu, chief of this bureau.”

He had not stepped forward until now, choosing to appear at this decisive moment.

“A distinguished guest has arrived, so I would invite you inside. Please, come in.”

Geom Mugeuk politely refused.

“I have my people here, so that would be difficult. Let us speak here. It is snowing, and the atmosphere is rather pleasant.”

U Sochu took Geom Mugeuk’s refusal as an act of coercion.

“Even if you are the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, is it acceptable to oppress an escort bureau of the Orthodox faction like this?”

“Oh, is the Hwangnyong Escort Bureau part of the Orthodox faction? Seeing you frame someone and try to cut off his hands, I thought you were from the Unorthodox faction.”

U Sochu’s expression stiffened slightly.

“If you lay a hand on us, the Alliance Leader will never forgive you.”

Geom Mugeuk looked up at him and smiled.

“Have you ever met the Alliance Leader?”

U Sochu could not answer. He had seen the Alliance Leader from a distance while attending a major event. But he had never once met with him alone.

“I thought as much. You must be saying that because you have not. If the Alliance Leader knew that you were trying to cut off the hands and tongue of an innocent man, your heads would already have been smashed to pieces by his hand. Now, stop making empty threats and come down here.”

Geom Mugeuk walked across the snow that had only just begun to pile up, crunching beneath his feet, and went to stand before Chochunbae’s family. He lightly brushed the snow from Jonghak’s shoulder as he spoke.

“You defend your man, and I defend mine. So let us determine who is right and who is wrong here, upon this pure white snow.”

U Sochu continued to look down at Geom Mugeuk from where he stood.

“I have been treating you with courtesy, so you do not seem to understand the situation. The man standing before you is the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, who nearly lost a friend. Therefore…”

Geom Mugeuk, who had remained polite throughout, added in a low, cold voice,

“Come down while I’m asking nicely, U Sochu.”

Absolute Regression — Chapter 337

Chapter 337: Because I Think My Sword Will Hurt Less.
-----------------------------------------------------
How could U Sochu not be confused?

There was only one reason he had hesitated.

The very same question Gong Chan had asked himself.

‘Is this man really the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult?’

Fine. Let us concede a hundred times over that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had come alone. Let us also say that he could have ended up in a miserable state.

But he had rushed here to save the tavern owner and his family? He had remained standing while those lowly people sat in chairs? He had even brushed the snow from the porter’s shoulder?

According to U Sochu’s common sense, such things were absolutely impossible.

‘He isn’t the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. There’s no way.’

He was certain of it.

But he could not refuse the man’s demand. The martial arts he had displayed while subduing Gong Chan earlier had not been fake. U Sochu had been unable to observe the details from inside the building, but he had seen Gong Chan get beaten without being able to land a single blow. That was no ordinary level of skill.

“If the Young Cult Leader tells me to come down, then I must come down.”

As he watched U Sochu slowly descend the stairs, Geom Mugeuk said coldly,

“You shouldn’t come down just because I told you to. You should come down because one of your subordinates has his life hanging in the balance below. Instead of coming down so leisurely, you should have jumped down and checked on your subordinate first.”

Irritation surged within U Sochu.

The words calling him by name and telling him to come down had already been humiliating enough, but so were these words now, which he could not even refute. His anger welled up because the escorts were watching, and even the villagers were looking on.

That was why the five people sitting behind Geom Mugeuk particularly offended him.

‘Worthless trash!’

This was the first time he had ever suffered such humiliation at the hands of his inferiors. Once this matter was over, he would never let them off easily.

The three people other than Jo Chunbae and Yang In could not even dare meet U Sochu’s gaze.

The person who wielded the greatest power in this region was the chief of the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency. Yet right now, they were seated while the chief stood.

“It hurts.”

Yang In spoke to her younger sister. U Sochu’s gaze was so frightening that she had gripped her sister’s hand too tightly.

“Sorry.”

“Seon-a, it’s all right now.”

Though U Sochu was listening, Yang In spoke confidently. Had she not known the truth, she might have been uncertain, but she believed everything was truly all right, because the man who had come was certainly the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.

Yang In looked at Jo Chunbae and asked with her eyes,

‘It really is all right, isn’t it?’

Jo Chunbae nodded firmly, reinforcing his wife’s reassurance.

“Don’t worry, Sister-in-law.”

“Brother-in-law!”

“This is a moment that will never come again in your life, so you may enjoy it.”

Jo Chunbae knew what kind of person the man standing before them and shielding them like a fortress wall was. He also knew that even if the wall crumbled, that back would never buckle.

He was the one who had brought Demon Venerables to his shabby Pungnyu Tavern and made them leave writings on its walls. He was the one who had brought the Heavenly Demon to his tavern.

That was a strength entirely different from martial prowess. A unique Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, one who had never existed before and never would again.

In that sense, Jo Chunbae developed a desire to live a long life. What would the Young Cult Leader look like once he became the Heavenly Demon? What would the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult led by him be like? What would the martial world be like with him as the Heavenly Demon? He wanted to see all of it.

Yang In quietly gazed at her husband.

Her husband had never once spoken about the tavern business. How could she not know how difficult it was to deal with drunkards?

But he had changed ever since the Young Cult Leader came to the tavern. Whenever Geom Mugeuk or a Demon Venerable visited, her husband’s eyes would shine like stars as he excitedly recounted everything that had happened that day. He was looking at Geom Mugeuk with that same expression now. Today, the star was shining brighter than ever.

Geom Mugeuk once again returned to his polite and composed manner. His foremost goal here was to clear Jonghak of the false accusation before exacting punishment.

“Now that you have come down, let us hear everything together. Chief, are you not curious? Why did Escort Leader Gong try to silence an innocent porter by killing him?”

U Sochu immediately retorted,

“He was not trying to kill him. He was trying to cut off the hand that had stolen from us. And what do you mean, ‘silence him by killing him’? A severed wrist does not necessarily mean death, does it?”

Geom Mugeuk was certain that Gong Chan had tried to kill Jonghak.

“Could he leave this place with both hands cut off and still make it alive to a physician? Let us suppose I cut off both your hands. Do you think you could run to a physician without using qinggong and survive? Someone who has never learned martial arts would faint the moment his hands were severed.”

U Sochu could not refute him. The bleeding would be so severe that even he would have difficulty surviving without proper assistance.

The watching villagers turned to one another and began whispering. They had been summoned under the impression that someone had stolen something and that his hands were to be cut off as punishment. Yet a truth they had never anticipated was being revealed.

The escorts had already begun to suspect that there was some conspiracy behind the incident from Gong Chan and U Sochu’s reactions. For the moment, they simply watched. Their opponent was an exceptionally extraordinary demonic practitioner, after all.

Geom Mugeuk said to Gong Chan and U Sochu,

“You tried to kill not only a man’s life, but also his honor.”

Jonghak let out a long sigh. When Geom Mugeuk said those words in front of everyone, it felt as though something tightly lodged in his chest had finally been cleared away. What had tormented him most was being falsely accused of theft. It hurt all the more because he had lived his entire life without ever coveting what belonged to others.

Geom Mugeuk approached and once again brushed the snow from Jonghak’s shoulder.

Madam Yun silently watched the sight before quietly speaking to her eldest daughter.

“He may be young, but he is a man like an elder.”

There was something visible only to the eyes of an old person who had weathered every storm the world had to offer.

In the meantime, Geom Mugeuk was raising a question before U Sochu.

“Chief U, there is something I am curious about. Looking at this man, Escort Leader Gong, he is hot-tempered and cruel. So why did he not kill this man during the escort journey? He certainly has the kind of personality that would do so.”

Gong Chan watched Geom Mugeuk and U Sochu with a face full of fear.

Geom Mugeuk stared directly into Gong Chan’s eyes and asked him,

“Did you perhaps have to return and report to someone?”

Who would an escort leader have to report to? Naturally, everyone’s gaze turned toward U Sochu.

“Was that person Chief U?”

Gong Chan did not answer. His reaction alone already gave them the answer.

“You are not immediately denying it.”

U Sochu quickly spoke up.

“You have not received an admission, either. How can he give a clear answer when you are threatening him as if you intend to kill him?”

Geom Mugeuk could sense that U Sochu had sent Gong Chan a voice transmission at the very moment he was about to answer.

“What did your chief say to you through voice transmission? Did he threaten you with your life? Did he promise you a huge sum of money if you got past this crisis? Did he promise to make you deputy chief escort leader? Or all three?”

Gong Chan stared at Geom Mugeuk with a startled expression.

‘What in the world is this man?’

Amazingly, all three were true.

The person Gong Chan had returned to report to was U Sochu. This entire affair had been carried out entirely under the chief’s orders.

It was also true that the chief had threatened him through voice transmission moments ago. He had threatened to kill Gong Chan with his own hands if he confessed, while promising him a large sum of money and the position of deputy chief escort leader as a reward.

Of course, since the opponent was Geom Mugeuk, there was no way it would work.

“I am sorry to say this, but even if you offered me not ten million gold, but a hundred million, it would be useless. If you do not tell the truth here, you will die by my hand.”

U Sochu tried to persuade Gong Chan.

“If that man really is the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, he absolutely cannot kill you. A demonic practitioner killing the escort leader of an orthodox escort agency? And the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult at that? The martial world would be thrown into an uproar. Trust me.”

In the end, Gong Chan did not open his mouth. If he could only get past this crisis, he would gain both an enormous amount of money and the position of deputy chief escort leader. U Sochu would save him, if only to ensure his own survival.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned cold as demonic energy emerged.

“Indeed. If he were the sort of person who would listen to reason, he would never have done something like this in the first place.”

The demonic energy flew toward Gong Chan alone, ignoring everyone else.

Gong Chan looked around. He was standing in the sea. For a brief moment, he thought it beautiful. Then he began to be dragged beneath the water.

A pitch-black abyss awaited him, and he could see nothing. This was no illusion. It was truly like drowning; he genuinely could not breathe. How could a prayer do this?

The pain was unbearable. He wished he would simply lose consciousness, but Geom Mugeuk’s prayer reflected Geom Mugeuk’s intentions exactly.

The excruciating pain continued. Whenever he was about to die, he was given a few more breaths of air and brought back to consciousness, only to be dragged into the agony of suffocation once more.

‘Please! Please! Just kill me!’

He must have been in such agony that, instead of asking to be spared, his first plea was for death.

Then Geom Mugeuk’s voice reached him.

―Do you want to live?

Willpower? Loyalty? If he could only breathe right now, he would offer even his soul.

‘Yes, please save me!’

―Then tell me the truth.

Gong Chan, who had been gasping for breath, suddenly opened his eyes wide and shouted,

“The one who gave the order was the chief!”

U Sochu could not say a word. He looked as though he were confessing.

But he was not confessing. Without anyone noticing, Geom Mugeuk had subdued his speech-sealing acupoint and his demonic meridian acupoint.

It was exactly the same situation as before, when Geom Mugeuk had subdued those acupoints while Gong Chan was trying to cut off Jonghak’s hand, making it appear as if he were admitting his guilt.

U Sochu was aghast. No matter how powerful his opponent’s martial arts were, it was impossible for his acupoints to be subdued without him even sensing it.

‘He really is the Young Cult Leader!’

Meanwhile, Geom Mugeuk continued questioning Gong Chan.

“Then who was the man with the mole on his forehead?”

“I do not know, either. The chief told me to meet him, so I did. I returned after learning from him when and where to receive the next consignment.”

“Do you know what the consignment was?”

“No.”

“When were you supposed to receive the next consignment?”

“We were to receive it at Cheongsurim in three days.”

Geom Mugeuk asked about something more important.

“Why did you try to cut off Porter Jong’s hands?”

“When we reported that Jonghak had seen our contact, the chief said we should cut off his hands and deal with him.”

It was the moment Jonghak was completely cleared of the false accusation.

The escorts and villagers turned their gazes toward the chief. His face had gone crimson, but he could not say a word.

“Which escort was involved in this affair?”

“Only me. The other escorts merely did as they were told. No, I am the same. I only acted according to the chief’s orders.”

He shifted all the blame onto the chief.

“I have told you everything, so you will spare my life, won’t you?”

Gong Chan was not the only one who waited tensely for the answer. Jo Chunbae’s family behind Geom Mugeuk was even more anxious.

Jonghak, in particular, prayed in his heart, ‘Please, do not spare him!’ He feared that if Gong Chan were left alive, he might seek revenge against him later.

“Why would I?”

When Geom Mugeuk asked in return, Gong Chan raised his voice.

“Didn’t you say you would spare me if I told you everything?”

“You did not speak when I told you to, did you? You only spoke because I pressured you with demonic energy and you had no choice, didn’t you?”

Gong Chan understood. Geom Mugeuk had never intended to spare him from the beginning. The moment Gong Chan had tried to hurl insults and curses at him, his speech-sealing acupoint had already been subdued. It was a level of skill that could make even ghosts weep—or rather, even a ghost would be rendered helpless if its speech-sealing and demonic meridian acupoints were subdued.

There was no parting advice to be a good person in his next life. Geom Mugeuk utterly rejected Gong Chan’s entire life.

Hwoooooong!

Geom Mugeuk’s merciless fist flew toward him.

Kkwa-reu-reu-reung!

At the same time, thunder rang out like heaven’s punishment descending.

Ppeo-eok.

Gong Chan’s ribs shattered in a single blow, and he was sent flying into the wall of a distant building. He was already dead before his body struck the wall.

“You tried to cut off an innocent man’s hands, and went so far as to try to cut out my friend’s tongue. You were already a dead man.”

He said this so the villagers and escorts could hear. They needed to understand exactly what these deaths meant. Only then could Jonghak’s family continue living here.

The watching escorts and villagers thought it a fitting result.

Jonghak let out a relieved sigh.

Knowing what he must be feeling, Yang Seon embraced her husband tightly.

Yang In felt it. The moment Geom Mugeuk called Jonghak his friend, the hand she was holding tightened in her husband’s.

Jo Chunbae spoke in a trembling voice.

“You are far too good a friend for me.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk turned to him and smiled.

“Of course I am. I’m going to become the Heavenly Demon, after all. I’m a friend who’s too good for anyone.”

Jo Chunbae felt better at Geom Mugeuk’s lighthearted banter, which put his mind at ease.

“You have no idea how grateful I am.”

Jo Chunbae would live the rest of his life with gratitude in his heart.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“I’m the one who should be grateful.”

“What?”

“If anything, innkeeper, you were the one who saved me.”

Geom Mugeuk gave the wide-eyed Jo Chunbae, who clearly had no idea what he meant, a faint smile before turning away.

Thank you for allowing me to protect my demonic path, innkeeper.

Geom Mugeuk walked toward U Sochu.

“Fortunately, it seems we’ve managed to distinguish right from wrong, as we intended.”

That was not the only thing that had been released. U Sochu’s sealed acupoint had also been freed without him realizing it.

“You! Do you know what you’ve done? You just killed the chief escort of the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency!”

He shouted as he looked at the escorts.

“This is a separate matter from Gongchan’s crimes! A demonic cultivator has killed an orthodox martial artist! This is an attack by the Demonic Cult against the orthodox sects!”

His cries rang hollowly through the air. Not a single person agreed with him. The moment it was revealed that he had ordered Jonghak’s wrist to be cut off, the authority of the Yellow Dragon Escort Agency’s chief had already vanished. He was nothing more than a hypocrite who had tried to kill an innocent man for his own benefit.

“If you lay a hand on me, the War Between Good and Evil will break out! Countless people will die because of you!”

While U Sochu was worked into a frenzy, Geom Mugeuk remained calm.

“It’s already been established that you can’t understand the situation and that there’s no point speaking to you nicely, so I’ll do things my way. No—in your way, I suppose.”

U Sochu was wondering what he meant when—

Swoosh.

His left hand stretched forward of its own accord. His demonic acupoint had already been subdued, leaving him unable to resist.

“What are you trying to do?”

“What you were trying to do. You gathered all these people here to show them that those who commit crimes are punished, didn’t you?”

When Geom Mugeuk held out his hand, the sword at the waist of Gongchan’s corpse in the distance was drawn. It flew across the air and landed in Geom Mugeuk’s hand.

“My sword is too sharp. It might hurt less.”

Realizing Geom Mugeuk’s intention, U Sochu was seized by terror.

“Stop! Don’t do it! Stop!”

“It’s all right. You said it yourself, didn’t you? Losing a wrist won’t kill you.”

“Why are you doing this, Young Cult Master? Young Cult Master! Please!”

“Fine, then. I’ll give you a chance.”

A frightening statement, as cold as the look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes, spilled from his lips.

“If you don’t answer my question by the time I count to three, I’ll cut off your left hand first and ask again.”

Holding the sword high, as though he might bring it down at any moment, Geom Mugeuk asked,

“Who is the man with the mole on his forehead? One, two…”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 338

Chapter 338: In a Place Where You Cannot Die Even If You Want To.
-----------------------------------------------------------------
“Three!”

After counting to three, Geom Mugeuk did not hesitate and brought his sword down.

Usochu shouted urgently.

“It’s not a mole!”

Swish.

The sword that had been descending stopped in mid-air.

Swish.

His wrist was cut, and blood trickled down. Fortunately, it had only sliced through the skin, not severing the bone.

‘This crazy bastard! He really was going to cut it off!’

A chill ran down Usochu’s spine. It was the first time in his life he had ever been so frightened; one could say he had lived a truly easy life.

“It’s not a mole?”

“That thing on the forehead, it is not a mole but a tattoo.”

“A tattoo? What kind of tattoo?”

Usochu hesitated.

“One, two.”

This time, the answer came as he counted to two.

“A snake’s head.”

“A snake?”

“A snake’s head is engraved on the forehead. It seems you mistook it for a mole because it is black.”

There was only one place in the martial world where lunatics who carved snake heads into their foreheads gathered.

“Don’t tell me it’s the Black Snake Society?”

At the mention of the Black Snake Society, not only the escorts but also the villagers turned pale. In particular, Jonghak broke out in goosebumps all over his body at the realization that what he had seen was the symbol of the Black Snake Society.

The Black Snake Society was a group belonging to the demonic path, people who committed all manner of evil deeds. Even among the villains of the demonic path, they were truly infamous.

“Did you take the Black Snake Society’s escort goods?!”

Usochu lowered his head deeply, unable to answer.

The escorts’ expressions completely stiffened. One middle-aged man among them could not contain himself and shouted.

“Chief, is what he said true?”

Usochu raised his head and glared at him. His true feelings could be read in that gaze.

‘No matter what wrong I have committed, how dare a mere wretch like you question the Chief?’

He was truly a hopeless man.

Besides the one who had asked earlier, the other escorts also glared at Usochu with cold eyes. That he had accepted escort goods from none other than the Black Snake Society?

In the end, unable to contain himself, Usochu burst out shouting.

“It was all for the sake of the escort bureau! What do you bastards know to be shooting your mouths off?”

Geom Mugeuk tapped his wrist with the tip of his sword.

Usochu, who had been shouting loudly at the escorts, jumped in fright.

“Don’t cut it! Please!”

At his frightened appearance, the escorts let out sighs. It was shameful that they had served such a man as their chief.

“What were the goods?”

“I do not know.”

“How could that be? No matter how blinded by money you were, there is no way someone like you would join hands with the Black Snake Society without knowing what the goods were.”

Geom Mugeuk raised his hand high.

“One, two……”

“I do not know, I truly do not know.”

“Three.”

Swish.

Shhk!

The sword passed through his wrist.

Thud.

The moment he saw his hand falling to the ground, Usochu’s eyes went wide. He had thought surely not.

Spurt!

As blood spurted from his wrist, Usochu let out a scream.

“Uwaaaaaa!”

The onlookers turned their heads and closed their eyes.

Jo Chunbae and Jonghak watched the scene with eyes wide open. If Geom Mugeuk had not come, that hand lying on the ground would have been Jonghak’s.

The spurting blood stopped. Geom Mugeuk had sent a gust of wind to press his acupoint.

Just as Usochu was about to spew curses of resentment at Geom Mugeuk.

Slide.

His right hand rose into the air on its own.

Usochu panicked.

“No! Stop! You crazy bastard, stop!”

Geom Mugeuk counted again.

“One, two!”

Usochu shouted.

“It is children!”

Silence fell in an instant. It was an answer no one had expected.

“The children are the goods?”

“That is correct. I put the children into a deep sleep using the Turtle Breath Great Technique and packed them in boxes, then delivered them to wherever they wanted.”

Because the Turtle Breath Great Technique made them fall into a sleep like that of the dead, they could be moved like cargo for several days. They likely filled the boxes containing the children with other soft items as camouflage. This way, they could move many children quickly without drawing attention.

“Please, take me to a physician.”

He picked up his fallen hand and spoke earnestly. Only a master physician would be able to reattach it.

Geom Mugeuk’s reaction was cold.

“Why would they entrust that task to you? They could have used an escort bureau on the demonic path. Or the Black Snake Society could have moved them themselves. You need to answer quickly if you want to go quickly.”

“I asked the same thing. They said the Martial Alliance had caught wind of this matter and was watching them.”

Geom Mugeuk remembered. He had heard a story about the Black Snake Society kidnapping children with excellent bone frames, selling them for enormous sums, and amassing wealth. He remembered it as an incident that had occurred much later than the present.

“They said the Demon Extermination Commander of the Martial Alliance had personally launched an investigation. So they said they were lying low.”

The Demon Extermination Commander was Jinhagun, the grandson of the Martial Alliance Lord. His encounter with him had changed Jinhagun’s course of action. As his fate changed, the fate of the martial world would change as a result.

“Please! Please take me!”

“I still have questions.”

“I will answer them at the physician’s.”

“No! Answer here.”

Usochu’s eyes were filled with uncontainable hatred and resentment.

“How much extra money did you receive?”

“I received ten times the usual amount.”

After answering, Usochu felt the gazes directed at him.

He turned his head, and everyone was looking at him. Both the villagers and the escorts were people with families.

They were fathers raising children, and they were someone’s children. Thus, their gazes could not be gentle.

“You think you’re any diff—”

Before Usochu could even finish his words.

Swish.

Shhk!

This time, without even counting to three, Geom Mugeuk cut off his right hand as well.

Watching the blood spurt, he screamed. Having lost both hands, he was in a state of madness.

“This is what you tried to do to that man. You have committed a sin, at the very least.”

As he screamed and tried to stop the bleeding by somehow pressing his arm’s acupoint with his chin.

Just as he was thrashing about like a madman!

Rumble! Boom!

The sound of thunder heralding heavenly punishment rang out.

Crack!

Geom Mugeuk’s fist embedded itself in his face. Usochu’s face caved in, and he fell backward, his breath severed on the spot. Geom Mugeuk had not permitted him so much as a final word.

Jo Chunbae and his family were startled and bewildered, yet also relieved.

Geom Mugeuk sent the villagers away first.

“I believe it is time for you to return home. Moreover, since this matter is related to the Black Snake Society, it would be best to keep silent about today’s events for the time being.”

Since it was related to the Black Snake Society, even if they were encouraged to gossip, there would be no one who would spread word of this.

Once they had all left, Geom Mugeuk asked the escorts.

“If I am to discuss matters regarding the escort bureau, with whom should I speak now?”

At that, the escorts’ gazes turned to one man. The one they all acknowledged was the very person who had earlier asked Usochu if it was true.

“I am Chief Escort Huang Yuan.”

“For the time being, you will serve as the temporary Bureau Chief.”

“I shall.”

Originally, he would not have intervened at all in the affairs of the Yellow Dragon Escort Bureau from now on, but since Jonghak was there as a porter, he decided to take an interest in their future course as well.

“Lend me about ten sets of Yellow Dragon Escort Bureau escort uniforms.”

“May I ask the reason?”

“You all heard as well that they arranged to receive the escort goods at Green Water Forest in three days. I intend to take martial artists from our cult’s branch and rescue those children first, at the very least.”

“Will word of what happened here not reach their ears?”

“If so, and they do not show up, then it cannot be helped. However, if we are fortunate enough to reach Green Water Forest before word of this does, we should at least be able to rescue the children loaded in this shipment.”

Huang Yuan stared intently at Geom Mugeuk and asked.

“Why do you intend to rescue them? You are……”

The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. Weren’t his people even more cruel than those demonic path bastards?

He had asked with such thoughts in mind, but an astonishing answer came.

“Because they are children. I may not know about others, but surely children must be rescued?”

If they had not seen the spectacle Geom Mugeuk had shown today, they would never have accepted these words at face value.

But the escorts had seen everything from beginning to end.

“May we discuss amongst ourselves for a moment?”

“Do so.”

Huang Yuan spoke with the escorts and reached an unexpected conclusion.

“We will go to Green Water Forest together. If people with unfamiliar faces go, the enemy will be suspicious. Though they may not show themselves in the first place.”

Several escorts stepped forward. It seemed only a few had volunteered, not all.

“It may be dangerous.”

“We are aware.”

“Then why do you wish to go?”

“We are orthodox martial artists. Though we may be people who merely carry others’ goods, we are still of the orthodox path. This is a golden opportunity to save kidnapped children; we cannot pretend not to know.”

And though they did not say it, they felt a sense of belief that if this Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult went with them, this matter could be handled well.

“Please restore the honor of our bureau that the Bureau Chief has dragged through the mud.”

“The Black Snake Society may seek revenge later.”

It seemed they had thought about that as well.

“The moment we rescue the children at Green Water Forest, we will inform the Martial Alliance and request their aid.”

“Very well. Let us do so.”

Geom Mugeuk accepted their request. With them along, handling matters would certainly be advantageous in many ways.

He obtained a set of escort clothes from Huang Yuan, changed into them, and promised to meet at Green Water Forest in three days.

Geom Mugeuk rented a carriage from the escort bureau.

“Now, please get in. We have somewhere to go.”

He had Mrs. Yun, Yangin, Yangseon, Jo Chunbae, and Jonghak all board the carriage.

“I will drive the carriage.”

Jonghak, the porter, stepped forward, but Geom Mugeuk declined.

“Take care of your wounds. If you do not tend to them properly now, you will suffer for life.”

He forcefully seated him in the carriage.

Jo Chunbae and his family sat facing one another in the carriage. Sitting together in one space like this, they finally felt the relief of having safely escaped the crisis.

The voice of the person who had made this miraculous event possible came from the coachman’s seat.

“Then we shall depart.”

* * *

The carriage ran without rest for half a day.

Entering the forest path, the carriage forged through places where there were no roads and then stopped somewhere.

“You must get down and walk for a while.”

Geom Mugeuk led them into the forest. After walking for some time, they reached a place thick with fog.

“From now on, will you step exactly where I step and follow me?”

The women did not know the reason, but Jo Chunbae and Jonghak, who had seen and heard much, understood. That they were trying to pass through a formation.

Geom Mugeuk moved extremely slowly, considerate of the elderly Mrs. Yun. Passing through the formation under his guidance, they arrived at an estate where the spectacular sight of a winter waterfall unfolded.

A middle-aged man greeted them with respectful manners.

“Welcome.”

Jo Chunbae could sense that he was an expert of the Demonic Cult.

The scenery of the snow-covered estate was breathtakingly beautiful.

“I have never seen such a wonderful estate.”

“Me neither, unni.”

Yangin and Yangseon exclaimed in admiration.

Jo Chunbae asked Geom Mugeuk.

“Where is this?”

“This is our cult’s safe haven. Not an ordinary safe haven, but a special one that only those of Demon Lord rank or higher may use. It is a place where you cannot die even if you wish to.”

Everyone was startled at the words “safe haven of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.” They had never imagined Geom Mugeuk would bring them to a safe haven. Moreover, a special safe haven?

“Since the Black Snake Society is involved, I thought it best to be cautious. Please rest comfortably here until this matter is concluded.”

Geom Mugeuk absolutely ensured the safety of Jo Chunbae’s family. He eliminated every possible risk, down to the very last one.

“How could we possibly impose by staying in such a precious place?”

“Because you are a friend of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Young Cult Leader.”

“Just how greatly are you trying to move me?”

“I shall look forward to free side dishes at the Winds and Streams Tavern.”

Jo Chunbae was so overwhelmed with gratitude that he was at a complete loss. Even if he made free side dishes for a lifetime, he could not repay this grace.

The reason Geom Mugeuk had brought them here was not solely for their safety.

“When was the last time you traveled?”

Jo Chunbae was bewildered by the unexpected question. When was it? There was no need to even think about it.

Jo Chunbae cast a shameful expression toward his wife.

“Then, as the proprietor, have you ever closed the tavern to rest?”

“Never.”

There had been times he rested because of matters at home, but never once had he rested for his own sake. Not a day went by that he did not open the doors. He opened them when he was sick, and opened them when things were hard. There were days when he truly wished to rest. Even on those days, he opened them.

“The Young Leader? Surely not?”

“Yes. Please think of this as having come here for a journey, and as a long-overdue vacation—rest well.”

Jo Chunbae did not know what to say. A person could become dazed when overwhelmed by emotion.

“For me, it is easy work requiring only that I move my lips. It is no great matter, so please do not feel burdened. I shall come to escort you as soon as my work is finished.”

Before departing, Geommugeuk transmitted several matters to the middle-aged steward through voice transmission. And finally, he commanded:

“Serve these people as you would serve me.”

The middle-aged steward bowed his head respectfully.

In a flash, Geommugeuk disappeared into the distance, becoming a mere dot.

Jo Chunbae and his family stood still, watching that sight.

The middle-aged steward who oversaw the estate guided them with a kind smile.

“Now, please come inside.”

“Oh my, yes.”

Jo Chunbae and his family entered the manor. The carpets covering the floors, the furniture set in the corridors, each painting hanging on the walls—all were uniformly precious and luxurious.

“I have never been to a place like this in my entire life.”

As if it were only Yang In? It was the first time in such a place for them all.

As they passed by, they saw a chef preparing food in the open kitchen—dishes one could only eat at high-end restaurants.

The middle-aged steward prepared three separate rooms: one for Lady Yun, one for Jo Chunbae and his wife, and one for Jonghak and his wife. The rooms were decorated even more luxuriously.

“Is it alright for us to be treated like this?”

At Yang In's question, Jo Chunbae shook his head.

“I do not know either.”

Yang Seon laid Jonghak on a bed covered with a white, fluffy quilt.

“Now don't worry about anything and just rest.”

Perhaps because Geommugeuk had asked before he left, a physician from the An family came, took Jonghak's pulse, gave him medicine, and left.
